package org.example.sto.repository;

import org.example.sto.db.ConnectionManager;
import org.example.sto.model.Service;

import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.sql.Types;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

public final class ServiceRepository {

    private final ConnectionManager connectionManager;

    public ServiceRepository(ConnectionManager connectionManager) {
        this.connectionManager = connectionManager;
    }

    public List<Service> findAll() {
        return connectionManager.withConnection("services.findAll", connection -> {
            String sql = "SELECT id, name, price, duration_min FROM services ORDER BY id";
            try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql);
                 ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {
                List<Service> services = new ArrayList<>();
                while (resultSet.next()) {
                    services.add(mapService(resultSet));
                }
                return services;
            }
        });
    }

    public Optional<Service> findById(int id) {
        return connectionManager.withConnection("services.findById", connection -> {
            String sql = "SELECT id, name, price, duration_min FROM services WHERE id = ?";
            try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {
                statement.setInt(1, id);
                try (ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {
                    return resultSet.next() ? Optional.of(mapService(resultSet)) : Optional.empty();
                }
            }
        });
    }

    public Service create(String name, java.math.BigDecimal price, Integer durationMin) {
        return connectionManager.withConnection("services.create", connection -> {
            String sql = """
                    INSERT INTO services (name, price, duration_min)
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?)
                    RETURNING id, name, price, duration_min
                    """;
            try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {
                statement.setString(1, name);
                statement.setBigDecimal(2, price);
                if (durationMin == null) {
                    statement.setNull(3, Types.INTEGER);
                } else {
                    statement.setInt(3, durationMin);
                }
                try (ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {
                    resultSet.next();
                    return mapService(resultSet);
                }
            }
        });
    }

    private Service mapService(ResultSet resultSet) throws SQLException {
        int duration = resultSet.getInt("duration_min");
        Integer nullableDuration = resultSet.wasNull() ? null : duration;
        return new Service(
                resultSet.getInt("id"),
                resultSet.getString("name"),
                resultSet.getBigDecimal("price"),
                nullableDuration
        );
    }
}
````

package org.example.sto.repository;

import org.example.sto.db.ConnectionManager;
import org.example.sto.model.User;

import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

/**
 * Доступ к таблицам roles/users. Проверка пароля выполняется в PostgreSQL
 * функцией crypt() из расширения pgcrypto, поэтому хеш не загружается в Java.
 */
public final class UserRepository {

    private final ConnectionManager connectionManager;

    public UserRepository(ConnectionManager connectionManager) {
        this.connectionManager = connectionManager;
    }

    public Optional<User> authenticate(String username, char[] password) {
        String passwordText = new String(password);
        try {
            String finalPassword = passwordText;
            return connectionManager.withConnection("users.authenticate", connection -> {
                String sql = """
                        SELECT u.id, u.username, u.full_name, r.name AS role
                        FROM users u
                        JOIN roles r ON r.id = u.role_id
                        WHERE u.username = ?
                          AND u.password_hash = crypt(?, u.password_hash)
                        """;
                try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql)) {
                    statement.setString(1, username);
                    statement.setString(2, finalPassword);
                    try (ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {
                        return resultSet.next() ? Optional.of(mapUser(resultSet)) : Optional.empty();
                    }
                }
            });
        } finally {
            java.util.Arrays.fill(password, '\0');
        }
    }

    public List<User> findAll() {
        return connectionManager.withConnection("users.findAll", connection -> {
            String sql = """
                    SELECT u.id, u.username, u.full_name, r.name AS role
                    FROM users u
                    JOIN roles r ON r.id = u.role_id
                    ORDER BY u.id
                    """;
            try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(sql);
                 ResultSet resultSet = statement.executeQuery()) {
                List<User> users = new ArrayList<>();
                while (resultSet.next()) {
                    users.add(mapUser(resultSet));
                }
                return users;
            }
        });
    }

    private User mapUser(ResultSet resultSet) throws java.sql.SQLException {
        return new User(
                resultSet.getInt("id"),
                resultSet.getString("username"),
                resultSet.getString("full_name"),
                resultSet.getString("role")
        );
    }
}
````

``java
package org.example.sto.service;

import org.example.sto.exception.AuthenticationException;
import org.example.sto.exception.ValidationException;
import org.example.sto.model.User;
import org.example.sto.repository.UserRepository;

import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Optional;

/**
 * Аутентификация пользователей. Пароль проверяется в БД через pgcrypto/bcrypt.
 */
public final class AuthService {

    private final UserRepository userRepository;

    public AuthService(UserRepository userRepository) {
        this.userRepository = userRepository;
    }

    public User login(String username, char[] password) {
        if (username == null || username.isBlank()) {
            throw new ValidationException("Логин не может быть пустым");
        }
        if (password == null || password.length == 0) {
            throw new ValidationException("Пароль не может быть пустым");
        }

        Optional<User> user = userRepository.authenticate(
                username.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT), password);
        return user.orElseThrow(() -> new AuthenticationException("Неверный логин или пароль"));
    }
}
````

## 44. `src/main/java/org/example/sto/service/CatalogService.java`

Строк: 153

``java
package org.example.sto.service;

import org.example.sto.exception.DuplicateKeyException;
import org.example.sto.exception.EntityNotFoundException;
import org.example.sto.exception.ValidationException;
import org.example.sto.model.Car;
import org.example.sto.model.Client;
import org.example.sto.model.Master;
import org.example.sto.model.OrderStatus;
import org.example.sto.model.Service;
import org.example.sto.repository.CarRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.ClientRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.MasterRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.OrderRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.ServiceRepository;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Бизнес-операции со справочниками: клиенты, автомобили, услуги, мастера.
 * Здесь выполняется валидация входных данных до обращения к БД.
 */
public final class CatalogService {

    private final ClientRepository clientRepository;
    private final CarRepository carRepository;
    private final ServiceRepository serviceRepository;
    private final MasterRepository masterRepository;
    private final OrderRepository orderRepository;

    public CatalogService(ClientRepository clientRepository,
                          CarRepository carRepository,
                          ServiceRepository serviceRepository,
                          MasterRepository masterRepository,
                          OrderRepository orderRepository) {
        this.clientRepository = clientRepository;
        this.carRepository = carRepository;
        this.serviceRepository = serviceRepository;
        this.masterRepository = masterRepository;
        this.orderRepository = orderRepository;
    }

    public List<Client> clients() {
        return clientRepository.findAll();
    }

    public List<Car> cars() {
        return carRepository.findAll();
    }

    public List<Car> clientCars(int clientId) {
        return carRepository.findByClientId(clientId);
    }

    public List<Service> services() {
        return serviceRepository.findAll();
    }

    public List<Master> masters() {
        return masterRepository.findAll();
    }

    public List<OrderStatus> orderStatuses() {
        return orderRepository.findStatuses();
    }

    public Client clientById(int id) {
        return clientRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new EntityNotFoundException("Клиент с id=" + id + " не найден"));
    }

    public Car carById(int id) {
        return carRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new EntityNotFoundException("Автомобиль с id=" + id + " не найден"));
    }

    public Service serviceById(int id) {
        return serviceRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new EntityNotFoundException("Услуга с id=" + id + " не найдена"));
    }

    public Client createClient(String fullName, String phone, String email) {
        requireText(fullName, "ФИО клиента", 150);
        requireText(phone, "Телефон", 20);
        if (!phone.matches("\\+?[0-9()\\-\\s]{5,20}")) {
            throw new ValidationException("Телефон содержит недопустимые символы");
        }
        if (email != null && !email.isBlank()) {
            requireText(email, "E-mail", 100);
            if (!email.contains("@")) {
                throw new ValidationException("E-mail должен содержать символ @");
            }
        }
        try {
            return clientRepository.create(fullName.trim(), phone.trim(), normalize(email));
        } catch (DuplicateKeyException e) {
            throw new ValidationException("Клиент с телефоном " + phone + " уже существует", e);
        }
    }

    public Car createCar(int clientId, String brand, String model, Integer year, String plateNum, String vin) {
        clientById(clientId);
        requireText(brand, "Марка", 50);
        requireText(model, "Модель", 50);
        requireText(plateNum, "Гос. номер", 15);
        if (year != null && (year < 1950 || year > 2100)) {
            throw new ValidationException("Год выпуска должен быть в диапазоне 1950..2100");
        }
        if (vin != null && !vin.isBlank() && vin.trim().length() > 17) {
            throw new ValidationException("VIN не может быть длиннее 17 символов");
        }
        try {
            return carRepository.create(clientId, brand.trim(), model.trim(), year, plateNum.trim(), normalize(vin));
        } catch (DuplicateKeyException e) {
            throw new ValidationException("Автомобиль с номером " + plateNum + " уже существует", e);
        }
    }

    public Service createService(String name, BigDecimal price, Integer durationMin) {
        requireText(name, "Наименование услуги", 150);
        if (price == null || price.signum() < 0) {
            throw new ValidationException("Стоимость услуги не может быть отрицательной");
        }
        if (durationMin != null && durationMin <= 0) {
            throw new ValidationException("Длительность услуги должна быть больше нуля");
        }
        return serviceRepository.create(name.trim(), price, durationMin);
    }

    public Client findClientByPhone(String phone) {
        return clientRepository.findByPhone(phone)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new EntityNotFoundException("Клиент с телефоном " + phone + " не найден"));
    }

    public Car findCarByPlate(String plate) {
        return carRepository.findByPlate(plate)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new EntityNotFoundException("Автомобиль с номером " + plate + " не найден"));
    }

    private static void requireText(String value, String field, int maxLength) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new ValidationException("Поле «" + field + "» обязательно");
        }
        if (value.trim().length() > maxLength) {
            throw new ValidationException("Поле «" + field + "» длиннее " + maxLength + " символов");
        }
    }

    private static String normalize(String value) {
        return value == null || value.isBlank() ? null : value.trim();
    }
}

``java
package org.example.sto.service;

import org.example.sto.exception.EntityNotFoundException;
import org.example.sto.exception.ValidationException;
import org.example.sto.model.ClientOrder;
import org.example.sto.model.Master;
import org.example.sto.model.Order;
import org.example.sto.model.OrderServiceItem;
import org.example.sto.model.OrderStatus;
import org.example.sto.model.OrderStatusLogEntry;
import org.example.sto.model.OrderSummary;
import org.example.sto.model.Service;
import org.example.sto.repository.CarRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.ClientRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.MasterRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.OrderRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.ServiceRepository;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;

/**
 * Бизнес-логика работы с заявками.
 *
 * <p>Часть правил продублирована в БД (триггеры и хранимые функции), чтобы
 * их нельзя было обойти в обход приложения. Приложение проверяет данные
 * заранее, чтобы выдавать понятные сообщения пользователю.
 */
public final class OrderService {

    private static final Set<String> CLOSED_STATUSES = Set.of("завершена", "отменена");

    private final OrderRepository orderRepository;
    private final ClientRepository clientRepository;
    private final CarRepository carRepository;
    private final MasterRepository masterRepository;
    private final ServiceRepository serviceRepository;

    public OrderService(OrderRepository orderRepository,
                        ClientRepository clientRepository,
                        CarRepository carRepository,
                        MasterRepository masterRepository,
                        ServiceRepository serviceRepository) {
        this.orderRepository = orderRepository;
        this.clientRepository = clientRepository;
        this.carRepository = carRepository;
        this.masterRepository = masterRepository;
        this.serviceRepository = serviceRepository;
    }

    public List<OrderSummary> listAll() {
        return orderRepository.findAllSummaries();
    }

    public Order getOrder(int orderId) {
        return orderRepository.findById(orderId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new EntityNotFoundException("Заявка с id=" + orderId + " не найдена"));
    }

    public int createOrder(int clientId, int carId, Integer masterId, String description) {
        if (clientRepository.findById(clientId).isEmpty()) {
            throw new EntityNotFoundException("Клиент с id=" + clientId + " не найден");
        }
        var car = carRepository.findById(carId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new EntityNotFoundException("Автомобиль с id=" + carId + " не найден"));
        if (car.clientId() != clientId) {
            throw new ValidationException(
                    "Автомобиль " + car.plateNum() + " принадлежит другому клиенту (id=" + car.clientId() + ")");
        }
        if (description == null || description.isBlank()) {
            throw new ValidationException("Описание неисправности обязательно");
        }
        if (description.trim().length() > 2000) {
            throw new ValidationException("Описание не должно превышать 2000 символов");
        }
        if (masterId != null) {
            Master master = masterRepository.findById(masterId)
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new EntityNotFoundException("Мастер с id=" + masterId + " не найден"));
            if (!master.active()) {
                throw new ValidationException("Мастер " + master.fullName() + " неактивен");
            }
        }
        return orderRepository.createOrder(clientId, carId, masterId, description.trim());
    }

    public void addService(int orderId, int serviceId, int quantity) {
        if (quantity <= 0 || quantity > 100) {
            throw new ValidationException("Количество должно быть от 1 до 100");
        }
        Order order = getOrder(orderId);
        if (CLOSED_STATUSES.contains(order.statusName())) {
            throw new ValidationException(
                    "Нельзя добавить услугу в заявку со статусом «" + order.statusName() + "»");
        }
        Service service = serviceRepository.findById(serviceId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new EntityNotFoundException("Услуга с id=" + serviceId + " не найдена"));
        orderRepository.addService(orderId, service.id(), quantity);
    }

    public void closeOrder(int orderId) {
        Order order = getOrder(orderId);
        if ("завершена".equals(order.statusName())) {
            throw new ValidationException("Заявка уже завершена");
        }
        if ("отменена".equals(order.statusName())) {
            throw new ValidationException("Нельзя завершить отменённую заявку");
        }
        orderRepository.closeOrder(orderId);
    }

    public BigDecimal calculateTotal(int orderId) {
        getOrder(orderId);
        return orderRepository.calculateTotal(orderId);
    }

    public List<OrderServiceItem> orderServices(int orderId) {
        getOrder(orderId);
        return orderRepository.findServices(orderId);
    }

    public List<ClientOrder> clientOrders(int clientId) {
        if (clientRepository.findById(clientId).isEmpty()) {
            throw new EntityNotFoundException("Клиент с id=" + clientId + " не найден");
        }
        return orderRepository.findClientOrders(clientId);
    }

    public List<OrderStatus> statuses() {
        return orderRepository.findStatuses();
    }

    public void changeStatus(int orderId, int statusId) {
        getOrder(orderId);
        boolean exists = statuses().stream().anyMatch(status -> status.id() == statusId);
        if (!exists) {
            throw new EntityNotFoundException("Статус с id=" + statusId + " не найден");
        }
        orderRepository.updateStatus(orderId, statusId);
    }

    public List<OrderStatusLogEntry> statusLog(Integer orderId) {
        return orderRepository.findStatusLog(orderId);
    }

    public Map<Integer, Integer> masterWorkload() {
        return orderRepository.activeOrdersByMaster();
    }
}
````

## 46. `src/main/java/org/example/sto/web/ApiServer.java`

Строк: 402

``java
package org.example.sto.web;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.SerializationFeature;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.datatype.jsr310.JavaTimeModule;
import com.sun.net.httpserver.HttpExchange;
import com.sun.net.httpserver.HttpServer;
import org.example.sto.db.ConnectionManager;
import org.example.sto.exception.AuthenticationException;
import org.example.sto.exception.ConnectionUnavailableException;
import org.example.sto.exception.DataIntegrityException;
import org.example.sto.exception.DatabaseException;
import org.example.sto.exception.EntityNotFoundException;
import org.example.sto.exception.ValidationException;
import org.example.sto.model.Car;
import org.example.sto.model.Client;
import org.example.sto.model.ClientOrder;
import org.example.sto.model.Master;
import org.example.sto.model.Order;
import org.example.sto.model.OrderServiceItem;
import org.example.sto.model.User;
import org.example.sto.service.AuthService;
import org.example.sto.service.CatalogService;
import org.example.sto.service.OrderService;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.OutputStream;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.net.InetSocketAddress;
import java.net.URI;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

/**
 * Небольшой REST API и статический сервер для Angular-интерфейса.
 *
 * <p>Запускается режимом {@code --web}. Использует те же сервисы и репозитории,
 * что и консольное приложение, поэтому бизнес-правила и права БД остаются
 * едиными для CLI и веб-интерфейса.
 */
public final class ApiServer {

    private static final Logger log = LoggerFactory.getLogger(ApiServer.class);
    private static final String FRONTEND_DIR = "frontend/dist/sto-frontend/browser";

    private final HttpServer server;
    private final ObjectMapper mapper;
    private final AuthService authService;
    private final CatalogService catalogService;
    private final OrderService orderService;
    private final ConnectionManager connectionManager;
    private final CountDownLatch stopped = new CountDownLatch(1);

    public ApiServer(int port,
                     AuthService authService,
                     CatalogService catalogService,
                     OrderService orderService,
                     ConnectionManager connectionManager) throws IOException {
        this.authService = authService;
        this.catalogService = catalogService;
        this.orderService = orderService;
        this.connectionManager = connectionManager;
        this.mapper = new ObjectMapper()
                .registerModule(new JavaTimeModule())
                .disable(SerializationFeature.WRITE_DATES_AS_TIMESTAMPS);

        this.server = HttpServer.create(new InetSocketAddress(port), 0);
        this.server.createContext("/api", this::handleApi);
        this.server.createContext("/", this::handleStatic);
        this.server.setExecutor(Executors.newFixedThreadPool(6));
    }

    public void start() {
        server.start();
        log.info("REST API доступен на http://localhost:{}", server.getAddress().getPort());
        log.info("Angular-интерфейс: http://localhost:{}/", server.getAddress().getPort());
    }

    public void await() throws InterruptedException {
        stopped.await();
    }

    public void stop() {
        server.stop(0);
        stopped.countDown();
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ API

    private void handleApi(HttpExchange exchange) throws IOException {
        addCors(exchange);
        if ("OPTIONS".equalsIgnoreCase(exchange.getRequestMethod())) {
            exchange.sendResponseHeaders(204, -1);
            exchange.close();
            return;
        }

        String path = exchange.getRequestURI().getPath();
        String method = exchange.getRequestMethod();
        try {
            if (path.equals("/api/health") && method.equals("GET")) {
                sendJson(exchange, 200, Map.of(
                        "status", "ok",
                        "database", connectionManager.ping()));
                return;
            }
            if (path.equals("/api/login") && method.equals("POST")) {
                LoginRequest request = readBody(exchange, LoginRequest.class);
                User user = authService.login(request.username(), request.password().toCharArray());
                sendJson(exchange, 200, user);
                return;
            }
            if (path.equals("/api/orders") && method.equals("GET")) {
                sendJson(exchange, 200, orderService.listAll());
                return;
            }
            if (path.equals("/api/orders") && method.equals("POST")) {
                CreateOrderRequest request = readBody(exchange, CreateOrderRequest.class);
                int id = orderService.createOrder(
                        request.clientId(), request.carId(), request.masterId(), request.description());
                sendJson(exchange, 201, Map.of("orderId", id));
                return;
            }
            if (path.equals("/api/clients") && method.equals("GET")) {
                sendJson(exchange, 200, catalogService.clients());
                return;
            }
            if (path.equals("/api/clients") && method.equals("POST")) {
                CreateClientRequest request = readBody(exchange, CreateClientRequest.class);
                Client client = catalogService.createClient(
                        request.fullName(), request.phone(), request.email());
                sendJson(exchange, 201, client);
                return;
            }
            Integer clientId = matchId(path, "/api/clients/");
            if (clientId != null && method.equals("GET") && path.equals("/api/clients/" + clientId)) {
                Client client = catalogService.clientById(clientId);
                sendJson(exchange, 200, new ClientDetail(
                        client,
                        catalogService.clientCars(clientId),
                        orderService.clientOrders(clientId)));
                return;
            }
            if (path.equals("/api/cars") && method.equals("GET")) {
                sendJson(exchange, 200, carViews());
                return;
            }
            if (path.equals("/api/cars") && method.equals("POST")) {
                CreateCarRequest request = readBody(exchange, CreateCarRequest.class);
                Car car = catalogService.createCar(
                        request.clientId(), request.brand(), request.model(),
                        request.year(), request.plateNum(), request.vin());
                sendJson(exchange, 201, new CarView(car, catalogService.clientById(car.clientId()).fullName()));
                return;
            }
            if (path.equals("/api/services") && method.equals("GET")) {
                sendJson(exchange, 200, catalogService.services());
                return;
            }
            if (path.equals("/api/services") && method.equals("POST")) {
                CreateServiceRequest request = readBody(exchange, CreateServiceRequest.class);
                org.example.sto.model.Service service = catalogService.createService(
                        request.name(), request.price(), request.durationMin());
                sendJson(exchange, 201, service);
                return;
            }
            if (path.equals("/api/masters/workload") && method.equals("GET")) {
                sendJson(exchange, 200, orderService.masterWorkload());
                return;
            }
            if (path.equals("/api/masters") && method.equals("GET")) {
                sendJson(exchange, 200, catalogService.masters());
                return;
            }
            if (path.equals("/api/statuses") && method.equals("GET")) {
                sendJson(exchange, 200, catalogService.orderStatuses());
                return;
            }
            if (path.equals("/api/log") && method.equals("GET")) {
                Integer orderId = intParam(exchange.getRequestURI(), "orderId");
                sendJson(exchange, 200, orderService.statusLog(orderId));
                return;
            }

            Integer orderId = matchId(path, "/api/orders/");
            if (orderId != null) {
                if (path.endsWith("/close") && method.equals("POST")) {
                    orderService.closeOrder(orderId);
                    sendJson(exchange, 200, Map.of("closed", true, "orderId", orderId));
                    return;
                }
                if (path.endsWith("/services") && method.equals("POST")) {
                    AddServiceRequest request = readBody(exchange, AddServiceRequest.class);
                    orderService.addService(orderId, request.serviceId(), request.quantity());
                    sendJson(exchange, 201, Map.of("added", true, "orderId", orderId));
                    return;
                }
                if (path.endsWith("/log") && method.equals("GET")) {
                    sendJson(exchange, 200, orderService.statusLog(orderId));
                    return;
                }
                if (path.equals("/api/orders/" + orderId) && method.equals("GET")) {
                    sendJson(exchange, 200, orderDetail(orderId));
                    return;
                }
            }

            sendJson(exchange, 404, new ApiError("Метод или ресурс не найден", "not_found"));
        } catch (AuthenticationException e) {
            sendJson(exchange, 401, new ApiError(e.getMessage(), "unauthorized"));
        } catch (EntityNotFoundException e) {
            sendJson(exchange, 404, new ApiError(e.getMessage(), "not_found"));
        } catch (ValidationException e) {
            sendJson(exchange, 400, new ApiError(e.getMessage(), "validation"));
        } catch (DataIntegrityException e) {
            sendJson(exchange, 409, new ApiError(e.getMessage(), sqlCode(e)));
        } catch (ConnectionUnavailableException e) {
            sendJson(exchange, 503, new ApiError(e.getMessage(), sqlCode(e)));
        } catch (DatabaseException e) {
            log.warn("Ошибка API {} {}: {}", method, path, e.getMessage());
            sendJson(exchange, 500, new ApiError(e.getMessage(), sqlCode(e)));
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            sendJson(exchange, 400, new ApiError(e.getMessage(), "bad_request"));
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("Непредвиденная ошибка API {} {}", method, path, e);
            sendJson(exchange, 500, new ApiError("Внутренняя ошибка сервера", "internal"));
        } finally {
            exchange.close();
        }
    }

    private List<CarView> carViews() {
        Map<Integer, String> owners = catalogService.clients().stream()
                .collect(Collectors.toMap(Client::id, Client::fullName));
        return catalogService.cars().stream()
                .map(car -> new CarView(car, owners.get(car.clientId())))
                .toList();
    }

    private OrderDetail orderDetail(int orderId) {
        Order order = orderService.getOrder(orderId);
        Client client = catalogService.clientById(order.clientId());
        Car car = catalogService.carById(order.carId());
        String masterName = order.masterId() == null ? null : catalogService.masters().stream()
                .filter(master -> master.id() == order.masterId())
                .map(Master::fullName)
                .findFirst()
                .orElse(null);
        List<OrderServiceItem> services = orderService.orderServices(orderId);
        BigDecimal total = orderService.calculateTotal(orderId);
        return new OrderDetail(order, client, car, masterName, total, services);
    }

    private static Integer matchId(String path, String prefix) {
        if (!path.startsWith(prefix)) {
            return null;
        }
        String tail = path.substring(prefix.length());
        String idPart = tail.contains("/") ? tail.substring(0, tail.indexOf('/')) : tail;
        try {
            return Integer.valueOf(idPart);
        } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    private static Integer intParam(URI uri, String name) {
        String query = uri.getQuery();
        if (query == null || query.isBlank()) {
            return null;
        }
        for (String pair : query.split("&")) {
            String[] parts = pair.split("=", 2);
            if (parts[0].equals(name) && parts.length == 2 && !parts[1].isBlank()) {
                return Integer.valueOf(parts[1]);
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    private <T> T readBody(HttpExchange exchange, Class<T> type) throws IOException {
        byte[] body = exchange.getRequestBody().readAllBytes();
        if (body.length == 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Тело запроса пустое");
        }
        return mapper.readValue(body, type);
    }

    private void sendJson(HttpExchange exchange, int status, Object value) throws IOException {
        byte[] body = mapper.writeValueAsBytes(value);
        exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("Content-Type", "application/json; charset=utf-8");
        exchange.sendResponseHeaders(status, body.length);
        try (OutputStream output = exchange.getResponseBody()) {
            output.write(body);
        }
    }

    private static String sqlCode(DatabaseException e) {
        return e.getSqlState() == null ? "database" : e.getSqlState();
    }

    private static void addCors(HttpExchange exchange) {
        exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "*");
        exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "GET, POST, OPTIONS");
        exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", "Content-Type, Authorization");
        exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("Access-Control-Max-Age", "86400");
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------- frontend

    private void handleStatic(HttpExchange exchange) throws IOException {
        String path = exchange.getRequestURI().getPath();
        if (path.equals("/")) {
            path = "/index.html";
        }
        Path base = Path.of(FRONTEND_DIR).toAbsolutePath().normalize();
        Path file = base.resolve(path.substring(1)).normalize();
        if (!file.startsWith(base) || !Files.isRegularFile(file)) {
            file = base.resolve("index.html");
        }
        if (!Files.isRegularFile(file)) {
            byte[] message = ("Angular-интерфейс не собран. Выполните: cd frontend && npm install && npm run build")
                    .getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
            exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("Content-Type", "text/plain; charset=utf-8");
            exchange.sendResponseHeaders(404, message.length);
            try (OutputStream output = exchange.getResponseBody()) {
                output.write(message);
            }
            exchange.close();
            return;
        }
        String contentType = switch (extension(file.getFileName().toString())) {
            case "html" -> "text/html; charset=utf-8";
            case "css" -> "text/css; charset=utf-8";
            case "js", "mjs" -> "text/javascript; charset=utf-8";
            case "json" -> "application/json; charset=utf-8";
            case "svg" -> "image/svg+xml";
            case "ico" -> "image/x-icon";
            case "png" -> "image/png";
            case "woff2" -> "font/woff2";
            default -> "application/octet-stream";
        };
        exchange.getResponseHeaders().set("Content-Type", contentType);
        exchange.sendResponseHeaders(200, Files.size(file));
        try (OutputStream output = exchange.getResponseBody()) {
            Files.copy(file, output);
        }
        exchange.close();
    }

    private static String extension(String name) {
        int dot = name.lastIndexOf('.');
        return dot < 0 ? "" : name.substring(dot + 1).toLowerCase();
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ DTO

    public record LoginRequest(String username, String password) {
    }

    public record CreateOrderRequest(Integer clientId, Integer carId, Integer masterId, String description) {
    }

    public record CreateClientRequest(String fullName, String phone, String email) {
    }

    public record CreateCarRequest(Integer clientId, String brand, String model, Integer year,
                                   String plateNum, String vin) {
    }

    public record CreateServiceRequest(String name, BigDecimal price, Integer durationMin) {
    }

    public record AddServiceRequest(Integer serviceId, Integer quantity) {
    }

    public record CarView(int id, int clientId, String brand, String model, Integer year,
                          String plateNum, String vin, String clientName) {
        CarView(Car car, String clientName) {
            this(car.id(), car.clientId(), car.brand(), car.model(), car.year(),
                    car.plateNum(), car.vin(), clientName);
        }
    }

    public record OrderDetail(Order order, Client client, Car car, String masterName,
                              BigDecimal total, List<OrderServiceItem> services) {
    }

    public record ClientDetail(Client client, List<Car> cars, List<ClientOrder> orders) {
    }

    public record ApiError(String message, String code) {
    }
}

## 47. `src/test/java/org/example/sto/config/DbSettingsTest.java`

``java
package org.example.sto.config;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

class DbSettingsTest {

    @Test
    void readsClasspathConfiguration() {
        DbSettings settings = AppConfig.load().dbSettings();

        assertTrue(settings.url().startsWith("jdbc:postgresql://"));
        assertEquals("sto_admin", settings.username());
        assertEquals(5, settings.maximumPoolSize());
        assertEquals(4, settings.retryMaxAttempts());
    }

    @Test
    void doesNotExposePasswordInSafeString() {
        DbSettings settings = new DbSettings(
                "jdbc:postgresql://localhost:5434/sto", "sto_admin", "secret",
                "org.postgresql.Driver", 5, 1, 3000, 1000, 60000, 600000,
                4, 250, 2.0, 4000);

        assertTrue(settings.toSafeString().contains("sto_admin"));
        assertTrue(!settings.toSafeString().contains("secret"));
    }

    @Test
    void validatesMandatoryParameters() {
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> new DbSettings(
                "", "user", "pass", "org.postgresql.Driver", 5, 1, 3000, 1000,
                60000, 600000, 4, 250, 2.0, 4000));

        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -> new DbSettings(
                "jdbc:postgresql://localhost/sto", "user", "pass", "org.postgresql.Driver",
                0, 0, 3000, 1000, 60000, 600000, 4, 250, 2.0, 4000));
    }
}
````

``java
package org.example.sto;

import org.example.sto.config.AppConfig;
import org.example.sto.config.DbSettings;
import org.example.sto.db.ConnectionManager;
import org.example.sto.db.RetryPolicy;
import org.example.sto.exception.AuthenticationException;
import org.example.sto.exception.DataIntegrityException;
import org.example.sto.exception.ForeignKeyViolationException;
import org.example.sto.exception.ValidationException;
import org.example.sto.model.Car;
import org.example.sto.model.Client;
import org.example.sto.model.ClientOrder;
import org.example.sto.model.Order;
import org.example.sto.model.OrderSummary;
import org.example.sto.model.Service;
import org.example.sto.model.User;
import org.example.sto.repository.CarRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.ClientRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.MasterRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.OrderRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.ServiceRepository;
import org.example.sto.repository.UserRepository;
import org.example.sto.service.AuthService;
import org.example.sto.service.CatalogService;
import org.example.sto.service.OrderService;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterAll;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Assumptions;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeAll;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.TestInstance;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.sql.PreparedStatement;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

/**
 * Интеграционные тесты связки «Java-приложение — PostgreSQL».
 *
 * <p>Перед запуском должен быть поднят стенд:
 * {@code docker compose up -d}. Если БД недоступна, тесты помечаются
 * как пропущенные (Assumption), а не падают.
 */
@TestInstance(TestInstance.Lifecycle.PER_CLASS)
class DatabaseIntegrationTest {

    private static ConnectionManager connectionManager;
    private static UserRepository userRepository;
    private static ClientRepository clientRepository;
    private static CarRepository carRepository;
    private static ServiceRepository serviceRepository;
    private static OrderRepository orderRepository;
    private static AuthService authService;
    private static CatalogService catalogService;
    private static OrderService orderService;

    @BeforeAll
    void connect() {
        DbSettings settings = AppConfig.load().dbSettings();
        ConnectionManager manager = new ConnectionManager(
                settings, new RetryPolicy(1, 0, 1.0, 0));
        try {
            manager.ping();
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            manager.close();
            Assumptions.assumeTrue(false,
                    "PostgreSQL недоступен (" + settings.url() + "). Запустите docker compose up -d. " + e.getMessage());
            return;
        }

        connectionManager = manager;
        userRepository = new UserRepository(manager);
        clientRepository = new ClientRepository(manager);
        carRepository = new CarRepository(manager);
        MasterRepository masterRepository = new MasterRepository(manager);
        serviceRepository = new ServiceRepository(manager);
        orderRepository = new OrderRepository(manager);

        authService = new AuthService(userRepository);
        catalogService = new CatalogService(
                clientRepository, carRepository, serviceRepository, masterRepository, orderRepository);
        orderService = new OrderService(
                orderRepository, clientRepository, carRepository, masterRepository, serviceRepository);
    }

    @AfterAll
    void disconnect() {
        if (connectionManager != null) {
            connectionManager.close();
        }
    }

    @Test
    void authenticatesSeededAdmin() {
        User user = authService.login("admin", "admin123".toCharArray());

        assertEquals("admin", user.username());
        assertEquals("admin", user.role());
        assertTrue(user.fullName().contains("Поляков"));

        // Логин не зависит от регистра: удобно при вводе с клавиатуры.
        User upperCaseLogin = authService.login("Admin", "admin123".toCharArray());
        assertEquals("admin", upperCaseLogin.username());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsWrongPassword() {
        assertThrows(AuthenticationException.class,
                () -> authService.login("admin", "wrong-password".toCharArray()));
    }

    @Test
    void sqlInjectionInLoginDoesNotBypassAuthentication() {
        assertThrows(AuthenticationException.class,
                () -> authService.login("' OR '1'='1", "anything".toCharArray()));
        assertThrows(AuthenticationException.class,
                () -> authService.login("admin' --", "anything".toCharArray()));
        assertThrows(AuthenticationException.class,
                () -> authService.login("admin", "' OR '1'='1".toCharArray()));
    }

    @Test
    void readsOrdersFromView() {
        List<OrderSummary> orders = orderService.listAll();

        assertFalse(orders.isEmpty());
        assertTrue(orders.stream().anyMatch(order -> order.status().equals("в работе")));
        assertTrue(orders.stream().allMatch(order -> order.totalCost() != null));
    }

    @Test
    void viewTotalEqualsStoredFunctionResult() {
        OrderSummary summary = orderService.listAll().stream()
                .filter(order -> order.totalCost().signum() > 0)
                .findFirst()
                .orElseThrow();

        assertEquals(0, summary.totalCost().compareTo(orderService.calculateTotal(summary.orderId())));
    }

    @Test
    void returnsClientOrdersThroughStoredFunction() {
        Client client = catalogService.findClientByPhone("+7-900-100-10-01");
        List<ClientOrder> orders = orderService.clientOrders(client.id());

        assertFalse(orders.isEmpty());
        assertTrue(orders.stream().allMatch(order -> order.carPlate() != null && !order.carPlate().isBlank()));
    }

    @Test
    void createsAddsServiceAndClosesOrder() throws SQLException {
        Client client = catalogService.findClientByPhone("+7-900-100-10-01");
        Car car = catalogService.clientCars(client.id()).get(0);
        Service service = catalogService.services().get(0);

        int orderId = orderService.createOrder(
                client.id(), car.id(), null, "Интеграционный тест: создание и закрытие заявки");
        try {
            orderService.addService(orderId, service.id(), 2);

            BigDecimal expected = service.price().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(2));
            assertEquals(0, expected.compareTo(orderService.calculateTotal(orderId)));

            orderService.closeOrder(orderId);
            Order closed = orderService.getOrder(orderId);
            assertEquals("завершена", closed.statusName());
            assertNotNull(closed.closedAt());

            assertTrue(orderService.statusLog(orderId).stream()
                    .anyMatch(entry -> entry.newStatusId() == 3));
        } finally {
            cleanupOrder(orderId);
        }
    }

    @Test
    void preventsAddingServiceToClosedOrder() {
        Order closedOrder = orderService.listAll().stream()
                .filter(order -> order.status().equals("завершена"))
                .findFirst()
                .map(summary -> orderService.getOrder(summary.orderId()))
                .orElseThrow();

        Service service = catalogService.services().get(0);
        assertThrows(ValidationException.class,
                () -> orderService.addService(closedOrder.id(), service.id(), 1));
    }

    @Test
    void createsClientAndCar() {
        String phone = "+7-999-000-00-88";
        deleteClientByPhone(phone);
        Client client = catalogService.createClient("Тестовый Владелец", phone, "owner@example.ru");
        try {
            Car car = catalogService.createCar(
                    client.id(), "Test", "Model", 2020, "Т888ТТ88", "TESTVIN1234567890");
            assertEquals(client.id(), car.clientId());
            assertEquals("Т888ТТ88", car.plateNum());
            assertFalse(catalogService.clientCars(client.id()).isEmpty());
        } finally {
            clientRepository.delete(client.id());
        }
    }

    @Test
    void createsOrderWithoutMaster() throws SQLException {
        Client client = catalogService.findClientByPhone("+7-900-100-10-01");
        Car car = catalogService.clientCars(client.id()).get(0);

        int orderId = orderService.createOrder(
                client.id(), car.id(), null, "Заявка без назначенного мастера");
        try {
            Order order = orderService.getOrder(orderId);
            assertEquals("новая", order.statusName());
            assertEquals(null, order.masterId());
        } finally {
            cleanupOrder(orderId);
        }
    }

    @Test
    void transactionRollsBackOnRuntimeException() {
        String phone = "+7-999-000-00-99";
        assertThrows(IllegalStateException.class, () ->
                connectionManager.inTransaction("test.rollback", connection -> {
                    try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(
                            "INSERT INTO clients (full_name, phone) VALUES (?, ?)")) {
                        statement.setString(1, "Откат");
                        statement.setString(2, phone);
                        statement.executeUpdate();
                    }
                    throw new IllegalStateException("Искусственная ошибка после INSERT");
                }));

        assertTrue(clientRepository.findByPhone(phone).isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    void databaseRejectsTooLongVarchar() {
        DataIntegrityException exception = assertThrows(DataIntegrityException.class, () ->
                connectionManager.withConnection("test.varchar", connection -> {
                    try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(
                            "INSERT INTO clients (full_name, phone) VALUES ('Длина', ?)")) {
                        statement.setString(1, "1".repeat(21));
                        return statement.executeUpdate();
                    }
                }));

        assertEquals("22001", exception.getSqlState());
    }

    @Test
    void databaseRejectsInvalidYearCheckConstraint() {
        DataIntegrityException exception = assertThrows(DataIntegrityException.class, () ->
                carRepository.create(1, "Test", "Year", 1800, "У777УУ77", null));

        assertEquals("23514", exception.getSqlState());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsDuplicateClientPhone() {
        String phone = "+7-999-000-00-77";
        deleteClientByPhone(phone);
        Client first = catalogService.createClient("Тестовый Клиент", phone, "test@example.ru");
        try {
            assertThrows(ValidationException.class,
                    () -> catalogService.createClient("Другой Клиент", phone, "other@example.ru"));
        } finally {
            clientRepository.delete(first.id());
        }
    }

    @Test
    void databaseRejectsForeignKeyViolation() {
        assertThrows(ForeignKeyViolationException.class,
                () -> carRepository.create(999_999, "Test", "FK", 2020, "Т999ТТ99", null));
    }

    @Test
    void databaseRejectsNullInNotNullColumn() {
        DataIntegrityException exception = assertThrows(DataIntegrityException.class,
                () -> connectionManager.withConnection("test.notNull", connection -> {
                    try (PreparedStatement statement = connection.prepareStatement(
                            "INSERT INTO clients (full_name, phone) VALUES ('Без телефона', NULL)")) {
                        return statement.executeUpdate();
                    }
                }));

        assertEquals("23502", exception.getSqlState());
    }

    private void cleanupOrder(int orderId) throws SQLException {
        connectionManager.inTransaction("test.cleanupOrder", connection -> {
            try (PreparedStatement services = connection.prepareStatement(
                    "DELETE FROM order_services WHERE order_id = ?");
                 PreparedStatement log = connection.prepareStatement(
                         "DELETE FROM order_status_log WHERE order_id = ?");
                 PreparedStatement order = connection.prepareStatement(
                         "DELETE FROM orders WHERE id = ?")) {
                services.setInt(1, orderId);
                services.executeUpdate();
                log.setInt(1, orderId);
                log.executeUpdate();
                order.setInt(1, orderId);
                order.executeUpdate();
            }
            return null;
        });
    }

    private void deleteClientByPhone(String phone) {
        connectionManager.withConnection("test.cleanupClient", connection -> {
            try (PreparedStatement services = connection.prepareStatement(
                    "DELETE FROM order_services WHERE order_id IN (SELECT id FROM orders WHERE client_id IN "
                            + "(SELECT id FROM clients WHERE phone = ?))");
                 PreparedStatement log = connection.prepareStatement(
                         "DELETE FROM order_status_log WHERE order_id IN (SELECT id FROM orders WHERE client_id IN "
                                 + "(SELECT id FROM clients WHERE phone = ?))");
                 PreparedStatement orders = connection.prepareStatement(
                         "DELETE FROM orders WHERE client_id IN (SELECT id FROM clients WHERE phone = ?)");
                 PreparedStatement cars = connection.prepareStatement(
                         "DELETE FROM cars WHERE client_id IN (SELECT id FROM clients WHERE phone = ?)");
                 PreparedStatement clients = connection.prepareStatement(
                         "DELETE FROM clients WHERE phone = ?")) {
                for (PreparedStatement statement : List.of(services, log, orders, cars, clients)) {
                    statement.setString(1, phone);
                    statement.executeUpdate();
                }
            }
            return null;
        });
    }
}
````

## 49. `src/test/java/org/example/sto/db/RetryPolicyTest.java`

Строк: 61

``java
package org.example.sto.db;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.net.ConnectException;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.sql.SQLTransientConnectionException;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

class RetryPolicyTest {

    @Test
    void delayGrowsExponentiallyAndIsCapped() {
        RetryPolicy policy = new RetryPolicy(6, 250, 2.0, 1_000);

        assertEquals(250, policy.delayMillis(1));
        assertEquals(500, policy.delayMillis(2));
        assertEquals(1_000, policy.delayMillis(3));
        assertEquals(1_000, policy.delayMillis(4));
    }

    @Test
    void sleeperReceivesCalculatedDelay() throws InterruptedException {
        List<Long> delays = new ArrayList<>();
        RetryPolicy policy = new RetryPolicy(4, 100, 2.0, 10_000, delays::add);

        policy.sleepBeforeRetry(1);
        policy.sleepBeforeRetry(2);

        assertEquals(List.of(100L, 200L), delays);
    }

    @Test
    void retriesConnectionLoss() {
        assertTrue(RetryPolicy.isRetryable(new SQLException("connection lost", "08006")));
        assertTrue(RetryPolicy.isRetryable(new SQLTransientConnectionException("pool exhausted")));
        assertTrue(RetryPolicy.isRetryable(new ConnectException("refused")));
        assertTrue(RetryPolicy.isRetryable(new SQLException("deadlock", "40P01")));
        assertTrue(RetryPolicy.isRetryable(new RuntimeException(new SQLException("serialization", "40001"))));
    }

    @Test
    void doesNotRetryPermanentErrors() {
        assertFalse(RetryPolicy.isRetryable(new SQLException("duplicate", "23505")));
        assertFalse(RetryPolicy.isRetryable(new SQLException("syntax", "42601")));
        assertFalse(RetryPolicy.isRetryable(new IllegalArgumentException("bad input")));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsInvalidConfiguration() {
        org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -> new RetryPolicy(0, 100, 2.0, 1_000));
        org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -> new RetryPolicy(3, 100, 0.5, 1_000));
    }
}
````

## 50. `src/test/java/org/example/sto/db/SqlExceptionTranslatorTest.java`

Строк: 84

``java
package org.example.sto.db;

import org.example.sto.exception.ConnectionUnavailableException;
import org.example.sto.exception.DataIntegrityException;
import org.example.sto.exception.DatabaseException;
import org.example.sto.exception.DuplicateKeyException;
import org.example.sto.exception.ForeignKeyViolationException;
import org.example.sto.exception.QueryException;
import org.example.sto.exception.TransientDatabaseException;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.sql.SQLException;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertInstanceOf;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

class SqlExceptionTranslatorTest {

    @Test
    void translatesUniqueViolation() {
        DatabaseException result = SqlExceptionTranslator.translate(
                new SQLException("duplicate key value violates unique constraint \"clients_phone_key\"", "23505"),
                "clients.create");

        assertInstanceOf(DuplicateKeyException.class, result);
        assertEquals("23505", result.getSqlState());
        assertEquals("clients.create", result.getOperation());
    }

    @Test
    void translatesForeignKeyViolation() {
        DatabaseException result = SqlExceptionTranslator.translate(
                new SQLException("insert or update violates foreign key", "23503"), "cars.create");
        assertInstanceOf(ForeignKeyViolationException.class, result);
    }

    @Test
    void translatesNotNullAndLengthViolationsToIntegrityError() {
        assertInstanceOf(DataIntegrityException.class,
                SqlExceptionTranslator.translate(new SQLException("null value", "23502"), "test"));
        assertInstanceOf(DataIntegrityException.class,
                SqlExceptionTranslator.translate(new SQLException("value too long", "22001"), "test"));
    }

    @Test
    void translatesConnectionProblems() {
        assertInstanceOf(ConnectionUnavailableException.class,
                SqlExceptionTranslator.translate(new SQLException("connection refused", "08001"), "connect"));
        assertInstanceOf(ConnectionUnavailableException.class,
                SqlExceptionTranslator.translate(new SQLException("starting up", "57P03"), "connect"));
    }

    @Test
    void translatesSyntaxErrors() {
        DatabaseException result = SqlExceptionTranslator.translate(
                new SQLException("syntax error at or near \"SELEC\"", "42601"), "query");
        assertInstanceOf(QueryException.class, result);
        assertTrue(result.getMessage().contains("SELEC"));
    }

    @Test
    void translatesSerializationFailure() {
        assertInstanceOf(TransientDatabaseException.class,
                SqlExceptionTranslator.translate(new SQLException("could not serialize", "40001"), "update"));
    }

    @Test
    void fallsBackToGenericDatabaseException() {
        DatabaseException result = SqlExceptionTranslator.translate(
                new SQLException("unknown", "XX000"), "unknown.operation");
        assertEquals(DatabaseException.class, result.getClass());
        assertEquals("XX000", result.getSqlState());
    }

    @Test
    void usesSqlStateFromNextExceptionChain() {
        SQLException wrapper = new SQLException("batch failed", "99999");
        wrapper.setNextException(new SQLException("duplicate", "23505"));

        DatabaseException result = SqlExceptionTranslator.translate(wrapper, "batch");
        assertInstanceOf(DuplicateKeyException.class, result);
    }
}
````

## 51. `src/test/java/org/example/sto/service/ValidationTest.java`

Строк: 54

``java
package org.example.sto.service;

import org.example.sto.exception.ValidationException;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;

/**
 * Тесты бизнес-валидации, не требующие подключения к БД:
 * проверки выполняются до обращения к репозиториям.
 */
class ValidationTest {

    private final CatalogService catalogService =
            new CatalogService(null, null, null, null, null);
    private final OrderService orderService =
            new OrderService(null, null, null, null, null);

    @Test
    void rejectsEmptyClientName() {
        assertThrows(ValidationException.class,
                () -> catalogService.createClient("  ", "+7-900-000-00-00", null));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsInvalidPhone() {
        assertThrows(ValidationException.class,
                () -> catalogService.createClient("Иванов Иван", "телефон", null));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsNullServicePrice() {
        assertThrows(ValidationException.class,
                () -> catalogService.createService("Услуга", null, 30));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsNegativeServicePrice() {
        assertThrows(ValidationException.class,
                () -> catalogService.createService("Услуга", new BigDecimal("-1.00"), 30));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsNonPositiveQuantity() {
        assertThrows(ValidationException.class,
                () -> orderService.addService(1, 1, 0));
        assertThrows(ValidationException.class,
                () -> orderService.addService(1, 1, -5));
        assertThrows(ValidationException.class,
                () -> orderService.addService(1, 1, 101));
    }
}
````
