Строк: 1617

``css
@import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Manrope:wght@400;500;600;700;800&display=swap');

:root {
  --bg: #f3f6fb;
  --bg-soft: #eef2f9;
  --surface: #ffffff;
  --surface-2: #f8fafd;
  --ink: #0f172a;
  --ink-soft: #334155;
  --muted: #7b8aa0;
  --line: #e5ebf4;
  --line-strong: #d6dfec;
  --brand: #2463eb;
  --brand-dark: #1d4ed8;
  --brand-soft: #e8efff;
  --teal: #0ea5a5;
  --teal-soft: #e2f8f7;
  --accent: #f97316;
  --accent-soft: #fff1e7;
  --success: #10b981;
  --success-soft: #e7f8f1;
  --warn: #f59e0b;
  --warn-soft: #fef5e3;
  --danger: #ef4444;
  --danger-soft: #fdeceb;
  --violet: #7c3aed;
  --violet-soft: #f0eafd;
  --sidebar: #0b1220;
  --sidebar-2: #111b2e;
  --radius-xl: 24px;
  --radius-lg: 18px;
  --radius-md: 13px;
  --shadow-sm: 0 1px 2px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.04), 0 4px 16px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.04);
  --shadow-md: 0 16px 40px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.09);
  --shadow-lg: 0 30px 80px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.18);
  --font: 'Manrope', 'Segoe UI', system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif;
}

* {
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

html,
body {
  margin: 0;
  padding: 0;
  min-height: 100%;
}

body {
  font-family: var(--font);
  color: var(--ink);
  background: var(--bg);
  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
  text-rendering: optimizeLegibility;
}

button,
input,
select,
textarea {
  font: inherit;
}

button {
  cursor: pointer;
}

a {
  color: inherit;
  text-decoration: none;
}

::selection {
  background: #cddcff;
  color: #0b1220;
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- layout */

.shell {
  min-height: 100vh;
  display: flex;
}

.sidebar {
  position: fixed;
  inset: 0 auto 0 0;
  z-index: 40;
  width: 272px;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  padding: 24px 18px 18px;
  color: #dbe4f4;
  background:
    radial-gradient(circle at 0% 0%, rgba(36, 99, 235, 0.25), transparent 34%),
    radial-gradient(circle at 100% 100%, rgba(14, 165, 165, 0.13), transparent 40%),
    linear-gradient(180deg, var(--sidebar) 0%, var(--sidebar-2) 100%);
  border-right: 1px solid rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.12);
  overflow-y: auto;
}

.sidebar-brand {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
  padding: 4px 10px 24px;
}

.brand-mark {
  width: 42px;
  height: 42px;
  border-radius: 14px;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  color: #fff;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #3b82f6 0%, #06b6d4 100%);
  box-shadow: 0 10px 24px rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.4);
}

.brand-text strong {
  display: block;
  font-size: 16px;
  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
  color: #fff;
}

.brand-text span {
  display: block;
  margin-top: 2px;
  font-size: 11px;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: #7f8daa;
}

.nav-caption {
  padding: 16px 12px 8px;
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: 0.16em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: #64748b;
}

.nav {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 4px;
}

.nav-item {
  position: relative;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
  padding: 11px 12px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  color: #a8b5cc;
  font-size: 13.5px;
  font-weight: 600;
  transition: background 0.18s ease, color 0.18s ease, transform 0.18s ease;
}

.nav-item:hover {
  color: #f8fbff;
  background: rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.12);
  transform: translateX(2px);
}

.nav-item.active {
  color: #fff;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.26), rgba(14, 165, 165, 0.13));
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(148, 197, 255, 0.16);
}

.nav-item.active::before {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  left: -18px;
  top: 50%;
  width: 3px;
  height: 24px;
  transform: translateY(-50%);
  border-radius: 0 4px 4px 0;
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #60a5fa, #22d3ee);
}

.nav-icon {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 26px;
  height: 26px;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.1);
}

.nav-item.active .nav-icon {
  color: #7dd3fc;
  background: rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.14);
}

.nav-badge {
  margin-left: auto;
  min-width: 21px;
  height: 21px;
  padding: 0 7px;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: #ef4444;
  color: #fff;
  font-size: 10.5px;
  font-weight: 800;
}

.sidebar-footer {
  margin-top: auto;
  padding-top: 22px;
}

.sidebar-tip {
  margin-bottom: 14px;
  padding: 15px;
  border-radius: 16px;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.16), rgba(14, 165, 165, 0.08));
  border: 1px solid rgba(125, 211, 252, 0.15);
}

.sidebar-tip strong {
  display: block;
  font-size: 12.5px;
  color: #e2e8f0;
}

.sidebar-tip span {
  display: block;
  margin-top: 5px;
  color: #8190aa;
  font-size: 11.5px;
  line-height: 1.45;
}

.user-card {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 11px;
  padding: 12px;
  border-radius: 16px;
  background: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.5);
  border: 1px solid rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.14);
}

.avatar {
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  width: 38px;
  height: 38px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  font-size: 12.5px;
  font-weight: 800;
  color: #eaf2ff;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #3b82f6, #06b6d4);
  box-shadow: 0 6px 16px rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.3);
}

.avatar.lg {
  width: 52px;
  height: 52px;
  font-size: 16px;
}

.avatar.sm {
  width: 30px;
  height: 30px;
  font-size: 10.5px;
}

.user-meta {
  min-width: 0;
  flex: 1;
}

.user-meta strong {
  display: block;
  overflow: hidden;
  white-space: nowrap;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
  font-size: 12.5px;
  color: #f1f5f9;
}

.user-meta span {
  display: block;
  margin-top: 1px;
  font-size: 10.5px;
  color: #7f8daa;
}

.logout-btn {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 32px;
  height: 32px;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 10px;
  color: #8fa0bb;
  background: rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.1);
  transition: 0.18s ease;
}

.logout-btn:hover {
  color: #fecaca;
  background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.16);
}

.main {
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
  margin-left: 272px;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
}

.topbar {
  position: sticky;
  top: 0;
  z-index: 30;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 18px;
  height: 78px;
  padding: 0 34px;
  background: rgba(243, 246, 251, 0.82);
  backdrop-filter: blur(18px);
  border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(229, 235, 244, 0.85);
}

.topbar-left {
  min-width: 0;
}

.topbar-title {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 19px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: -0.02em;
}

.topbar-sub {
  margin: 2px 0 0;
  font-size: 11.5px;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.topbar-right {
  margin-left: auto;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
}

.search {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 9px;
  width: 280px;
  height: 42px;
  padding: 0 14px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 12px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.75);
  color: var(--muted);
  transition: 0.18s ease;
}

.search:focus-within {
  border-color: #b9cdf5;
  background: #fff;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 4px rgba(36, 99, 235, 0.08);
}

.search input {
  width: 100%;
  border: 0;
  outline: 0;
  background: transparent;
  color: var(--ink);
  font-size: 13px;
}

.search input::placeholder {
  color: #9aa8bd;
}

.icon-btn {
  position: relative;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 42px;
  height: 42px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 12px;
  color: var(--ink-soft);
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.75);
  transition: 0.18s ease;
}

.icon-btn:hover {
  border-color: #c6d6f2;
  background: #fff;
  transform: translateY(-1px);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-sm);
}

.icon-btn .dot {
  position: absolute;
  top: 10px;
  right: 11px;
  width: 7px;
  height: 7px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--accent);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px #fff;
}

.mobile-menu {
  display: none;
}

.content {
  padding: 30px 34px 48px;
}

.page {
  animation: fade-up 0.35s ease both;
}

.page-head {
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-end;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 20px;
  margin-bottom: 24px;
}

.eyebrow {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 7px;
  margin-bottom: 7px;
  font-size: 10.5px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.15em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--brand);
}

.eyebrow::before {
  content: '';
  width: 16px;
  height: 2px;
  border-radius: 2px;
  background: var(--brand);
}

.page-title {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: clamp(24px, 2.4vw, 32px);
  line-height: 1.1;
  letter-spacing: -0.035em;
  font-weight: 800;
}

.page-sub {
  margin: 7px 0 0;
  max-width: 620px;
  font-size: 13px;
  line-height: 1.5;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.page-actions {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- controls */

.btn {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  gap: 8px;
  height: 42px;
  padding: 0 17px;
  border: 1px solid transparent;
  border-radius: 12px;
  font-size: 12.5px;
  font-weight: 700;
  white-space: nowrap;
  transition: 0.18s ease;
}

.btn:disabled {
  opacity: 0.55;
  cursor: not-allowed;
}

.btn-primary {
  color: #fff;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, #2f6df0, #1f56d8);
  box-shadow: 0 10px 24px rgba(36, 99, 235, 0.25);
}

.btn-primary:hover:not(:disabled) {
  transform: translateY(-1px);
  box-shadow: 0 14px 30px rgba(36, 99, 235, 0.32);
}

.btn-ghost {
  color: var(--ink-soft);
  background: #fff;
  border-color: var(--line);
}

.btn-ghost:hover:not(:disabled) {
  border-color: #c5d5ef;
  color: var(--brand-dark);
  transform: translateY(-1px);
}

.btn-danger {
  color: #b91c1c;
  background: var(--danger-soft);
  border-color: #f7c9c6;
}

.btn-danger:hover:not(:disabled) {
  background: #fbdad8;
}

.btn-sm {
  height: 34px;
  padding: 0 12px;
  border-radius: 10px;
  font-size: 11.5px;
}

.btn-icon {
  width: 36px;
  padding: 0;
}

.btn-block {
  width: 100%;
}

.tabs {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 4px;
  padding: 4px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 13px;
  background: #fff;
}

.tab {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 7px;
  height: 34px;
  padding: 0 13px;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 9px;
  background: transparent;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 700;
  transition: 0.16s ease;
}

.tab:hover {
  color: var(--ink);
  background: var(--surface-2);
}

.tab.active {
  color: var(--brand-dark);
  background: var(--brand-soft);
}

.tab-count {
  min-width: 18px;
  padding: 1px 5px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.07);
  font-size: 10px;
}

.tab.active .tab-count {
  background: rgba(36, 99, 235, 0.14);
}

/* ----------------------------------------------------------------- cards */

.card {
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: var(--radius-xl);
  background: var(--surface);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-sm);
}

.card-head {
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 16px;
  padding: 22px 24px 0;
}

.card-title {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 14.5px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
}

.card-sub {
  margin: 4px 0 0;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 11.5px;
}

.card-body {
  padding: 20px 24px 24px;
}

.card-link {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 5px;
  border: 0;
  background: transparent;
  color: var(--brand);
  font-size: 11.5px;
  font-weight: 800;
}

.card-link:hover {
  color: var(--brand-dark);
}

.stat-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr));
  gap: 16px;
  margin-bottom: 20px;
}

.stat-card {
  position: relative;
  overflow: hidden;
  padding: 20px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
  background: var(--surface);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-sm);
  transition: transform 0.2s ease, box-shadow 0.2s ease;
}

.stat-card:hover {
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-md);
}

.stat-card::after {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  top: -38px;
  right: -38px;
  width: 104px;
  height: 104px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(36, 99, 235, 0.1), transparent 68%);
}

.stat-top {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 10px;
}

.stat-icon {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 40px;
  height: 40px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  color: var(--brand);
  background: var(--brand-soft);
}

.stat-icon.teal {
  color: #0f8f8f;
  background: var(--teal-soft);
}

.stat-icon.orange {
  color: #c2410c;
  background: var(--accent-soft);
}

.stat-icon.violet {
  color: #6d28d9;
  background: var(--violet-soft);
}

.stat-icon.green {
  color: #047857;
  background: var(--success-soft);
}

.stat-label {
  font-size: 11.5px;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.stat-value {
  margin-top: 13px;
  font-size: 26px;
  line-height: 1;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: -0.04em;
}

.stat-foot {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  margin-top: 9px;
  font-size: 10.5px;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.stat-delta {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 3px;
  color: #047857;
  font-weight: 800;
}

.stat-delta.down {
  color: #b91c1c;
}

.grid-2 {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1.55fr) minmax(300px, 1fr);
  gap: 20px;
  align-items: start;
}

.grid-3 {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
  gap: 16px;
}

.grid-4 {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr));
  gap: 16px;
}

.stack {
  display: grid;
  gap: 20px;
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- badges */

.badge {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 5px;
  padding: 5px 9px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  font-size: 10.5px;
  font-weight: 800;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.badge::before {
  content: '';
  width: 5px;
  height: 5px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: currentColor;
}

.badge.new {
  color: #1d4ed8;
  background: var(--brand-soft);
}

.badge.progress {
  color: #b45309;
  background: var(--warn-soft);
}

.badge.done {
  color: #047857;
  background: var(--success-soft);
}

.badge.cancel {
  color: #b91c1c;
  background: var(--danger-soft);
}

.badge.neutral {
  color: #475569;
  background: #eef2f7;
}

.badge.plain {
  padding: 4px 8px;
  color: var(--muted);
  background: var(--surface-2);
}

.badge.plain::before {
  display: none;
}

.chip {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 6px;
  padding: 5px 9px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: var(--surface-2);
  color: var(--ink-soft);
  font-size: 10.5px;
  font-weight: 700;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.chip.plate {
  color: #1e293b;
  background: #fff;
  border-color: #d7deea;
  font-family: 'Consolas', 'SFMono-Regular', monospace;
  letter-spacing: 0.04em;
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- tables */

.table-wrap {
  overflow-x: auto;
}

.data-table {
  width: 100%;
  border-collapse: collapse;
  font-size: 12.5px;
}

.data-table th {
  padding: 12px 14px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
  text-align: left;
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.1em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: #8a97ab;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.data-table td {
  padding: 14px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid #eef2f8;
  vertical-align: middle;
}

.data-table tbody tr {
  transition: background 0.15s ease;
}

.data-table tbody tr:hover {
  background: #f9fbff;
}

.data-table tbody tr:last-child td {
  border-bottom: 0;
}

.data-table .strong {
  font-weight: 700;
  color: var(--ink);
}

.data-table .sub {
  display: block;
  margin-top: 2px;
  font-size: 10.5px;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.data-table .right {
  text-align: right;
}

.row-link {
  cursor: pointer;
}

/* ----------------------------------------------------------------- forms */

.field {
  display: grid;
  gap: 6px;
}

.label {
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 800;
  color: var(--ink-soft);
}

.input,
.select,
.textarea {
  width: 100%;
  border: 1px solid var(--line-strong);
  border-radius: 11px;
  background: #fff;
  color: var(--ink);
  font-size: 13px;
  transition: 0.16s ease;
}

.input,
.select {
  height: 42px;
  padding: 0 12px;
}

.textarea {
  min-height: 96px;
  padding: 11px 12px;
  resize: vertical;
}

.input:focus,
.select:focus,
.textarea:focus {
  outline: 0;
  border-color: #93b4f8;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 4px rgba(36, 99, 235, 0.09);
}

.input::placeholder,
.textarea::placeholder {
  color: #a4b0c2;
}

.form-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  gap: 14px;
}

.form-grid .span-2 {
  grid-column: 1 / -1;
}

.hint {
  font-size: 10.5px;
  line-height: 1.45;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.error-text {
  padding: 10px 12px;
  border: 1px solid #f6c8c5;
  border-radius: 10px;
  background: var(--danger-soft);
  color: #991b1b;
  font-size: 11.5px;
  font-weight: 600;
}

/* ----------------------------------------------------------------- modal */

.modal-backdrop {
  position: fixed;
  inset: 0;
  z-index: 80;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  padding: 22px;
  background: rgba(8, 15, 30, 0.55);
  backdrop-filter: blur(6px);
  animation: fade-in 0.18s ease both;
}

.modal {
  width: min(680px, 100%);
  max-height: min(86vh, 780px);
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  overflow: hidden;
  border: 1px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.6);
  border-radius: 22px;
  background: #fff;
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-lg);
  animation: modal-up 0.24s cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.9, 0.25, 1) both;
}

.modal.wide {
  width: min(820px, 100%);
}

.modal-head {
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 18px;
  padding: 22px 24px 18px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
}

.modal-head h2 {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 18px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: -0.02em;
}

.modal-head p {
  margin: 4px 0 0;
  font-size: 11.5px;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.modal-body {
  padding: 20px 24px;
  overflow-y: auto;
}

.modal-foot {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: flex-end;
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 16px 24px;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--line);
  background: #fbfcfe;
}

.modal-close {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 34px;
  height: 34px;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 10px;
  color: var(--muted);
  background: var(--surface-2);
  transition: 0.16s ease;
}

.modal-close:hover {
  color: var(--ink);
  background: #eef2f8;
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- states */

.empty {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  gap: 9px;
  padding: 52px 24px;
  text-align: center;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 12.5px;
}

.empty-icon {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 52px;
  height: 52px;
  border-radius: 16px;
  color: var(--brand);
  background: var(--brand-soft);
}

.empty strong {
  color: var(--ink);
  font-size: 14px;
}

.skeleton {
  border-radius: 12px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #eef2f8 25%, #f7f9fc 37%, #eef2f8 63%);
  background-size: 400% 100%;
  animation: shimmer 1.3s ease infinite;
}

.skeleton-card {
  height: 142px;
}

.skeleton-row {
  height: 46px;
  margin-bottom: 10px;
}

.spinner {
  width: 20px;
  height: 20px;
  border: 2px solid rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.35);
  border-top-color: #fff;
  border-radius: 50%;
  animation: spin 0.7s linear infinite;
}

.spinner.dark {
  border-color: rgba(36, 99, 235, 0.2);
  border-top-color: var(--brand);
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- charts */

.donut-wrap {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 22px;
}

.donut {
  position: relative;
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  width: 132px;
  height: 132px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
}

.donut::after {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  inset: 17px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: #fff;
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px #eef2f8;
}

.donut-center {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1;
  text-align: center;
}

.donut-center strong {
  display: block;
  font-size: 23px;
  line-height: 1;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: -0.04em;
}

.donut-center span {
  display: block;
  margin-top: 4px;
  font-size: 9.5px;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: 0.1em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.legend {
  display: grid;
  gap: 10px;
  min-width: 0;
  flex: 1;
}

.legend-row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 9px;
  font-size: 11.5px;
}

.legend-dot {
  width: 8px;
  height: 8px;
  border-radius: 3px;
  background: var(--brand);
}

.legend-row strong {
  margin-left: auto;
  font-size: 11.5px;
}

.bars {
  display: grid;
  gap: 14px;
}

.bar-row {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(90px, 150px) 1fr auto;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
  font-size: 11.5px;
}

.bar-track {
  height: 8px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: #eef2f8;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.bar-fill {
  height: 100%;
  border-radius: inherit;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #3b82f6, #22d3ee);
}

.bar-value {
  font-weight: 800;
  color: var(--ink-soft);
  white-space: nowrap;
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- timeline */

.timeline {
  display: grid;
  gap: 0;
}

.timeline-item {
  position: relative;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 30px 1fr;
  gap: 12px;
  padding-bottom: 20px;
}

.timeline-item:last-child {
  padding-bottom: 0;
}

.timeline-item::before {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  left: 14px;
  top: 30px;
  bottom: 0;
  width: 2px;
  background: var(--line);
}

.timeline-item:last-child::before {
  display: none;
}

.timeline-dot {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 30px;
  height: 30px;
  border-radius: 10px;
  color: var(--brand);
  background: var(--brand-soft);
}

.timeline-body strong {
  display: block;
  font-size: 12.5px;
}

.timeline-body span {
  display: block;
  margin-top: 3px;
  font-size: 10.5px;
  color: var(--muted);
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- toasts */

.toast-stack {
  position: fixed;
  z-index: 120;
  right: 22px;
  bottom: 22px;
  display: grid;
  gap: 10px;
  width: min(380px, calc(100vw - 44px));
}

.toast {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 12px 13px;
  border: 1px solid #cdeee0;
  border-radius: 13px;
  background: #f2fcf8;
  color: #065f46;
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-md);
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 600;
  animation: modal-up 0.22s ease both;
}

.toast-error {
  border-color: #f6c8c5;
  background: #fff5f4;
  color: #991b1b;
}

.toast-info {
  border-color: #cdddf9;
  background: #f4f8ff;
  color: #1e40af;
}

.toast-icon {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  width: 28px;
  height: 28px;
  border-radius: 9px;
  background: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.12);
}

.toast-error .toast-icon {
  background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.12);
}

.toast-info .toast-icon {
  background: rgba(36, 99, 235, 0.1);
}

.toast-close {
  margin-left: auto;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 24px;
  height: 24px;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 7px;
  color: inherit;
  background: transparent;
  opacity: 0.55;
}

.toast-close:hover {
  opacity: 1;
  background: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.06);
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- helpers */

.muted {
  color: var(--muted);
}

.mono {
  font-family: 'Consolas', 'SFMono-Regular', monospace;
}

.right {
  text-align: right;
}

.spacer {
  flex: 1;
}

.divider {
  height: 1px;
  background: var(--line);
}

.pill-row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 7px;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
}

.detail-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  gap: 14px;
}

.detail-item {
  padding: 12px 14px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 12px;
  background: var(--surface-2);
}

.detail-item span {
  display: block;
  font-size: 9.5px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.1em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.detail-item strong {
  display: block;
  margin-top: 5px;
  font-size: 12.5px;
  font-weight: 700;
}

.loading-line {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 9px;
  padding: 30px;
  justify-content: center;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 12px;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------- responsive */

@media (max-width: 1180px) {
  .stat-grid {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }

  .grid-2 {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .grid-4 {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }
}

@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .sidebar {
    transform: translateX(-100%);
    transition: transform 0.24s cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.9, 0.25, 1);
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-lg);
  }

  .sidebar.open {
    transform: translateX(0);
  }

  .sidebar-overlay {
    position: fixed;
    inset: 0;
    z-index: 35;
    background: rgba(8, 15, 30, 0.45);
    backdrop-filter: blur(3px);
  }

  .main {
    margin-left: 0;
  }

  .mobile-menu {
    display: grid;
  }

  .topbar {
    padding: 0 18px;
    height: 68px;
  }

  .content {
    padding: 22px 18px 40px;
  }

  .search {
    display: none;
  }

  .page-head {
    align-items: flex-start;
    flex-direction: column;
  }

  .grid-3 {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 680px) {
  .stat-grid,
  .grid-4 {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .form-grid,
  .detail-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .topbar-date {
    display: none;
  }

  .modal-backdrop {
    padding: 10px;
    align-items: end;
  }

  .modal {
    max-height: 92vh;
    border-radius: 20px 20px 0 0;
  }

  .tabs {
    width: 100%;
    overflow-x: auto;
  }

  .bar-row {
    grid-template-columns: minmax(84px, 120px) 1fr;
  }

  .bar-value {
    display: none;
  }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------- animations */

@keyframes fade-up {
  from {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(10px);
  }
  to {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: translateY(0);
  }
}

@keyframes fade-in {
  from {
    opacity: 0;
  }
  to {
    opacity: 1;
  }
}

@keyframes modal-up {
  from {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(14px) scale(0.985);
  }
  to {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: translateY(0) scale(1);
  }
}

@keyframes shimmer {
  0% {
    background-position: 100% 0;
  }
  100% {
    background-position: -100% 0;
  }
}

@keyframes spin {
  to {
    transform: rotate(360deg);
  }
}

@keyframes pulse-soft {
  0%,
  100% {
    opacity: 0.55;
  }
  50% {
    opacity: 1;
  }
}
````

import { ApplicationConfig, provideBrowserGlobalErrorListeners, provideZoneChangeDetection } from '@angular/core';
import { provideHttpClient } from '@angular/common/http';
import { provideRouter, withInMemoryScrolling } from '@angular/router';

import { routes } from './app.routes';

export const appConfig: ApplicationConfig = {
  providers: [
    provideBrowserGlobalErrorListeners(),
    provideZoneChangeDetection({ eventCoalescing: true }),
    provideHttpClient(),
    provideRouter(routes, withInMemoryScrolling({ scrollPositionRestoration: 'top' })),
  ],
};
````

``ts
import { Routes } from '@angular/router';
import { authGuard } from './core/auth.guard';
import { Shell } from './layout/shell';
import { Login } from './pages/login/login';
import { Dashboard } from './pages/dashboard/dashboard';
import { Orders } from './pages/orders/orders';
import { Clients } from './pages/clients/clients';
import { Cars } from './pages/cars/cars';
import { ServicesPage } from './pages/services/services';
import { Masters } from './pages/masters/masters';
import { StatusLogPage } from './pages/status-log/status-log';

export const routes: Routes = [
  { path: 'login', component: Login, title: 'Вход — СТО' },
  {
    path: '',
    component: Shell,
    canActivate: [authGuard],
    children: [
      { path: '', pathMatch: 'full', redirectTo: 'dashboard' },
      { path: 'dashboard', component: Dashboard, title: 'Обзор — СТО' },
      { path: 'orders', component: Orders, title: 'Заявки — СТО' },
      { path: 'clients', component: Clients, title: 'Клиенты — СТО' },
      { path: 'cars', component: Cars, title: 'Автомобили — СТО' },
      { path: 'services', component: ServicesPage, title: 'Услуги — СТО' },
      { path: 'masters', component: Masters, title: 'Мастера — СТО' },
      { path: 'log', component: StatusLogPage, title: 'Журнал — СТО' },
    ],
  },
  { path: '**', redirectTo: '' },
];
````

## 64. `frontend/src/app/core/models.ts`

Строк: 201

``ts
export interface User {
  id: number;
  username: string;
  fullName: string;
  role: string;
}

export interface OrderSummary {
  orderId: number;
  client: string;
  car: string;
  status: string;
  totalCost: number;
}

export interface Client {
  id: number;
  fullName: string;
  phone: string;
  email: string | null;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface ClientOrder {
  orderId: number;
  carPlate: string;
  status: string;
  totalCost: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

export interface ClientDetail {
  client: Client;
  cars: Car[];
  orders: ClientOrder[];
}

export interface Car {
  id: number;
  clientId: number;
  brand: string;
  model: string;
  year: number | null;
  plateNum: string;
  vin: string | null;
}

export interface CarView extends Car {
  clientName: string;
}

export interface Service {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  price: number;
  durationMin: number | null;
}

export interface Master {
  id: number;
  userId: number;
  fullName: string;
  specialization: string | null;
  active: boolean;
}

export interface OrderStatus {
  id: number;
  name: string;
}

export interface Order {
  id: number;
  clientId: number;
  carId: number;
  masterId: number | null;
  statusId: number;
  statusName: string;
  description: string;
  createdAt: string;
  closedAt: string | null;
}

export interface OrderServiceItem {
  id: number;
  serviceId: number;
  serviceName: string;
  quantity: number;
  priceAt: number;
  lineTotal: number;
}

export interface OrderDetail {
  order: Order;
  client: Client;
  car: Car;
  masterName: string | null;
  total: number;
  services: OrderServiceItem[];
}

export interface StatusLogEntry {
  id: number;
  orderId: number;
  oldStatusId: number | null;
  newStatusId: number | null;
  changedAt: string;
  changedBy: string;
}

export interface Health {
  status: string;
  database: string;
}

export interface OrderRow extends OrderSummary {
  description?: string;
}

export const STATUS_CLASS: Record<string, string> = {
  'новая': 'new',
  'в работе': 'progress',
  'завершена': 'done',
  'отменена': 'cancel',
};

export function statusClass(status: string): string {
  return STATUS_CLASS[status] ?? 'neutral';
}

export function initials(name: string): string {
  return name
    .split(/\s+/)
    .filter(Boolean)
    .slice(0, 2)
    .map((part) => part[0]?.toUpperCase() ?? '')
    .join('');
}

/**
 * Выбирает форму слова для русского счётного оборота.
 * pluralWord(1, 'заявка', 'заявки', 'заявок') → «заявка»
 */
export function pluralWord(
  count: number,
  one: string,
  few: string,
  many: string,
): string {
  const abs = Math.abs(count) % 100;
  const last = abs % 10;
  if (abs > 10 && abs < 20) {
    return many;
  }
  if (last === 1) {
    return one;
  }
  return last >= 2 && last <= 4 ? few : many;
}

/** Счётчик вместе с правильной формой слова: plural(2, 'услуга', 'услуги', 'услуг') → «2 услуги». */
export function plural(
  count: number,
  one: string,
  few: string,
  many: string,
): string {
  return `${count} ${pluralWord(count, one, few, many)}`;
}

export function money(value: number | null | undefined): string {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('ru-RU', {
    style: 'currency',
    currency: 'RUB',
    maximumFractionDigits: 0,
  }).format(value ?? 0);
}

export function shortDate(value: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!value) {
    return '—';
  }
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ru-RU', {
    day: '2-digit',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
  }).format(new Date(value));
}

export function dateTime(value: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!value) {
    return '—';
  }
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ru-RU', {
    day: '2-digit',
    month: '2-digit',
    year: 'numeric',
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit',
  }).format(new Date(value));
}
````

## 65. `frontend/src/app/core/api.service.ts`

Строк: 126

``ts
import { HttpClient, HttpErrorResponse } from '@angular/common/http';
import { Injectable, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';
import {
  CarView,
  Client,
  ClientDetail,
  Health,
  Master,
  OrderDetail,
  OrderStatus,
  OrderSummary,
  Service,
  StatusLogEntry,
  User,
} from './models';

export const API_BASE = 'http://localhost:8080/api';

@Injectable({ providedIn: 'root' })
export class ApiService {
  private readonly http = inject(HttpClient);

  health(): Observable<Health> {
    return this.http.get<Health>(`${API_BASE}/health`);
  }

  login(username: string, password: string): Observable<User> {
    return this.http.post<User>(`${API_BASE}/login`, { username, password });
  }

  orders(): Observable<OrderSummary[]> {
    return this.http.get<OrderSummary[]>(`${API_BASE}/orders`);
  }

  order(id: number): Observable<OrderDetail> {
    return this.http.get<OrderDetail>(`${API_BASE}/orders/${id}`);
  }

  createOrder(payload: {
    clientId: number;
    carId: number;
    masterId: number | null;
    description: string;
  }): Observable<{ orderId: number }> {
    return this.http.post<{ orderId: number }>(`${API_BASE}/orders`, payload);
  }

  addService(orderId: number, serviceId: number, quantity: number): Observable<unknown> {
    return this.http.post(`${API_BASE}/orders/${orderId}/services`, { serviceId, quantity });
  }

  closeOrder(orderId: number): Observable<unknown> {
    return this.http.post(`${API_BASE}/orders/${orderId}/close`, {});
  }

  clients(): Observable<Client[]> {
    return this.http.get<Client[]>(`${API_BASE}/clients`);
  }

  client(id: number): Observable<ClientDetail> {
    return this.http.get<ClientDetail>(`${API_BASE}/clients/${id}`);
  }

  cars(): Observable<CarView[]> {
    return this.http.get<CarView[]>(`${API_BASE}/cars`);
  }

  createCar(payload: {
    clientId: number;
    brand: string;
    model: string;
    year: number | null;
    plateNum: string;
    vin: string | null;
  }): Observable<CarView> {
    return this.http.post<CarView>(`${API_BASE}/cars`, payload);
  }

  services(): Observable<Service[]> {
    return this.http.get<Service[]>(`${API_BASE}/services`);
  }

  masters(): Observable<Master[]> {
    return this.http.get<Master[]>(`${API_BASE}/masters`);
  }

  masterWorkload(): Observable<Record<string, number>> {
    return this.http.get<Record<string, number>>(`${API_BASE}/masters/workload`);
  }

  statuses(): Observable<OrderStatus[]> {
    return this.http.get<OrderStatus[]>(`${API_BASE}/statuses`);
  }

  statusLog(orderId?: number): Observable<StatusLogEntry[]> {
    const suffix = orderId ? `?orderId=${orderId}` : '';
    return this.http.get<StatusLogEntry[]>(`${API_BASE}/log${suffix}`);
  }

  createClient(payload: { fullName: string; phone: string; email: string | null }): Observable<Client> {
    return this.http.post<Client>(`${API_BASE}/clients`, payload);
  }

  createService(payload: {
    name: string;
    price: number;
    durationMin: number | null;
  }): Observable<Service> {
    return this.http.post<Service>(`${API_BASE}/services`, payload);
  }
}

export function errorMessage(error: unknown): string {
  if (error instanceof HttpErrorResponse) {
    const body = error.error;
    if (body && typeof body === 'object' && 'message' in body) {
      return String((body as { message: unknown }).message);
    }
    if (error.status === 0) {
      return 'Нет связи с API. Запустите: java -jar target/sto-db-module.jar --web';
    }
    return `Ошибка ${error.status}: ${error.statusText}`;
  }
  return error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Неизвестная ошибка';
}
````

## 66. `frontend/src/app/core/auth.service.ts`

Строк: 44

``ts
import { Injectable, inject, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { Router } from '@angular/router';
import { Observable, tap } from 'rxjs';
import { ApiService } from './api.service';
import { User } from './models';

const STORAGE_KEY = 'sto_user';

@Injectable({ providedIn: 'root' })
export class AuthService {
  private readonly api = inject(ApiService);
  private readonly router = inject(Router);
  private readonly currentUser = signal<User | null>(this.restore());

  readonly user = this.currentUser.asReadonly();

  login(username: string, password: string): Observable<User> {
    return this.api.login(username.trim(), password).pipe(
      tap((user) => {
        this.currentUser.set(user);
        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(user));
      }),
    );
  }

  logout(): void {
    this.currentUser.set(null);
    localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    void this.router.navigate(['/login']);
  }

  isAuthenticated(): boolean {
    return this.currentUser() !== null;
  }

  private restore(): User | null {
    try {
      const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
      return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as User) : null;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }
}
````

import { inject } from '@angular/core';
import { CanActivateFn, Router } from '@angular/router';
import { AuthService } from './auth.service';

export const authGuard: CanActivateFn = () => {
  const auth = inject(AuthService);
  const router = inject(Router);
  return auth.isAuthenticated() ? true : router.createUrlTree(['/login']);
};

## 68. `frontend/src/app/core/toast.service.ts`

Строк: 33

``ts
import { Injectable, signal } from '@angular/core';

export type ToastKind = 'success' | 'error' | 'info';

export interface Toast {
  id: number;
  text: string;
  kind: ToastKind;
}

@Injectable({ providedIn: 'root' })
export class ToastService {
  private seq = 0;
  readonly toasts = signal<Toast[]>([]);

  show(text: string, kind: ToastKind = 'success'): void {
    const id = ++this.seq;
    this.toasts.update((items) => [...items, { id, text, kind }]);
    window.setTimeout(() => this.dismiss(id), 4200);
  }

  success(text: string): void {
    this.show(text, 'success');
  }

  error(text: string): void {
    this.show(text, 'error');
  }

  dismiss(id: number): void {
    this.toasts.update((items) => items.filter((item) => item.id !== id));
  }
}
````

## 71. `frontend/src/app/layout/shell.ts`

Строк: 124

``ts
import { Component, computed, inject, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { NavigationEnd, Router, RouterLink, RouterLinkActive, RouterOutlet } from '@angular/router';
import { filter } from 'rxjs';
import { ApiService } from '../core/api.service';
import { AuthService } from '../core/auth.service';
import { initials, OrderSummary } from '../core/models';
import { Icon } from '../shared/icon';

interface NavItem {
  path: string;
  label: string;
  icon: string;
  exact?: boolean;
}

const PAGE_META: Record<string, { title: string; sub: string }> = {
  dashboard: { title: 'Обзор', sub: 'Главные показатели и события сервиса' },
  orders: { title: 'Заявки', sub: 'Приём, сопровождение и закрытие заказов' },
  clients: { title: 'Клиенты', sub: 'База владельцев автомобилей' },
  cars: { title: 'Автомобили', sub: 'Автопарк клиентов сервиса' },
  services: { title: 'Услуги', sub: 'Прайс-лист и длительность работ' },
  masters: { title: 'Мастера', sub: 'Команда и специализации' },
  log: { title: 'Журнал статусов', sub: 'История изменений, записанная триггерами БД' },
};

@Component({
  selector: 'app-shell',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [RouterOutlet, RouterLink, RouterLinkActive, Icon],
  templateUrl: './shell.html',
  styleUrl: './shell.css',
})
export class Shell {
  readonly auth = inject(AuthService);
  private readonly api = inject(ApiService);
  private readonly router = inject(Router);

  readonly initials = initials;
  readonly menuOpen = signal(false);
  readonly search = signal('');
  readonly orders = signal<OrderSummary[]>([]);
  readonly databaseOnline = signal<boolean | null>(null);

  readonly title = signal(PAGE_META['dashboard'].title);
  readonly subtitle = signal(PAGE_META['dashboard'].sub);

  readonly today = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ru-RU', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'long',
    year: 'numeric',
  }).format(new Date());

  readonly nav: NavItem[] = [
    { path: '/dashboard', label: 'Обзор', icon: 'dashboard', exact: true },
    { path: '/orders', label: 'Заявки', icon: 'orders' },
    { path: '/clients', label: 'Клиенты', icon: 'clients' },
    { path: '/cars', label: 'Автомобили', icon: 'car' },
    { path: '/services', label: 'Услуги', icon: 'services' },
    { path: '/masters', label: 'Мастера', icon: 'masters' },
    { path: '/log', label: 'Журнал', icon: 'history' },
  ];

  readonly activeCount = computed(
    () =>
      this.orders().filter((order) => order.status !== 'завершена' && order.status !== 'отменена')
        .length,
  );

  constructor() {
    this.refresh();
    this.api.health().subscribe({
      next: () => this.databaseOnline.set(true),
      error: () => this.databaseOnline.set(false),
    });
    this.router.events
      .pipe(filter((event): event is NavigationEnd => event instanceof NavigationEnd))
      .subscribe((event) => {
        this.menuOpen.set(false);
        this.updateMeta(event.urlAfterRedirects);
        this.refresh();
      });
    this.updateMeta(this.router.url);
  }

  refresh(): void {
    this.api.orders().subscribe({
      next: (orders) => this.orders.set(orders),
      error: () => this.orders.set([]),
    });
  }

  goSearch(event: Event): void {
    event.preventDefault();
    const query = this.search().trim();
    this.search.set('');
    void this.router.navigate(['/orders'], { queryParams: query ? { q: query } : {} });
  }

  logout(): void {
    this.auth.logout();
  }

  roleLabel(role: string | undefined): string {
    switch (role) {
      case 'admin':
        return 'Администратор';
      case 'manager':
        return 'Менеджер';
      case 'master':
        return 'Мастер';
      case 'client':
        return 'Клиент';
      default:
        return 'Пользователь';
    }
  }

  private updateMeta(url: string): void {
    const segment = url.split('?')[0].split('/').filter(Boolean)[0] ?? 'dashboard';
    const meta = PAGE_META[segment] ?? PAGE_META['dashboard'];
    this.title.set(meta.title);
    this.subtitle.set(meta.sub);
  }
}
````

<div class="shell">
  @if (menuOpen()) {
    <div class="sidebar-overlay" (click)="menuOpen.set(false)"></div>
  }

  <aside class="sidebar" [class.open]="menuOpen()">
    <a class="sidebar-brand" routerLink="/dashboard">
      <span class="brand-mark">
        <app-icon name="car" [size]="24" />
      </span>
      <span class="brand-text">
        <strong>СТО · Гараж</strong>
        <span>учёт и заявки</span>
      </span>
    </a>

    <div class="nav-caption">Рабочее пространство</div>
    <nav class="nav">
      @for (item of nav; track item.path) {
        <a
          class="nav-item"
          [routerLink]="item.path"
          routerLinkActive="active"
          [routerLinkActiveOptions]="{ exact: item.exact === true }"
        >
          <span class="nav-icon"><app-icon [name]="item.icon" [size]="17" /></span>
          <span>{{ item.label }}</span>
          @if (item.path === '/orders' && activeCount() > 0) {
            <span class="nav-badge">{{ activeCount() }}</span>
          }
        </a>
      }
    </nav>

    <div class="sidebar-footer">
      <div class="sidebar-tip">
        <strong>
          <span class="status-dot" [class.offline]="databaseOnline() === false"></span>
          {{ databaseOnline() === false ? 'База недоступна' : 'PostgreSQL подключён' }}
        </strong>
        <span>Данные приходят из реальной базы СТО через REST API модуля доступа.</span>
      </div>

      <div class="user-card">
        <span class="avatar">{{ initials(auth.user()?.fullName ?? 'Гость') }}</span>
        <span class="user-meta">
          <strong>{{ auth.user()?.fullName ?? 'Гость' }}</strong>
          <span>{{ roleLabel(auth.user()?.role) }}</span>
        </span>
        <button type="button" class="logout-btn" (click)="logout()" title="Выйти">
          <app-icon name="logout" [size]="16" />
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </aside>

  <div class="main">
    <header class="topbar">
      <button
        type="button"
        class="icon-btn mobile-menu"
        (click)="menuOpen.set(true)"
        aria-label="Открыть меню"
      >
        <app-icon name="menu" [size]="19" />
      </button>

      <div class="topbar-left">
        <h1 class="topbar-title">{{ title() }}</h1>
        <p class="topbar-sub">{{ subtitle() }}</p>
      </div>

      <div class="topbar-right">
        <form class="search" (submit)="goSearch($event)">
          <app-icon name="search" [size]="17" />
          <input
            type="search"
            placeholder="Поиск по заявкам и клиентам…"
            [value]="search()"
            (input)="search.set($any($event.target).value)"
            aria-label="Поиск"
          />
        </form>

        <span class="topbar-date">
          <app-icon name="calendar" [size]="15" />
          {{ today }}
        </span>

        <a class="icon-btn" routerLink="/orders" title="Активные заявки">
          <app-icon name="bell" [size]="18" />
          @if (activeCount() > 0) {
            <span class="dot"></span>
          }
        </a>

        <a class="btn btn-primary" routerLink="/orders" [queryParams]="{ new: 1 }">
          <app-icon name="plus" [size]="16" />
          Новая заявка
        </a>
      </div>
    </header>

    <main class="content">
      <router-outlet />
    </main>
  </div>
</div>
````

## 74. `frontend/src/app/pages/login/login.ts`

Строк: 70

``ts
import { Component, inject, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { Router } from '@angular/router';
import { errorMessage } from '../../core/api.service';
import { AuthService } from '../../core/auth.service';
import { ToastService } from '../../core/toast.service';
import { Icon } from '../../shared/icon';

interface DemoAccount {
  role: string;
  username: string;
  password: string;
  icon: string;
}

@Component({
  selector: 'app-login',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [FormsModule, Icon],
  templateUrl: './login.html',
  styleUrl: './login.css',
})
export class Login {
  private readonly auth = inject(AuthService);
  private readonly router = inject(Router);
  private readonly toast = inject(ToastService);

  username = 'admin';
  password = 'admin123';
  showPassword = signal(false);
  loading = signal(false);
  error = signal('');

  readonly demoAccounts: DemoAccount[] = [
    { role: 'Администратор', username: 'admin', password: 'admin123', icon: 'settings' },
    { role: 'Менеджер', username: 'manager', password: 'manager123', icon: 'orders' },
    { role: 'Мастер', username: 'master', password: 'master123', icon: 'masters' },
    { role: 'Клиент', username: 'client', password: 'client123', icon: 'clients' },
  ];

  constructor() {
    if (this.auth.isAuthenticated()) {
      void this.router.navigate(['/dashboard']);
    }
  }

  useAccount(account: DemoAccount): void {
    this.username = account.username;
    this.password = account.password;
    this.error.set('');
  }

  submit(): void {
    if (this.loading()) {
      return;
    }
    this.loading.set(true);
    this.error.set('');
    this.auth.login(this.username, this.password).subscribe({
      next: (user) => {
        this.toast.success(`С возвращением, ${user.fullName.split(' ')[0]}!`);
        void this.router.navigate(['/dashboard']);
      },
      error: (error) => {
        this.loading.set(false);
        this.error.set(errorMessage(error));
      },
    });
  }
}
````

        </label>

        <label class="field">
          <span class="label">Пароль</span>
          <span class="input-wrap">
            <app-icon name="eyeOff" [size]="17" />
            <input
              class="input"
              name="password"
              [type]="showPassword() ? 'text' : 'password'"
              autocomplete="current-password"
              [(ngModel)]="password"
              placeholder="••••••••"
              required
            />
            <button
              type="button"
              class="input-action"
              (click)="showPassword.set(!showPassword())"
              [attr.aria-label]="showPassword() ? 'Скрыть пароль' : 'Показать пароль'"
            >
              <app-icon [name]="showPassword() ? 'eyeOff' : 'eye'" [size]="17" />
            </button>
          </span>
        </label>

        @if (error()) {
          <div class="error-text">
            <app-icon name="alert" [size]="15" />
            {{ error() }}
          </div>
        }

        <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary btn-block login-submit" [disabled]="loading()">
          @if (loading()) {
            <span class="spinner"></span>
            Проверяем…
          } @else {
            Войти в систему
            <app-icon name="arrow" [size]="16" />
          }
        </button>
      </form>

      <div class="demo-block">
        <div class="demo-head">
          <span>Демонстрационные учётные записи</span>
          <span class="demo-hint">нажмите, чтобы подставить</span>
        </div>
        <div class="demo-grid">
          @for (account of demoAccounts; track account.username) {
            <button type="button" class="demo-card" (click)="useAccount(account)">
              <span class="demo-icon"><app-icon [name]="account.icon" [size]="16" /></span>
              <span class="demo-meta">
                <strong>{{ account.role }}</strong>
                <span>{{ account.username }}</span>
              </span>
            </button>
          }
        </div>
      </div>

      <p class="login-note">
        <app-icon name="database" [size]="14" />
        Для входа нужен запущенный API:
        <code>java -jar sto-db-module.jar --web</code>
      </p>
    </div>
  </section>
</div>
````

``css
.login-page {
  min-height: 100vh;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1.08fr) minmax(420px, 0.92fr);
  background: var(--bg);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ hero */

.login-hero {
  position: relative;
  overflow: hidden;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  padding: 34px 46px 30px;
  color: #e8eefb;
  background:
    radial-gradient(circle at 12% 8%, rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.35), transparent 34%),
    radial-gradient(circle at 92% 88%, rgba(14, 165, 165, 0.24), transparent 42%),
    linear-gradient(160deg, #0a1120 0%, #0d1730 55%, #0a1222 100%);
}

.login-hero::after {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
  background-image: linear-gradient(rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.05) 1px, transparent 1px),
    linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.05) 1px, transparent 1px);
  background-size: 46px 46px;
  mask-image: radial-gradient(circle at 50% 40%, #000 20%, transparent 78%);
}

.hero-glow {
  position: absolute;
  border-radius: 50%;
  filter: blur(10px);
  pointer-events: none;
}

.hero-glow-a {
  top: -180px;
  right: -140px;
  width: 420px;
  height: 420px;
  background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(37, 99, 235, 0.35), transparent 65%);
  animation: float-slow 12s ease-in-out infinite;
}

.hero-glow-b {
  bottom: -220px;
  left: -120px;
  width: 480px;
  height: 480px;
  background: radial-gradient(circle, rgba(13, 148, 136, 0.28), transparent 65%);
  animation: float-slow 16s ease-in-out infinite reverse;
}

.hero-top {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 16px;
}

.hero-brand {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
}

.hero-badge {
  padding: 7px 12px;
  border: 1px solid rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.22);
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.45);
  color: #93a5c4;
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.13em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.hero-copy {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1;
  max-width: 620px;
  margin: auto 0;
  padding: 54px 0;
}

.hero-eyebrow {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 9px;

  color: #7dd3fc;
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.16em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.hero-eyebrow span {
  width: 28px;
  height: 2px;
  border-radius: 2px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #38bdf8, #22d3ee);
}

.hero-copy h1 {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: clamp(38px, 4.6vw, 62px);
  line-height: 1.02;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: -0.045em;
  color: #f8fbff;
}

.hero-copy h1 em {
  font-style: normal;
  background: linear-gradient(100deg, #60a5fa 0%, #22d3ee 60%, #5eead4 100%);
  -webkit-background-clip: text;
  background-clip: text;
  color: transparent;
}

.hero-lead {
  margin: 22px 0 0;
  max-width: 500px;
  color: #9db0cd;
  font-size: 14px;
  line-height: 1.65;
}

.hero-features {
  display: grid;
  gap: 16px;
  margin: 38px 0 0;
  padding: 0;
  list-style: none;
}

.hero-features li {
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  gap: 13px;
}

.feature-icon {
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 38px;
  height: 38px;
  border: 1px solid rgba(125, 211, 252, 0.18);
  border-radius: 12px;
  color: #7dd3fc;
  background: rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.09);
}

.hero-features strong {
  display: block;
  color: #e6edfa;

  font-weight: 700;
}

.hero-features span span {
  display: block;
  margin-top: 3px;
  color: #8194b3;
  font-size: 11.5px;
  line-height: 1.5;
}

.hero-foot {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 12px;
  padding-top: 22px;
  border-top: 1px solid rgba(148, 163, 184, 0.14);
  color: #7184a3;
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.hero-sep {
  width: 4px;
  height: 4px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: #334155;
}

/* ----------------------------------------------------------------- panel */

.login-panel {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  padding: 38px 30px;
  background:
    radial-gradient(circle at 100% 0%, rgba(36, 99, 235, 0.07), transparent 36%),
    var(--bg);
}

.login-card {
  width: min(430px, 100%);
  padding: 34px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 26px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.92);
  box-shadow: 0 28px 70px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.1);
  animation: fade-up 0.4s ease both;
}

.login-head h2 {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 27px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: -0.035em;
}

.login-head p:last-child {
  margin: 8px 0 0;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 12.5px;
  line-height: 1.55;
}

.login-form {
  display: grid;
  gap: 15px;
  margin-top: 26px;
}

.input-wrap {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  height: 46px;
  padding: 0 13px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line-strong);
  border-radius: 12px;
  background: #fff;
  color: #94a3b8;
  transition: 0.16s ease;
}

.input-wrap:focus-within {
  border-color: #93b4f8;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 4px rgba(36, 99, 235, 0.09);
  color: var(--brand);
}

.input-wrap .input {
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
  height: 44px;
  padding: 0;
  border: 0;
  background: transparent;
  outline: 0;
  box-shadow: none;
}

.input-action {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 28px;
  height: 28px;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 8px;
  color: var(--muted);
  background: transparent;
}

.input-action:hover {
  color: var(--ink);
  background: var(--surface-2);
}

.error-text {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
}

.login-submit {
  height: 46px;
  margin-top: 4px;
  border-radius: 12px;
}

.demo-block {
  margin-top: 26px;
  padding-top: 22px;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--line);
}

.demo-head {
  display: flex;
  align-items: baseline;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 10px;
  margin-bottom: 11px;
  font-size: 10.5px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.1em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--ink-soft);
}

.demo-hint {
  font-size: 9.5px;
  font-weight: 600;
  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
  text-transform: none;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.demo-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  gap: 8px;
}

.demo-card {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 9px;
  padding: 9px 10px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 11px;
  background: var(--surface-2);
  text-align: left;
  transition: 0.16s ease;
}

.demo-card:hover {
  border-color: #bed1f4;
  background: #fff;
  transform: translateY(-1px);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-sm);
}

.demo-icon {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 29px;
  height: 29px;
  border-radius: 9px;
  color: var(--brand);
  background: var(--brand-soft);
}

.demo-meta strong {
  display: block;
  font-size: 10.5px;
  color: var(--ink);
}

.demo-meta span {
  display: block;
  margin-top: 1px;
  font-size: 9.5px;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.login-note {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 7px;
  margin: 20px 0 0;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 10px;
  line-height: 1.5;
}

.login-note code {
  padding: 2px 5px;
  border-radius: 5px;
  background: var(--surface-2);
  color: var(--ink-soft);
  font-size: 9.5px;
}

@keyframes float-slow {
  0%,
  100% {
    transform: translate3d(0, 0, 0);
  }
  50% {
    transform: translate3d(-18px, 22px, 0);
  }
}

@media (max-width: 980px) {
  .login-page {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .login-hero {
    padding: 28px 28px 24px;
  }

  .hero-copy {
    padding: 44px 0 38px;
  }

  .hero-copy h1 {
    font-size: clamp(34px, 8vw, 48px);
  }

  .hero-features {
    display: none;
  }

  .login-panel {
    padding: 26px 18px 42px;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 560px) {
  .hero-badge,
  .hero-foot {
    display: none;
  }

  .login-card {
    padding: 26px 20px;
    border-radius: 20px;
  }
}

## 77. `frontend/src/app/pages/dashboard/dashboard.ts`

Строк: 183

``ts
import { Component, computed, inject, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { RouterLink } from '@angular/router';
import { forkJoin } from 'rxjs';
import { ApiService, errorMessage } from '../../core/api.service';
import { AuthService } from '../../core/auth.service';
import {
  CarView,
  Client,
  Master,
  OrderStatus,
  OrderSummary,
  Service,
  StatusLogEntry,
  dateTime,
  money,
  plural,
  pluralWord,
  statusClass,
} from '../../core/models';
import { ToastService } from '../../core/toast.service';
import { Icon } from '../../shared/icon';

const STATUS_COLORS: Record<string, string> = {
  'новая': '#2463eb',
  'в работе': '#f59e0b',
  'завершена': '#10b981',
  'отменена': '#ef4444',
};

@Component({
  selector: 'app-dashboard',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [RouterLink, Icon],
  templateUrl: './dashboard.html',
  styleUrl: './dashboard.css',
})
export class Dashboard {
  private readonly api = inject(ApiService);
  private readonly toast = inject(ToastService);
  readonly auth = inject(AuthService);

  readonly money = money;
  readonly plural = plural;
  readonly pluralWord = pluralWord;
  readonly statusClass = statusClass;
  readonly dateTime = dateTime;

  readonly loading = signal(true);
  readonly orders = signal<OrderSummary[]>([]);
  readonly clients = signal<Client[]>([]);
  readonly cars = signal<CarView[]>([]);
  readonly services = signal<Service[]>([]);
  readonly masters = signal<Master[]>([]);
  readonly statuses = signal<OrderStatus[]>([]);
  readonly log = signal<StatusLogEntry[]>([]);

  readonly greeting = computed(() => {
    const parts = (this.auth.user()?.fullName ?? '').split(' ').filter(Boolean);
    return parts[1] ?? parts[0] ?? 'коллега';
  });

  readonly totalOrders = computed(() => this.orders().length);

  readonly newOrders = computed(() => this.countByStatus('новая'));

  readonly inProgress = computed(() => this.countByStatus('в работе'));

  readonly completed = computed(() => this.countByStatus('завершена'));

  readonly revenue = computed(() =>
    this.orders().reduce((sum, order) => sum + Number(order.totalCost ?? 0), 0),
  );

  readonly avgCheck = computed(() => {
    const paid = this.orders().filter((order) => Number(order.totalCost) > 0);
    if (paid.length === 0) {
      return 0;
    }
    return paid.reduce((sum, order) => sum + Number(order.totalCost), 0) / paid.length;
  });

  readonly activeOrders = computed(() =>
    this.orders()
      .filter((order) => order.status !== 'завершена' && order.status !== 'отменена')
      .sort((a, b) => b.orderId - a.orderId)
      .slice(0, 7),
  );

  readonly completionRate = computed(() =>
    this.totalOrders() === 0 ? 0 : Math.round((this.completed() / this.totalOrders()) * 100),
  );

  readonly statusStats = computed(() =>
    this.statuses().map((status) => ({
      id: status.id,
      name: status.name,
      count: this.countByStatus(status.name),
      color: STATUS_COLORS[status.name] ?? '#94a3b8',
      className: statusClass(status.name),
    })),
  );

  readonly donutGradient = computed(() => {
    const total = this.totalOrders();
    if (total === 0) {
      return 'conic-gradient(#eef2f8 0 100%)';
    }
    let cursor = 0;
    const segments: string[] = [];
    for (const item of this.statusStats()) {
      if (item.count === 0) {
        continue;
      }
      const start = cursor;
      cursor += (item.count / total) * 100;
      segments.push(`${item.color} ${start}% ${cursor}%`);
    }
    return `conic-gradient(${segments.join(', ')})`;
  });

  readonly logPreview = computed(() =>
    this.log()
      .slice(0, 6)
      .map((entry) => ({
        ...entry,
        fromName: this.statusName(entry.oldStatusId) ?? '—',
        toName: this.statusName(entry.newStatusId) ?? '—',
      })),
  );

  readonly activeMasters = computed(() => this.masters().filter((master) => master.active).length);

  constructor() {
    this.load();
  }

  load(): void {
    this.loading.set(true);
    forkJoin({
      orders: this.api.orders(),
      clients: this.api.clients(),
      cars: this.api.cars(),
      services: this.api.services(),
      masters: this.api.masters(),
      statuses: this.api.statuses(),
      log: this.api.statusLog(),
    }).subscribe({
      next: (data) => {
        this.orders.set(data.orders);
        this.clients.set(data.clients);
        this.cars.set(data.cars);
        this.services.set(data.services);
        this.masters.set(data.masters);
        this.statuses.set(data.statuses);
        this.log.set(data.log);
        this.loading.set(false);
      },
      error: (error) => {
        this.loading.set(false);
        this.toast.error(errorMessage(error));
      },
    });
  }

  compactMoney(value: number): string {
    return (
      new Intl.NumberFormat('ru-RU', { notation: 'compact', maximumFractionDigits: 1 }).format(
        value ?? 0,
      ) + ' ₽'
    );
  }

  statusName(id: number | null): string | null {
    if (id === null) {
      return null;
    }
    return this.statuses().find((status) => status.id === id)?.name ?? null;
  }

  private countByStatus(status: string): number {
    return this.orders().filter((order) => order.status === status).length;
  }
}
````

## 78. `frontend/src/app/pages/dashboard/dashboard.html`

Строк: 288

``html
<div class="page">
  <div class="page-head">
    <div>
      <p class="eyebrow">Панель управления</p>
      <h1 class="page-title">Добрый день, {{ greeting() }}</h1>
      <p class="page-sub">
        Сводка по сервису на сегодня. Данные читаются напрямую из PostgreSQL через модуль доступа.
      </p>
    </div>
    <div class="page-actions">
      <button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost" (click)="load()" [disabled]="loading()">
        <app-icon name="history" [size]="16" />
        Обновить
      </button>
      <a class="btn btn-primary" routerLink="/orders" [queryParams]="{ new: 1 }">
        <app-icon name="plus" [size]="16" />
        Новая заявка
      </a>
    </div>
  </div>

  @if (loading()) {
    <div class="stat-grid">
      @for (item of [1, 2, 3, 4]; track item) {
        <div class="skeleton skeleton-card"></div>
      }
    </div>
  } @else {
    <div class="stat-grid">
      <article class="stat-card">
        <div class="stat-top">
          <span class="stat-icon"><app-icon name="orders" [size]="19" /></span>
          <span class="badge plain">всего</span>
        </div>
        <div class="stat-label">Заявки</div>
        <div class="stat-value">{{ totalOrders() }}</div>
        <div class="stat-foot">
          <span class="stat-delta"><app-icon name="trend" [size]="13" /> {{ inProgress() }}</span>
          сейчас в работе
        </div>
      </article>

      <article class="stat-card">
        <div class="stat-top">
          <span class="stat-icon orange"><app-icon name="clock" [size]="19" /></span>
          <span class="badge progress">активные</span>
        </div>
        <div class="stat-label">В работе</div>
        <div class="stat-value">{{ inProgress() + newOrders() }}</div>
        <div class="stat-foot">
          {{ plural(newOrders(), 'новая', 'новые', 'новых') }} ·
          {{ plural(inProgress(), 'в работе', 'в работе', 'в работе') }}
        </div>
      </article>

      <article class="stat-card">
        <div class="stat-top">
          <span class="stat-icon green"><app-icon name="check" [size]="19" /></span>
          <span class="badge done">{{ completionRate() }}%</span>
        </div>
        <div class="stat-label">Завершено</div>
        <div class="stat-value">{{ completed() }}</div>
        <div class="stat-foot">средний чек {{ money(avgCheck()) }}</div>
      </article>

      <article class="stat-card">
        <div class="stat-top">
          <span class="stat-icon teal"><app-icon name="ruble" [size]="19" /></span>
          <span class="badge plain">выручка</span>
        </div>
        <div class="stat-label">Стоимость работ</div>
        <div class="stat-value">{{ compactMoney(revenue()) }}</div>
        <div class="stat-foot">по всем заявкам в базе</div>
      </article>
    </div>
  }

  <div class="grid-2">
    <section class="card">
      <div class="card-head">
        <div>
          <h2 class="card-title">Активные заявки</h2>
          <p class="card-sub">Новые и находящиеся в работе</p>
        </div>
        <a class="card-link" routerLink="/orders">
          Все заявки
          <app-icon name="arrow" [size]="14" />
        </a>
      </div>

      <div class="card-body tight">
        @if (loading()) {
          <div class="skeleton skeleton-row"></div>
          <div class="skeleton skeleton-row"></div>
          <div class="skeleton skeleton-row"></div>
        } @else if (activeOrders().length === 0) {
          <div class="empty">
            <span class="empty-icon"><app-icon name="spark" [size]="22" /></span>
            <strong>Очередь пуста</strong>
            <span>Все заявки завершены — можно принимать новые.</span>
          </div>
        } @else {
          <div class="table-wrap">
            <table class="data-table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Заявка</th>
                  <th>Клиент</th>
                  <th>Автомобиль</th>
                  <th>Статус</th>
                  <th class="right">Стоимость</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                @for (order of activeOrders(); track order.orderId) {
                  <tr>
                    <td>
                      <a
                        class="order-link"
                        routerLink="/orders"
                        [queryParams]="{ order: order.orderId }"
                      >
                        #{{ order.orderId }}
                      </a>
                    </td>
                    <td class="strong">{{ order.client }}</td>
                    <td><span class="chip plate">{{ order.car }}</span></td>
                    <td>
                      <span class="badge" [class]="'badge ' + statusClass(order.status)">
                        {{ order.status }}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                    <td class="right strong">{{ money(order.totalCost) }}</td>
                  </tr>
                }
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        }
      </div>
    </section>

    <div class="stack">
      <section class="card">
        <div class="card-head">
          <div>
            <h2 class="card-title">Статусы заявок</h2>
            <p class="card-sub">Распределение по всей базе</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="card-body">
          <div class="donut-wrap">
            <div class="donut" [style.background]="donutGradient()">
              <div class="donut-center">
                <strong>{{ totalOrders() }}</strong>
                <span>{{ pluralWord(totalOrders(), 'заявка', 'заявки', 'заявок') }}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="legend">
              @for (item of statusStats(); track item.id) {
                <div class="legend-row">
                  <span class="legend-dot" [style.background]="item.color"></span>
                  <span>{{ item.name }}</span>
                  <strong>{{ item.count }}</strong>
                </div>
              }
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section class="card">
        <div class="card-head">
          <div>
            <h2 class="card-title">Данные в базе</h2>
            <p class="card-sub">Справочники, доступные сервису</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="card-body tight">
          <div class="data-row">
            <span class="data-icon blue"><app-icon name="clients" [size]="17" /></span>
            <span class="data-label">Клиенты</span>
            <strong>{{ clients().length }}</strong>
          </div>
          <div class="data-row">
            <span class="data-icon teal"><app-icon name="car" [size]="17" /></span>
            <span class="data-label">Автомобили</span>
            <strong>{{ cars().length }}</strong>
          </div>
          <div class="data-row">
            <span class="data-icon orange"><app-icon name="services" [size]="17" /></span>
            <span class="data-label">Услуги</span>
            <strong>{{ services().length }}</strong>
          </div>
          <div class="data-row">
            <span class="data-icon violet"><app-icon name="masters" [size]="17" /></span>
            <span class="data-label">Мастера</span>
            <strong>{{ activeMasters() }} / {{ masters().length }}</strong>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="grid-2 dashboard-bottom">
    <section class="card">
      <div class="card-head">
        <div>
          <h2 class="card-title">Последние события</h2>
          <p class="card-sub">Журнал изменений статусов от триггера БД</p>
        </div>
        <a class="card-link" routerLink="/log">
          Весь журнал
          <app-icon name="arrow" [size]="14" />
        </a>
      </div>
      <div class="card-body">
        @if (loading()) {
          <div class="skeleton skeleton-row"></div>
          <div class="skeleton skeleton-row"></div>
        } @else if (logPreview().length === 0) {
          <div class="empty">
            <span class="empty-icon"><app-icon name="history" [size]="22" /></span>
            <strong>Событий пока нет</strong>
            <span>Журнал заполнится после изменения статуса заявки.</span>
          </div>
        } @else {
          <div class="timeline">
            @for (entry of logPreview(); track entry.id) {
              <div class="timeline-item">
                <span class="timeline-dot"><app-icon name="history" [size]="14" /></span>
                <div class="timeline-body">
                  <strong>
                    Заявка #{{ entry.orderId }}: {{ entry.fromName }} → {{ entry.toName }}
                  </strong>
                  <span>{{ dateTime(entry.changedAt) }} · {{ entry.changedBy }}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            }
          </div>
        }
      </div>
    </section>

    <section class="card finance-card">
      <div class="card-head">
        <div>
          <h2 class="card-title">Загрузка сервиса</h2>
          <p class="card-sub">Доля завершённых заявок</p>
        </div>
        <span class="badge done">{{ completionRate() }}%</span>
      </div>
      <div class="card-body">
        <div class="progress-row">
          <span>Завершено</span>
          <strong>{{ completed() }} из {{ totalOrders() }}</strong>
        </div>
        <div class="progress-track">
          <span class="progress-fill" [style.width.%]="completionRate()"></span>
        </div>

        <div class="finance-stats">
          <div>
            <span>Выручка</span>
            <strong>{{ money(revenue()) }}</strong>
          </div>
          <div>
            <span>Средний чек</span>
            <strong>{{ money(avgCheck()) }}</strong>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="quick-actions">
          <a class="quick" routerLink="/clients">
            <span class="quick-icon"><app-icon name="clients" [size]="16" /></span>
            Добавить клиента
            <app-icon name="arrow" [size]="14" />
          </a>
          <a class="quick" routerLink="/services">
            <span class="quick-icon"><app-icon name="services" [size]="16" /></span>
            Прайс-лист услуг
            <app-icon name="arrow" [size]="14" />
          </a>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  </div>
</div>

``css
.card-body.tight {
  padding: 12px 10px 10px;
}

.card-body.tight .data-row:first-child {
  border-top: 0;
}

.order-link {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  padding: 3px 7px;
  border-radius: 7px;
  background: var(--brand-soft);
  color: var(--brand-dark);
  font-size: 11.5px;
  font-weight: 800;
  transition: 0.16s ease;
}

.order-link:hover {
  background: #d9e6ff;
  transform: translateY(-1px);
}

.data-row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 11px;
  padding: 11px 14px;
  border-top: 1px solid #eef2f8;
}

.data-icon {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 32px;
  height: 32px;
  border-radius: 10px;
}

.data-icon.blue {
  color: var(--brand);
  background: var(--brand-soft);
}

.data-icon.teal {
  color: #0f8f8f;
  background: var(--teal-soft);
}

.data-icon.orange {
  color: #c2410c;
  background: var(--accent-soft);
}

.data-icon.violet {
  color: #6d28d9;
  background: var(--violet-soft);
}

.data-label {
  flex: 1;
  color: var(--ink-soft);
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.data-row strong {
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 800;
}

.dashboard-bottom {
  margin-top: 20px;
}

.progress-row {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  margin-bottom: 8px;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 11.5px;
}

.progress-row strong {
  color: var(--ink);
  font-size: 11.5px;
}

.progress-track {
  height: 9px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: #eef2f8;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.progress-fill {
  display: block;
  height: 100%;
  border-radius: inherit;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #10b981, #22d3ee);
  transition: width 0.5s ease;
}

.finance-stats {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  gap: 12px;
  margin-top: 18px;
}

.finance-stats div {
  padding: 13px 14px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 13px;
  background: var(--surface-2);
}

.finance-stats span {
  display: block;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 9.5px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.1em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.finance-stats strong {
  display: block;
  margin-top: 5px;
  font-size: 15px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: -0.02em;
}

.quick-actions {
  display: grid;
  gap: 8px;
  margin-top: 18px;
}

.quick {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 10px 12px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 12px;
  color: var(--ink-soft);
  font-size: 11.5px;
  font-weight: 700;
  transition: 0.16s ease;
}

.quick:hover {
  border-color: #bed1f4;
  background: #fbfdff;
  color: var(--brand-dark);
  transform: translateX(2px);
}

.quick > app-icon:last-child {
  margin-left: auto;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.quick-icon {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 29px;
  height: 29px;
  border-radius: 9px;
  color: var(--brand);
  background: var(--brand-soft);
}

@media (max-width: 680px) {
  .finance-stats {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
}
