## 80. `frontend/src/app/pages/orders/orders.ts`

Строк: 388

``ts
import { Component, HostListener, inject, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { ActivatedRoute, Router } from '@angular/router';
import { forkJoin } from 'rxjs';
import { ApiService, errorMessage } from '../../core/api.service';
import {
  CarView,
  Client,
  Master,
  OrderDetail,
  OrderStatus,
  OrderSummary,
  Service,
  StatusLogEntry,
  dateTime,
  money,
  plural,
  pluralWord,
  statusClass,
} from '../../core/models';
import { ToastService } from '../../core/toast.service';
import { Icon } from '../../shared/icon';

type OrderFilter = 'all' | 'новая' | 'в работе' | 'завершена' | 'отменена';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-orders',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [FormsModule, Icon],
  templateUrl: './orders.html',
  styleUrl: './orders.css',
})
export class Orders {
  private readonly api = inject(ApiService);
  private readonly toast = inject(ToastService);
  private readonly route = inject(ActivatedRoute);
  private readonly router = inject(Router);

  readonly money = money;
  readonly plural = plural;
  readonly pluralWord = pluralWord;
  readonly statusClass = statusClass;
  readonly dateTime = dateTime;

  readonly loading = signal(true);
  readonly orders = signal<OrderSummary[]>([]);
  readonly clients = signal<Client[]>([]);
  readonly cars = signal<CarView[]>([]);
  readonly services = signal<Service[]>([]);
  readonly masters = signal<Master[]>([]);
  readonly statuses = signal<OrderStatus[]>([]);

  readonly filter = signal<OrderFilter>('all');
  readonly search = signal('');

  readonly createOpen = signal(false);
  readonly createError = signal('');
  readonly creating = signal(false);

  readonly detailOpen = signal(false);
  readonly detailLoading = signal(false);
  readonly detail = signal<OrderDetail | null>(null);
  readonly detailLog = signal<StatusLogEntry[]>([]);
  readonly addServiceError = signal('');
  readonly addingService = signal(false);
  readonly confirmClose = signal(false);
  readonly closing = signal(false);

  newOrder = {
    clientId: null as number | null,
    carId: null as number | null,
    masterId: null as number | null,
    description: '',
  };

  newService = {
    serviceId: null as number | null,
    quantity: 1,
  };

  private pendingClientId: number | null = null;

  readonly tabs = [
    { key: 'all' as OrderFilter, label: 'Все' },
    { key: 'новая' as OrderFilter, label: 'Новые' },
    { key: 'в работе' as OrderFilter, label: 'В работе' },
    { key: 'завершена' as OrderFilter, label: 'Завершённые' },
    { key: 'отменена' as OrderFilter, label: 'Отменённые' },
  ];

  constructor() {
    this.load();
    this.route.queryParamMap.subscribe((params) => {
      const query = params.get('q');
      if (query) {
        this.search.set(query);
      }
      if (params.get('new') === '1' && !this.createOpen()) {
        this.openCreate();
        const clientId = Number(params.get('clientId'));
        if (Number.isFinite(clientId) && clientId > 0) {
          this.pendingClientId = clientId;
          this.applyPendingClient();
        }
      }
      const orderId = Number(params.get('order'));
      if (orderId && (!this.detail() || this.detail()?.order.id !== orderId)) {
        this.openDetail(orderId);
      }
    });
  }

  @HostListener('document:keydown.escape')
  onEscape(): void {
    if (this.createOpen()) {
      this.closeCreate();
    } else if (this.detailOpen()) {
      this.closeDetail();
    }
  }

  load(): void {
    this.loading.set(true);
    forkJoin({
      orders: this.api.orders(),
      clients: this.api.clients(),
      cars: this.api.cars(),
      services: this.api.services(),
      masters: this.api.masters(),
      statuses: this.api.statuses(),
    }).subscribe({
      next: (data) => {
        this.orders.set(data.orders);
        this.clients.set(data.clients);
        this.cars.set(data.cars);
        this.services.set(data.services);
        this.masters.set(data.masters);
        this.statuses.set(data.statuses);
        this.loading.set(false);
        this.applyPendingClient();
      },
      error: (error) => {
        this.loading.set(false);
        this.toast.error(errorMessage(error));
      },
    });
  }

  countFor(filter: OrderFilter): number {
    return filter === 'all'
      ? this.orders().length
      : this.orders().filter((order) => order.status === filter).length;
  }

  filteredOrders(): OrderSummary[] {
    const query = this.search().trim().toLowerCase();
    const filter = this.filter();
    return this.orders().filter((order) => {
      if (filter !== 'all' && order.status !== filter) {
        return false;
      }
      if (!query) {
        return true;
      }
      return (
        String(order.orderId).includes(query) ||
        order.client.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        order.car.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        order.status.toLowerCase().includes(query)
      );
    });
  }

  setFilter(filter: OrderFilter): void {
    this.filter.set(filter);
  }

  openCreate(): void {
    this.newOrder = { clientId: null, carId: null, masterId: null, description: '' };
    this.createError.set('');
    this.detailOpen.set(false);
    this.createOpen.set(true);
  }

  closeCreate(): void {
    if (this.creating()) {
      return;
    }
    this.createOpen.set(false);
    if (this.route.snapshot.queryParamMap.has('new')) {
      void this.router.navigate(['/orders'], { replaceUrl: true });
    }
  }

  carsForSelected(): CarView[] {
    if (!this.newOrder.clientId) {
      return [];
    }
    return this.cars().filter((car) => car.clientId === this.newOrder.clientId);
  }

  selectedClient(): Client | undefined {
    return this.clients().find((client) => client.id === this.newOrder.clientId);
  }

  onClientChange(clientId: number | null): void {
    this.newOrder.clientId = clientId;
    this.newOrder.carId = null;
    const cars = this.carsForSelected();
    if (cars.length === 1) {
      this.newOrder.carId = cars[0].id;
    }
    this.createError.set('');
  }

  private applyPendingClient(): void {
    if (this.pendingClientId !== null && this.clients().length > 0) {
      this.onClientChange(this.pendingClientId);
      this.pendingClientId = null;
    }
  }

  canCreate(): boolean {
    return (
      !this.creating() &&
      this.newOrder.clientId !== null &&
      this.newOrder.carId !== null &&
      this.newOrder.description.trim().length > 0
    );
  }

  create(): void {
    if (!this.canCreate()) {
      this.createError.set('Выберите клиента, автомобиль и опишите неисправность');
      return;
    }
    this.creating.set(true);
    this.createError.set('');
    this.api
      .createOrder({
        clientId: this.newOrder.clientId!,
        carId: this.newOrder.carId!,
        masterId: this.newOrder.masterId,
        description: this.newOrder.description.trim(),
      })
      .subscribe({
        next: (result) => {
          this.creating.set(false);
          this.createOpen.set(false);
          this.toast.success(`Заявка #${result.orderId} создана`);
          this.load();
          this.openDetail(result.orderId);
          if (this.route.snapshot.queryParamMap.has('new')) {
            void this.router.navigate(['/orders'], { replaceUrl: true });
          }
        },
        error: (error) => {
          this.creating.set(false);
          this.createError.set(errorMessage(error));
        },
      });
  }

  openDetail(orderId: number): void {
    this.createOpen.set(false);
    this.detailOpen.set(true);
    this.detailLoading.set(true);
    this.detail.set(null);
    this.detailLog.set([]);
    this.addServiceError.set('');
    this.confirmClose.set(false);
    this.newService = { serviceId: null, quantity: 1 };

    this.api.order(orderId).subscribe({
      next: (detail) => {
        this.detail.set(detail);
        this.detailLoading.set(false);
      },
      error: (error) => {
        this.detailLoading.set(false);
        this.toast.error(errorMessage(error));
        this.closeDetail();
      },
    });
    this.api.statusLog(orderId).subscribe({
      next: (entries) => this.detailLog.set(entries),
      error: () => this.detailLog.set([]),
    });
  }

  refreshDetail(orderId: number): void {
    this.api.order(orderId).subscribe({
      next: (detail) => this.detail.set(detail),
      error: (error) => this.toast.error(errorMessage(error)),
    });
    this.api.statusLog(orderId).subscribe({
      next: (entries) => this.detailLog.set(entries),
      error: () => this.detailLog.set([]),
    });
  }

  closeDetail(): void {
    if (this.closing()) {
      return;
    }
    this.detailOpen.set(false);
    this.confirmClose.set(false);
    if (this.route.snapshot.queryParamMap.has('order')) {
      void this.router.navigate(['/orders'], { replaceUrl: true });
    }
  }

  statusName(id: number | null): string {
    if (id === null) {
      return '—';
    }
    return this.statuses().find((status) => status.id === id)?.name ?? `статус ${id}`;
  }

  isFinished(status: string): boolean {
    return status === 'завершена' || status === 'отменена';
  }

  addService(): void {
    const detail = this.detail();
    if (!detail) {
      return;
    }
    if (!this.newService.serviceId) {
      this.addServiceError.set('Выберите услугу');
      return;
    }
    this.addingService.set(true);
    this.addServiceError.set('');
    this.api
      .addService(
        detail.order.id,
        this.newService.serviceId,
        Number(this.newService.quantity) || 1,
      )
      .subscribe({
        next: () => {
          this.addingService.set(false);
          this.newService = { serviceId: null, quantity: 1 };
          this.toast.success('Услуга добавлена в заявку');
          this.refreshDetail(detail.order.id);
          this.load();
        },
        error: (error) => {
          this.addingService.set(false);
          this.addServiceError.set(errorMessage(error));
        },
      });
  }

  askClose(): void {
    this.confirmClose.set(true);
  }

  cancelClose(): void {
    this.confirmClose.set(false);
  }

  doClose(): void {
    const detail = this.detail();
    if (!detail || this.closing()) {
      return;
    }
    this.closing.set(true);
    this.api.closeOrder(detail.order.id).subscribe({
      next: () => {
        this.closing.set(false);
        this.confirmClose.set(false);
        this.toast.success(`Заявка #${detail.order.id} завершена`);
        this.refreshDetail(detail.order.id);
        this.load();
      },
      error: (error) => {
        this.closing.set(false);
        this.toast.error(errorMessage(error));
      },
    });
  }

  serviceName(id: number | null): Service | undefined {
    return this.services().find((service) => service.id === Number(id));
  }
}
````

## 81. `frontend/src/app/pages/orders/orders.html`

Строк: 485

``html
<div class="page">
  <div class="page-head">
    <div>
      <p class="eyebrow">Рабочий процесс</p>
      <h1 class="page-title">Заявки сервиса</h1>
      <p class="page-sub">
        Полный цикл: приём автомобиля, добавление услуг и закрытие заявки с записью в журнал.
      </p>
    </div>
    <div class="page-actions">
      <button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost" (click)="load()" [disabled]="loading()">
        <app-icon name="history" [size]="16" />
        Обновить
      </button>
      <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary" (click)="openCreate()">
        <app-icon name="plus" [size]="16" />
        Новая заявка
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="toolbar card">
    <div class="tabs">
      @for (tab of tabs; track tab.key) {
        <button
          type="button"
          class="tab"
          [class.active]="filter() === tab.key"
          (click)="setFilter(tab.key)"
        >
          {{ tab.label }}
          <span class="tab-count">{{ countFor(tab.key) }}</span>
        </button>
      }
    </div>

    <div class="search orders-search">
      <app-icon name="search" [size]="16" />
      <input
        type="search"
        placeholder="Номер, клиент, автомобиль…"
        [value]="search()"
        (input)="search.set($any($event.target).value)"
        aria-label="Поиск по заявкам"
      />
      @if (search()) {
        <button type="button" class="search-clear" (click)="search.set('')" aria-label="Очистить">
          <app-icon name="close" [size]="13" />
        </button>
      }
    </div>
  </div>

  <section class="card table-card">
    <div class="card-body">
      @if (loading()) {
        <div class="skeleton skeleton-row"></div>
        <div class="skeleton skeleton-row"></div>
        <div class="skeleton skeleton-row"></div>
        <div class="skeleton skeleton-row"></div>
      } @else if (filteredOrders().length === 0) {
        <div class="empty">
          <span class="empty-icon"><app-icon name="orders" [size]="22" /></span>
          <strong>Заявки не найдены</strong>
          <span>Измените фильтр или создайте новую заявку.</span>
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" (click)="openCreate()">
            <app-icon name="plus" [size]="14" />
            Создать заявку
          </button>
        </div>
      } @else {
        <div class="table-wrap">
          <table class="data-table">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>Заявка</th>
                <th>Клиент</th>
                <th>Автомобиль</th>
                <th>Статус</th>
                <th class="right">Стоимость</th>
                <th class="right">Действие</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              @for (order of filteredOrders(); track order.orderId) {
                <tr class="row-link" (click)="openDetail(order.orderId)">
                  <td>
                    <span class="order-link">#{{ order.orderId }}</span>
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <span class="strong">{{ order.client }}</span>
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <span class="chip plate">{{ order.car }}</span>
                  </td>
                  <td>
                    <span class="badge" [class]="'badge ' + statusClass(order.status)">
                      {{ order.status }}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                  <td class="right strong">{{ money(order.totalCost) }}</td>
                  <td class="right">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm"
                      (click)="$event.stopPropagation(); openDetail(order.orderId)"
                    >
                      <app-icon name="eye" [size]="14" />
                      Открыть
                    </button>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              }
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
        <div class="table-foot">
          Показано {{ filteredOrders().length }} из {{ orders().length }}
          {{ pluralWord(orders().length, 'заявки', 'заявок', 'заявок') }}
        </div>
      }
    </div>
  </section>
</div>

<!-- ------------------------------------------------------- создание заявки -->

@if (createOpen()) {
  <div class="modal-backdrop" (click)="closeCreate()">
    <div class="modal" (click)="$event.stopPropagation()">
      <div class="modal-head">
        <div>
          <h2>Новая заявка</h2>
          <p>Клиент, автомобиль и описание неисправности</p>
        </div>
        <button type="button" class="modal-close" (click)="closeCreate()" aria-label="Закрыть">
          <app-icon name="close" [size]="17" />
        </button>
      </div>

      <form (submit)="$event.preventDefault(); create()">
        <div class="modal-body">
          <div class="form-grid">
            <label class="field">
              <span class="label">Клиент *</span>
              <select
                class="select"
                name="clientId"
                [ngModel]="newOrder.clientId"
                (ngModelChange)="onClientChange($event)"
              >
                <option [ngValue]="null" disabled>Выберите клиента</option>
                @for (client of clients(); track client.id) {
                  <option [ngValue]="client.id">{{ client.fullName }} · {{ client.phone }}</option>
                }
              </select>
              @if (selectedClient(); as client) {
                <span class="hint">{{ client.email || 'E-mail не указан' }}</span>
              }
            </label>

            <label class="field">
              <span class="label">Автомобиль *</span>
              <select
                class="select"
                name="carId"
                [(ngModel)]="newOrder.carId"
                [disabled]="!newOrder.clientId"
              >
                <option [ngValue]="null" disabled>
                  {{ newOrder.clientId ? 'Выберите автомобиль' : 'Сначала выберите клиента' }}
                </option>
                @for (car of carsForSelected(); track car.id) {
                  <option [ngValue]="car.id">
                    {{ car.brand }} {{ car.model }} · {{ car.plateNum }}
                  </option>
                }
              </select>
              @if (newOrder.clientId && carsForSelected().length === 0) {
                <span class="hint hint-warn">
                  У клиента нет автомобилей. Добавьте его в разделе «Автомобили».
                </span>
              }
            </label>

            <label class="field span-2">
              <span class="label">Мастер</span>
              <select class="select" name="masterId" [(ngModel)]="newOrder.masterId">
                <option [ngValue]="null">Не назначать</option>
                @for (master of masters(); track master.id) {
                  <option [ngValue]="master.id" [disabled]="!master.active">
                    {{ master.fullName }}{{ master.specialization ? ' · ' + master.specialization : '' }}
                    {{ master.active ? '' : ' (неактивен)' }}
                  </option>
                }
              </select>
              <span class="hint">Мастера можно назначить позже — при изменении статуса заявки.</span>
            </label>

            <label class="field span-2">
              <span class="label">Описание неисправности *</span>
              <textarea
                class="textarea"
                name="description"
                maxlength="2000"
                placeholder="Например: стук в передней подвеске при проезде неровностей"
                [(ngModel)]="newOrder.description"
              ></textarea>
              <span class="hint">{{ newOrder.description.length }} / 2000 символов</span>
            </label>
          </div>

          @if (createError()) {
            <div class="error-text form-error">
              <app-icon name="alert" [size]="15" />
              {{ createError() }}
            </div>
          }
        </div>

        <div class="modal-foot">
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost" (click)="closeCreate()" [disabled]="creating()">
            Отмена
          </button>
          <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary" [disabled]="!canCreate()">
            @if (creating()) {
              <span class="spinner"></span>
              Создаём…
            } @else {
              <app-icon name="check" [size]="16" />
              Создать заявку
            }
          </button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  </div>
}

<!-- ------------------------------------------------------- карточка заявки -->

@if (detailOpen()) {
  <div class="modal-backdrop" (click)="closeDetail()">
    <div class="modal wide" (click)="$event.stopPropagation()">
      <div class="modal-head">
        <div>
          @if (detail(); as d) {
            <div class="ticket-title">
              <h2>Заявка #{{ d.order.id }}</h2>
              <span class="badge" [class]="'badge ' + statusClass(d.order.statusName)">
                {{ d.order.statusName }}
              </span>
            </div>
            <p>{{ d.client.fullName }} · {{ d.car.brand }} {{ d.car.model }} · {{ d.car.plateNum }}</p>
          } @else {
            <h2>Заявка</h2>
            <p>Загрузка данных…</p>
          }
        </div>
        <button type="button" class="modal-close" (click)="closeDetail()" aria-label="Закрыть">
          <app-icon name="close" [size]="17" />
        </button>
      </div>

      <div class="modal-body">
        @if (detailLoading()) {
          <div class="loading-line">
            <span class="spinner dark"></span>
            Читаем заявку из базы…
          </div>
        } @else if (detail(); as d) {
          <div class="ticket-hero">
            <div class="ticket-meta">
              <span class="ticket-icon blue"><app-icon name="clients" [size]="17" /></span>
              <span>
                <span class="ticket-label">Клиент</span>
                <strong>{{ d.client.fullName }}</strong>
                <span class="ticket-sub">{{ d.client.phone }}</span>
              </span>
            </div>
            <div class="ticket-meta">
              <span class="ticket-icon teal"><app-icon name="car" [size]="17" /></span>
              <span>
                <span class="ticket-label">Автомобиль</span>
                <strong>{{ d.car.brand }} {{ d.car.model }}</strong>
                <span class="ticket-sub">{{ d.car.plateNum }} · {{ d.car.year || 'год не указан' }}</span>
              </span>
            </div>
            <div class="ticket-meta">
              <span class="ticket-icon violet"><app-icon name="masters" [size]="17" /></span>
              <span>
                <span class="ticket-label">Мастер</span>
                <strong>{{ d.masterName || 'Не назначен' }}</strong>
                <span class="ticket-sub">{{ d.order.statusName }}</span>
              </span>
            </div>
            <div class="ticket-total">
              <span class="ticket-label">Итого по заявке</span>
              <strong>{{ money(d.total) }}</strong>
              <span class="ticket-sub">{{ plural(d.services.length, 'услуга', 'услуги', 'услуг') }}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="detail-grid">
            <div class="detail-item">
              <span>Создана</span>
              <strong>{{ dateTime(d.order.createdAt) }}</strong>
            </div>
            <div class="detail-item">
              <span>Закрыта</span>
              <strong>{{ dateTime(d.order.closedAt) }}</strong>
            </div>
            <div class="detail-item">
              <span>VIN</span>
              <strong class="mono">{{ d.car.vin || '—' }}</strong>
            </div>
            <div class="detail-item">
              <span>Телефон клиента</span>
              <strong>{{ d.client.phone }}</strong>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="description-box">
            <span>Описание неисправности</span>
            <p>{{ d.order.description }}</p>
          </div>

          <div class="section-head">
            <h3>Оказанные услуги</h3>
            <span class="badge plain">{{ d.services.length }}</span>
          </div>

          @if (d.services.length === 0) {
            <div class="empty small">
              <span class="empty-icon"><app-icon name="services" [size]="19" /></span>
              <span>В заявку пока не добавлено ни одной услуги.</span>
            </div>
          } @else {
            <div class="table-wrap service-table">
              <table class="data-table">
                <thead>
                  <tr>
                    <th>Услуга</th>
                    <th class="right">Цена</th>
                    <th class="right">Кол-во</th>
                    <th class="right">Сумма</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  @for (item of d.services; track item.id) {
                    <tr>
                      <td class="strong">{{ item.serviceName }}</td>
                      <td class="right">{{ money(item.priceAt) }}</td>
                      <td class="right">{{ item.quantity }}</td>
                      <td class="right strong">{{ money(item.lineTotal) }}</td>
                    </tr>
                  }
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          }

          @if (!isFinished(d.order.statusName)) {
            <div class="add-service">
              <div class="field">
                <span class="label">Добавить услугу</span>
                <select class="select" name="serviceId" [(ngModel)]="newService.serviceId">
                  <option [ngValue]="null" disabled>Выберите услугу</option>
                  @for (service of services(); track service.id) {
                    <option [ngValue]="service.id">
                      {{ service.name }} — {{ money(service.price) }}
                      {{ service.durationMin ? ' · ' + service.durationMin + ' мин' : '' }}
                    </option>
                  }
                </select>
              </div>
              <div class="field qty-field">
                <span class="label">Кол-во</span>
                <input
                  class="input"
                  type="number"
                  min="1"
                  max="100"
                  name="quantity"
                  [(ngModel)]="newService.quantity"
                />
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                class="btn btn-primary add-service-btn"
                (click)="addService()"
                [disabled]="addingService()"
              >
                @if (addingService()) {
                  <span class="spinner"></span>
                } @else {
                  <app-icon name="plus" [size]="15" />
                }
                Добавить
              </button>
            </div>
            @if (addServiceError()) {
              <div class="error-text form-error">
                <app-icon name="alert" [size]="15" />
                {{ addServiceError() }}
              </div>
            }
          }

          <div class="section-head">
            <h3>История статусов</h3>
            <span class="badge plain">{{ detailLog().length }}</span>
          </div>

          @if (detailLog().length === 0) {
            <p class="muted small-text">Статус ещё не менялся — журнал пуст.</p>
          } @else {
            <div class="timeline">
              @for (entry of detailLog(); track entry.id) {
                <div class="timeline-item">
                  <span class="timeline-dot"><app-icon name="history" [size]="14" /></span>
                  <div class="timeline-body">
                    <strong>
                      {{ statusName(entry.oldStatusId) }} → {{ statusName(entry.newStatusId) }}
                    </strong>
                    <span>{{ dateTime(entry.changedAt) }} · {{ entry.changedBy }}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              }
            </div>
          }

          @if (confirmClose()) {
            <div class="confirm-box">
              <span class="confirm-icon"><app-icon name="alert" [size]="19" /></span>
              <div class="confirm-text">
                <strong>Завершить заявку #{{ d.order.id }}?</strong>
                <span>
                  Статус изменится на «завершена», установится время закрытия, а триггер запишет
                  событие в журнал.
                </span>
              </div>
              <div class="confirm-actions">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm"
                  (click)="cancelClose()"
                  [disabled]="closing()"
                >
                  Отмена
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  class="btn btn-danger btn-sm"
                  (click)="doClose()"
                  [disabled]="closing()"
                >
                  @if (closing()) {
                    <span class="spinner dark"></span>
                  }
                  Да, завершить
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          }
        }
      </div>

      @if (detail(); as d) {
        <div class="modal-foot detail-foot">
          <span class="foot-hint">Стоимость рассчитана функцией calculate_order_total.</span>
          <div class="page-actions">
            @if (!isFinished(d.order.statusName)) {
              <button type="button" class="btn btn-danger" (click)="askClose()">
                <app-icon name="check" [size]="16" />
                Завершить заявку
              </button>
            }
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost" (click)="closeDetail()">Закрыть</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      }
    </div>
  </div>
}
````

.toolbar {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 16px;
  padding: 10px 12px;
  margin-bottom: 18px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
}

.orders-search {
  width: 300px;
  background: var(--surface-2);
}

.search-clear {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 22px;
  height: 22px;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 6px;
  color: var(--muted);
  background: transparent;
}

.search-clear:hover {
  color: var(--ink);
  background: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.06);
}

.table-card .card-body {
  padding: 8px 10px 10px;
}

.table-foot {
  padding: 12px 14px 4px;
  border-top: 1px solid #eef2f8;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 10.5px;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.order-link {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  padding: 3px 8px;
  border-radius: 7px;
  background: var(--brand-soft);
  color: var(--brand-dark);
  font-size: 11.5px;
  font-weight: 800;
}

.row-link {
  cursor: pointer;
}

.hint-warn {
  color: #b45309;
}

.form-error {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
  margin-top: 14px;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------- ticket */

.ticket-title {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
}

.ticket-hero {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1.15fr 1.15fr 1fr auto;
  gap: 18px;
  padding: 16px 18px;
  margin-bottom: 16px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 16px;
  background:
    radial-gradient(circle at 0% 0%, rgba(36, 99, 235, 0.06), transparent 50%),
    var(--surface-2);
}

.ticket-meta {
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  gap: 10px;
  min-width: 0;
}

.ticket-icon {
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 34px;
  height: 34px;
  border-radius: 10px;
}

.ticket-icon.blue {
  color: var(--brand);
  background: var(--brand-soft);
}

.ticket-icon.teal {
  color: #0f8f8f;
  background: var(--teal-soft);
}

.ticket-icon.violet {
  color: #6d28d9;
  background: var(--violet-soft);
}

.ticket-label {
  display: block;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 9px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.11em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.ticket-meta strong,
.ticket-total strong {
  display: block;
  margin-top: 3px;
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
  white-space: nowrap;
  font-size: 12px;
  font-weight: 700;
}

.ticket-sub {
  display: block;
  margin-top: 2px;
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
  white-space: nowrap;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 10px;
}

.ticket-total {
  padding-left: 18px;
  border-left: 1px solid var(--line);
  text-align: right;
}

.ticket-total strong {
  font-size: 18px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: -0.03em;
  color: var(--brand-dark);
}

.description-box {
  margin-top: 14px;
  padding: 13px 15px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-left: 3px solid var(--brand);
  border-radius: 12px;
  background: #fbfcff;
}

.description-box span {
  display: block;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 9px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.11em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.description-box p {
  margin: 6px 0 0;
  color: var(--ink-soft);
  font-size: 12.5px;
  line-height: 1.55;
}

.section-head {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 9px;
  margin: 22px 0 10px;
}

.section-head h3 {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
}

.service-table {
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 13px;
}

.empty.small {
  padding: 22px 16px;
  gap: 6px;
  border: 1px dashed var(--line-strong);
  border-radius: 13px;
}

.empty.small .empty-icon {
  width: 38px;
  height: 38px;
  border-radius: 11px;
}

.small-text {
  font-size: 11.5px;
}

.add-service {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) 96px auto;
  align-items: end;
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 13px;
  margin-top: 14px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 14px;
  background: var(--surface-2);
}

.add-service-btn {
  height: 42px;
}

.confirm-box {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 13px;
  padding: 14px;
  margin-top: 18px;
  border: 1px solid #f7c9c6;
  border-radius: 14px;
  background: #fff7f6;
}

.confirm-icon {
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 38px;
  height: 38px;
  border-radius: 11px;
  color: #b91c1c;
  background: var(--danger-soft);
}

.confirm-text {
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
}

.confirm-text strong {
  display: block;
  font-size: 12px;
  color: #991b1b;
}

.confirm-text span {
  display: block;
  margin-top: 3px;
  color: #b45b56;
  font-size: 10.5px;
  line-height: 1.45;
}

.confirm-actions {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
}

.detail-foot {
  justify-content: space-between;
}

.foot-hint {
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 10px;
}

@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .ticket-hero {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }

  .ticket-total {
    padding-left: 0;
    border-left: 0;
    text-align: left;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 720px) {
  .toolbar {
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: stretch;
  }

  .orders-search {
    width: 100%;
  }

  .tabs {
    overflow-x: auto;
  }

  .add-service {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .confirm-box {
    flex-wrap: wrap;
  }

  .confirm-actions {
    width: 100%;
    justify-content: flex-end;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 560px) {
  .ticket-hero {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
}

## 83. `frontend/src/app/pages/clients/clients.ts`

Строк: 210

``ts
import { Component, HostListener, computed, inject, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { ActivatedRoute, Router, RouterLink } from '@angular/router';
import { forkJoin } from 'rxjs';
import { ApiService, errorMessage } from '../../core/api.service';
import {
  CarView,
  Client,
  ClientDetail,
  initials,
  money,
  plural,
  pluralWord,
  shortDate,
  statusClass,
} from '../../core/models';
import { ToastService } from '../../core/toast.service';
import { Icon } from '../../shared/icon';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-clients',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [FormsModule, RouterLink, Icon],
  templateUrl: './clients.html',
  styleUrl: './clients.css',
})
export class Clients {
  private readonly api = inject(ApiService);
  private readonly toast = inject(ToastService);
  private readonly route = inject(ActivatedRoute);
  private readonly router = inject(Router);

  readonly initials = initials;
  readonly money = money;
  readonly plural = plural;
  readonly pluralWord = pluralWord;
  readonly shortDate = shortDate;
  readonly statusClass = statusClass;

  readonly loading = signal(true);
  readonly clients = signal<Client[]>([]);
  readonly cars = signal<CarView[]>([]);
  readonly search = signal('');

  readonly createOpen = signal(false);
  readonly createError = signal('');
  readonly creating = signal(false);

  readonly detailOpen = signal(false);
  readonly detailLoading = signal(false);
  readonly detail = signal<ClientDetail | null>(null);

  newClient = { fullName: '', phone: '', email: '' };

  readonly filtered = computed(() => {
    const query = this.search().trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!query) {
      return this.clients();
    }
    return this.clients().filter(
      (client) =>
        client.fullName.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        client.phone.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        (client.email ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(query),
    );
  });

  readonly withEmail = computed(() => this.clients().filter((client) => !!client.email).length);

  readonly avgCars = computed(() =>
    this.clients().length === 0
      ? '0'
      : (this.cars().length / this.clients().length).toFixed(1).replace('.', ','),
  );

  readonly detailTotal = computed(() =>
    (this.detail()?.orders ?? []).reduce((sum, order) => sum + Number(order.totalCost ?? 0), 0),
  );

  readonly detailLastVisit = computed(() => {
    const orders = this.detail()?.orders ?? [];
    if (orders.length === 0) {
      return null;
    }
    return orders
      .map((order) => order.createdAt)
      .sort()
      .at(-1) ?? null;
  });

  constructor() {
    this.load();
    this.route.queryParamMap.subscribe((params) => {
      if (params.get('new') === '1' && !this.createOpen()) {
        this.openCreate();
      }
      const clientId = Number(params.get('client'));
      if (
        Number.isFinite(clientId) &&
        clientId > 0 &&
        (!this.detail() || this.detail()?.client.id !== clientId)
      ) {
        this.openDetail(clientId);
      }
    });
  }

  @HostListener('document:keydown.escape')
  onEscape(): void {
    if (this.createOpen()) {
      this.closeCreate();
    } else if (this.detailOpen()) {
      this.closeDetail();
    }
  }

  load(): void {
    this.loading.set(true);
    forkJoin({ clients: this.api.clients(), cars: this.api.cars() }).subscribe({
      next: (data) => {
        this.clients.set(data.clients);
        this.cars.set(data.cars);
        this.loading.set(false);
      },
      error: (error) => {
        this.loading.set(false);
        this.toast.error(errorMessage(error));
      },
    });
  }

  carsOf(clientId: number): CarView[] {
    return this.cars().filter((car) => car.clientId === clientId);
  }

  openCreate(): void {
    this.newClient = { fullName: '', phone: '', email: '' };
    this.createError.set('');
    this.detailOpen.set(false);
    this.createOpen.set(true);
  }

  closeCreate(): void {
    if (this.creating()) {
      return;
    }
    this.createOpen.set(false);
    if (this.route.snapshot.queryParamMap.has('new')) {
      void this.router.navigate(['/clients'], { replaceUrl: true });
    }
  }

  canCreate(): boolean {
    return (
      !this.creating() && this.newClient.fullName.trim().length > 1 && this.newClient.phone.trim().length > 4
    );
  }

  create(): void {
    if (!this.canCreate()) {
      this.createError.set('Укажите ФИО и телефон клиента');
      return;
    }
    this.creating.set(true);
    this.createError.set('');
    this.api
      .createClient({
        fullName: this.newClient.fullName.trim(),
        phone: this.newClient.phone.trim(),
        email: this.newClient.email.trim() || null,
      })
      .subscribe({
        next: (client) => {
          this.creating.set(false);
          this.createOpen.set(false);
          this.toast.success(`Клиент ${client.fullName} добавлен`);
          this.load();
        },
        error: (error) => {
          this.creating.set(false);
          this.createError.set(errorMessage(error));
        },
      });
  }

  openDetail(clientId: number): void {
    this.createOpen.set(false);
    this.detailOpen.set(true);
    this.detailLoading.set(true);
    this.detail.set(null);
    this.api.client(clientId).subscribe({
      next: (detail) => {
        this.detail.set(detail);
        this.detailLoading.set(false);
      },
      error: (error) => {
        this.detailLoading.set(false);
        this.toast.error(errorMessage(error));
        this.closeDetail();
      },
    });
  }

  closeDetail(): void {
    this.detailOpen.set(false);
    if (this.route.snapshot.queryParamMap.has('client')) {
      void this.router.navigate(['/clients'], { replaceUrl: true });
    }
  }
}
````

## 84. `frontend/src/app/pages/clients/clients.html`

Строк: 360

``html
<div class="page">
  <div class="page-head">
    <div>
      <p class="eyebrow">Справочник</p>
      <h1 class="page-title">Клиенты сервиса</h1>
      <p class="page-sub">
        Владельцы автомобилей, история обращений и контактные данные из базы СТО.
      </p>
    </div>
    <div class="page-actions">
      <button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost" (click)="load()" [disabled]="loading()">
        <app-icon name="history" [size]="16" />
        Обновить
      </button>
      <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary" (click)="openCreate()">
        <app-icon name="plus" [size]="16" />
        Добавить клиента
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="mini-stats">
    <div class="mini-stat">
      <span class="mini-icon blue"><app-icon name="clients" [size]="18" /></span>
      <span>
        <strong>{{ clients().length }}</strong>
        <span>{{ pluralWord(clients().length, 'клиент', 'клиента', 'клиентов') }} в базе</span>
      </span>
    </div>
    <div class="mini-stat">
      <span class="mini-icon teal"><app-icon name="car" [size]="18" /></span>
      <span>
        <strong>{{ cars().length }}</strong>
        <span>{{ pluralWord(cars().length, 'автомобиль', 'автомобиля', 'автомобилей') }} в базе</span>
      </span>
    </div>
    <div class="mini-stat">
      <span class="mini-icon green"><app-icon name="mail" [size]="18" /></span>
      <span>
        <strong>{{ withEmail() }}</strong>
        <span>{{ pluralWord(withEmail(), 'клиент с e-mail', 'клиента с e-mail', 'клиентов с e-mail') }}</span>
      </span>
    </div>
    <div class="mini-stat">
      <span class="mini-icon orange"><app-icon name="trend" [size]="18" /></span>
      <span>
        <strong>{{ avgCars() }}</strong>
        <span>авто на клиента</span>
      </span>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="toolbar card">
    <div class="result-count">
      <strong>{{ filtered().length }}</strong>
      {{ pluralWord(filtered().length, 'клиент', 'клиента', 'клиентов') }}
    </div>
    <div class="search clients-search">
      <app-icon name="search" [size]="16" />
      <input
        type="search"
        placeholder="Имя, телефон или e-mail…"
        [value]="search()"
        (input)="search.set($any($event.target).value)"
        aria-label="Поиск клиентов"
      />
      @if (search()) {
        <button type="button" class="search-clear" (click)="search.set('')" aria-label="Очистить">
          <app-icon name="close" [size]="13" />
        </button>
      }
    </div>
  </div>

  @if (loading()) {
    <div class="client-grid">
      @for (item of [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6]; track item) {
        <div class="skeleton client-skeleton"></div>
      }
    </div>
  } @else if (filtered().length === 0) {
    <div class="card">
      <div class="empty">
        <span class="empty-icon"><app-icon name="clients" [size]="22" /></span>
        <strong>Клиенты не найдены</strong>
        <span>Попробуйте изменить запрос или добавьте нового клиента.</span>
        <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" (click)="openCreate()">
          <app-icon name="plus" [size]="14" />
          Добавить клиента
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  } @else {
    <div class="client-grid">
      @for (client of filtered(); track client.id) {
        <article class="client-card" (click)="openDetail(client.id)">
          <div class="client-top">
            <span class="avatar lg">{{ initials(client.fullName) }}</span>
            <div class="client-id">
              <strong>{{ client.fullName }}</strong>
              <span>Клиент #{{ client.id }} · с {{ shortDate(client.createdAt) }}</span>
            </div>
            <span class="client-arrow"><app-icon name="arrow" [size]="16" /></span>
          </div>

          <div class="client-contact">
            <span><app-icon name="phone" [size]="14" /> {{ client.phone }}</span>
            <span [class.muted]="!client.email">
              <app-icon name="mail" [size]="14" />
              {{ client.email || 'E-mail не указан' }}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div class="client-foot">
            <span class="chip">
              <app-icon name="car" [size]="13" />
              {{ carsOf(client.id).length }}
              {{ pluralWord(carsOf(client.id).length, 'автомобиль', 'авто', 'авто') }}
            </span>
            <div class="plate-row">
              @for (car of carsOf(client.id).slice(0, 2); track car.id) {
                <span class="chip plate">{{ car.plateNum }}</span>
              }
            </div>
          </div>
        </article>
      }
    </div>
  }
</div>

<!-- -------------------------------------------------- добавление клиента -->

@if (createOpen()) {
  <div class="modal-backdrop" (click)="closeCreate()">
    <div class="modal" (click)="$event.stopPropagation()">
      <div class="modal-head">
        <div>
          <h2>Новый клиент</h2>
          <p>Контактные данные для связи и оформления заявок</p>
        </div>
        <button type="button" class="modal-close" (click)="closeCreate()" aria-label="Закрыть">
          <app-icon name="close" [size]="17" />
        </button>
      </div>

      <form (submit)="$event.preventDefault(); create()">
        <div class="modal-body">
          <div class="form-grid">
            <label class="field span-2">
              <span class="label">ФИО клиента *</span>
              <input
                class="input"
                name="fullName"
                maxlength="150"
                placeholder="Иванов Иван Иванович"
                [(ngModel)]="newClient.fullName"
              />
            </label>
            <label class="field">
              <span class="label">Телефон *</span>
              <input
                class="input"
                name="phone"
                maxlength="20"
                placeholder="+7-900-000-00-00"
                [(ngModel)]="newClient.phone"
              />
              <span class="hint">Допускаются цифры, пробелы, скобки и дефисы.</span>
            </label>
            <label class="field">
              <span class="label">E-mail</span>
              <input
                class="input"
                name="email"
                type="email"
                maxlength="100"
                placeholder="client@example.ru"
                [(ngModel)]="newClient.email"
              />
              <span class="hint">Необязательное поле.</span>
            </label>
          </div>

          @if (createError()) {
            <div class="error-text form-error">
              <app-icon name="alert" [size]="15" />
              {{ createError() }}
            </div>
          }
        </div>

        <div class="modal-foot">
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost" (click)="closeCreate()" [disabled]="creating()">
            Отмена
          </button>
          <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary" [disabled]="!canCreate()">
            @if (creating()) {
              <span class="spinner"></span>
              Сохраняем…
            } @else {
              <app-icon name="check" [size]="16" />
              Добавить клиента
            }
          </button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  </div>
}

<!-- ------------------------------------------------------ карточка клиента -->

@if (detailOpen()) {
  <div class="modal-backdrop" (click)="closeDetail()">
    <div class="modal wide" (click)="$event.stopPropagation()">
      <div class="modal-head">
        <div class="client-modal-head">
          @if (detail(); as d) {
            <span class="avatar lg">{{ initials(d.client.fullName) }}</span>
            <div>
              <h2>{{ d.client.fullName }}</h2>
              <p>Клиент #{{ d.client.id }} · в базе с {{ shortDate(d.client.createdAt) }}</p>
            </div>
          } @else {
            <div>
              <h2>Карточка клиента</h2>
              <p>Загрузка данных…</p>
            </div>
          }
        </div>
        <button type="button" class="modal-close" (click)="closeDetail()" aria-label="Закрыть">
          <app-icon name="close" [size]="17" />
        </button>
      </div>

      <div class="modal-body">
        @if (detailLoading()) {
          <div class="loading-line">
            <span class="spinner dark"></span>
            Читаем клиента из базы…
          </div>
        } @else if (detail(); as d) {
          <div class="client-summary">
            <div>
              <span>Телефон</span>
              <strong>{{ d.client.phone }}</strong>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span>E-mail</span>
              <strong>{{ d.client.email || '—' }}</strong>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span>Заявок</span>
              <strong>{{ d.orders.length }}</strong>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span>Сумма работ</span>
              <strong>{{ money(detailTotal()) }}</strong>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span>Последний визит</span>
              <strong>{{ detailLastVisit() ? shortDate(detailLastVisit()) : '—' }}</strong>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="client-actions">
            <a
              class="btn btn-primary btn-sm"
              routerLink="/orders"
              [queryParams]="{ new: 1, clientId: d.client.id }"
              (click)="closeDetail()"
            >
              <app-icon name="plus" [size]="14" />
              Создать заявку
            </a>
            <a
              class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm"
              routerLink="/cars"
              [queryParams]="{ new: 1, clientId: d.client.id }"
              (click)="closeDetail()"
            >
              <app-icon name="car" [size]="14" />
              Добавить автомобиль
            </a>
          </div>

          <div class="section-head">
            <h3>Автомобили</h3>
            <span class="badge plain">{{ d.cars.length }}</span>
          </div>

          @if (d.cars.length === 0) {
            <p class="muted small-text">У клиента пока нет автомобилей в базе.</p>
          } @else {
            <div class="car-list">
              @for (car of d.cars; track car.id) {
                <div class="car-item">
                  <span class="car-icon"><app-icon name="car" [size]="17" /></span>
                  <span class="car-meta">
                    <strong>{{ car.brand }} {{ car.model }}</strong>
                    <span>{{ car.year || 'год не указан' }} · VIN {{ car.vin || '—' }}</span>
                  </span>
                  <span class="chip plate">{{ car.plateNum }}</span>
                </div>
              }
            </div>
          }

          <div class="section-head">
            <h3>История заявок</h3>
            <span class="badge plain">{{ d.orders.length }}</span>
          </div>

          @if (d.orders.length === 0) {
            <p class="muted small-text">Обращений пока не было.</p>
          } @else {
            <div class="table-wrap client-orders">
              <table class="data-table">
                <thead>
                  <tr>
                    <th>Заявка</th>
                    <th>Автомобиль</th>
                    <th>Статус</th>
                    <th>Дата</th>
                    <th class="right">Сумма</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  @for (order of d.orders; track order.orderId) {
                    <tr
                      class="row-link"
                      routerLink="/orders"
                      [queryParams]="{ order: order.orderId }"
                      (click)="closeDetail()"
                    >
                      <td class="strong">#{{ order.orderId }}</td>
                      <td><span class="chip plate">{{ order.carPlate }}</span></td>
                      <td>
                        <span class="badge" [class]="'badge ' + statusClass(order.status)">
                          {{ order.status }}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td class="muted">{{ shortDate(order.createdAt) }}</td>
                      <td class="right strong">{{ money(order.totalCost) }}</td>
                    </tr>
                  }
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          }
        }
      </div>

      <div class="modal-foot">
        <button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost" (click)="closeDetail()">Закрыть</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
}

``css
.mini-stats {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr));
  gap: 12px;
  margin-bottom: 18px;
}

.mini-stat {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 11px;
  padding: 13px 15px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 15px;
  background: var(--surface);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-sm);
}

.mini-icon {
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 36px;
  height: 36px;
  border-radius: 11px;
}

.mini-icon.blue {
  color: var(--brand);
  background: var(--brand-soft);
}

.mini-icon.teal {
  color: #0f8f8f;
  background: var(--teal-soft);
}

.mini-icon.green {
  color: #047857;
  background: var(--success-soft);
}

.mini-icon.orange {
  color: #c2410c;
  background: var(--accent-soft);
}

.mini-stat strong {
  display: block;
  font-size: 17px;
  font-weight: 800;
  line-height: 1;
  letter-spacing: -0.03em;
}

.mini-stat span span {
  display: block;

  font-weight: 600;
}

.toolbar {

.car-meta {

}

@media (max-width: 1180px) {
  .client-grid {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }

  .client-summary {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }
}

@media (max-width: 860px) {
  .mini-stats {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }

  .toolbar {
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: stretch;
    gap: 10px;
    padding: 12px;
  }

  .clients-search {
    width: 100%;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 680px) {
  .client-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .client-summary {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }
}

@media (max-width: 460px) {
  .mini-stats {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
}

``ts
import { Component, HostListener, computed, inject, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { ActivatedRoute, Router, RouterLink } from '@angular/router';
import { forkJoin } from 'rxjs';
import { ApiService, errorMessage } from '../../core/api.service';
import { CarView, Client, initials, plural, pluralWord } from '../../core/models';
import { ToastService } from '../../core/toast.service';
import { Icon } from '../../shared/icon';

const GRADIENTS = [
  'linear-gradient(135deg, #3b82f6, #06b6d4)',
  'linear-gradient(135deg, #8b5cf6, #6366f1)',
  'linear-gradient(135deg, #0d9488, #22d3ee)',
  'linear-gradient(135deg, #f97316, #f59e0b)',
  'linear-gradient(135deg, #ec4899, #8b5cf6)',
  'linear-gradient(135deg, #0ea5e9, #2563eb)',
];

@Component({
  selector: 'app-cars',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [FormsModule, RouterLink, Icon],
  templateUrl: './cars.html',
  styleUrl: './cars.css',
})
export class Cars {
  private readonly api = inject(ApiService);
  private readonly toast = inject(ToastService);
  private readonly route = inject(ActivatedRoute);
  private readonly router = inject(Router);

  readonly initials = initials;
  readonly plural = plural;
  readonly pluralWord = pluralWord;

  readonly loading = signal(true);
  readonly cars = signal<CarView[]>([]);
  readonly clients = signal<Client[]>([]);
  readonly search = signal('');

  readonly createOpen = signal(false);
  readonly createError = signal('');
  readonly creating = signal(false);

  newCar = {
    clientId: null as number | null,
    brand: '',
    model: '',
    year: null as number | null,
    plateNum: '',
    vin: '',
  };

  readonly filtered = computed(() => {
    const query = this.search().trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!query) {
      return this.cars();
    }
    return this.cars().filter(
      (car) =>
        car.brand.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        car.model.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        car.plateNum.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        (car.vin ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        car.clientName.toLowerCase().includes(query),
    );
  });

  readonly brands = computed(() => new Set(this.cars().map((car) => car.brand)).size);

  readonly owners = computed(() => new Set(this.cars().map((car) => car.clientId)).size);

  readonly recentCars = computed(
    () => this.cars().filter((car) => (car.year ?? 0) >= new Date().getFullYear() - 5).length,
  );

  constructor() {
    this.load();
    this.route.queryParamMap.subscribe((params) => {
      if (params.get('new') === '1') {
        const clientId = Number(params.get('clientId'));
        this.openCreate(Number.isFinite(clientId) && clientId > 0 ? clientId : null);
      }
    });
  }

  @HostListener('document:keydown.escape')
  onEscape(): void {
    if (this.createOpen()) {
      this.closeCreate();
    }
  }

  load(): void {
    this.loading.set(true);
    forkJoin({ cars: this.api.cars(), clients: this.api.clients() }).subscribe({
      next: (data) => {
        this.cars.set(data.cars);
        this.clients.set(data.clients);
        this.loading.set(false);
      },
      error: (error) => {
        this.loading.set(false);
        this.toast.error(errorMessage(error));
      },
    });
  }

  brandGradient(brand: string): string {
    let hash = 0;
    for (const char of brand) {
      hash = (hash + char.charCodeAt(0)) % GRADIENTS.length;
    }
    return GRADIENTS[hash];
  }

  openCreate(clientId: number | null = null): void {
    this.newCar = { clientId, brand: '', model: '', year: null, plateNum: '', vin: '' };
    this.createError.set('');
    this.createOpen.set(true);
  }

  closeCreate(): void {
    if (this.creating()) {
      return;
    }
    this.createOpen.set(false);
    if (this.route.snapshot.queryParamMap.has('new')) {
      void this.router.navigate(['/cars'], { replaceUrl: true });
    }
  }

  selectedClient(): Client | undefined {
    return this.clients().find((client) => client.id === this.newCar.clientId);
  }

  canCreate(): boolean {
    return (
      !this.creating() &&
      this.newCar.clientId !== null &&
      this.newCar.brand.trim().length > 0 &&
      this.newCar.model.trim().length > 0 &&
      this.newCar.plateNum.trim().length > 0
    );
  }

  create(): void {
    if (!this.canCreate()) {
      this.createError.set('Выберите владельца и заполните марку, модель и гос. номер');
      return;
    }
    this.creating.set(true);
    this.createError.set('');
    this.api
      .createCar({
        clientId: this.newCar.clientId!,
        brand: this.newCar.brand.trim(),
        model: this.newCar.model.trim(),
        year: this.newCar.year ?? null,
        plateNum: this.newCar.plateNum.trim(),
        vin: this.newCar.vin.trim() || null,
      })
      .subscribe({
        next: (car) => {
          this.creating.set(false);
          this.createOpen.set(false);
          this.toast.success(`Автомобиль ${car.brand} ${car.model} добавлен`);
          this.load();
          if (this.route.snapshot.queryParamMap.has('new')) {
            void this.router.navigate(['/cars'], { replaceUrl: true });
          }
        },
        error: (error) => {
          this.creating.set(false);
          this.createError.set(errorMessage(error));
        },
      });
  }
}
````

<div class="page">
  <div class="page-head">
    <div>
      <p class="eyebrow">Автопарк</p>
      <h1 class="page-title">Автомобили клиентов</h1>
      <p class="page-sub">
        Марки, модели, гос. номера и VIN всех машин, обслуживающихся в сервисе.
      </p>
    </div>
    <div class="page-actions">
      <button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost" (click)="load()" [disabled]="loading()">
        <app-icon name="history" [size]="16" />
        Обновить
      </button>
      <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary" (click)="openCreate()">
        <app-icon name="plus" [size]="16" />
        Добавить автомобиль
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="mini-stats">
    <div class="mini-stat">
      <span class="mini-icon teal"><app-icon name="car" [size]="18" /></span>
      <span>
        <strong>{{ cars().length }}</strong>
        <span>{{ pluralWord(cars().length, 'автомобиль', 'автомобиля', 'автомобилей') }} в парке</span>
      </span>
    </div>
    <div class="mini-stat">
      <span class="mini-icon blue"><app-icon name="dashboard" [size]="18" /></span>
      <span>
        <strong>{{ brands() }}</strong>
        <span>{{ pluralWord(brands(), 'марка', 'марки', 'марок') }} в парке</span>
      </span>
    </div>
    <div class="mini-stat">
      <span class="mini-icon violet"><app-icon name="clients" [size]="18" /></span>
      <span>
        <strong>{{ owners() }}</strong>
        <span>{{ pluralWord(owners(), 'владелец', 'владельца', 'владельцев') }}</span>
      </span>
    </div>
    <div class="mini-stat">
      <span class="mini-icon orange"><app-icon name="clock" [size]="18" /></span>
      <span>
        <strong>{{ recentCars() }}</strong>
        <span>{{ pluralWord(recentCars(), 'автомобиль', 'автомобиля', 'автомобилей') }} не старше 5 лет</span>
      </span>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="toolbar card">
    <div class="result-count">
      <strong>{{ filtered().length }}</strong>
      {{ pluralWord(filtered().length, 'автомобиль', 'автомобиля', 'автомобилей') }}
    </div>
    <div class="search cars-search">
      <app-icon name="search" [size]="16" />
      <input
        type="search"
        placeholder="Марка, номер, VIN или владелец…"
        [value]="search()"
        (input)="search.set($any($event.target).value)"
        aria-label="Поиск автомобилей"

      @if (search()) {

  @if (loading()) {

  } @else if (filtered().length === 0) {

          <div class="car-vin">

          </div>
        </article>
      }
    </div>
  }
</div>

<!-- ------------------------------------------------ добавление автомобиля -->

@if (createOpen()) {
  <div class="modal-backdrop" (click)="closeCreate()">
    <div class="modal" (click)="$event.stopPropagation()">
      <div class="modal-head">
        <div>
          <h2>Новый автомобиль</h2>
          <p>Привяжите машину к клиенту сервиса</p>
        </div>
        <button type="button" class="modal-close" (click)="closeCreate()" aria-label="Закрыть">
          <app-icon name="close" [size]="17" />
        </button>
      </div>

      <form (submit)="$event.preventDefault(); create()">
        <div class="modal-body">
          <div class="form-grid">
            <label class="field span-2">
              <span class="label">Владелец *</span>
              <select class="select" name="clientId" [(ngModel)]="newCar.clientId">
                <option [ngValue]="null" disabled>Выберите клиента</option>
                @for (client of clients(); track client.id) {
                  <option [ngValue]="client.id">{{ client.fullName }} · {{ client.phone }}</option>
                }
              </select>
              @if (selectedClient(); as client) {
                <span class="hint">{{ client.email || 'E-mail не указан' }}</span>
              } @else {
                <span class="hint">
                  Нет нужного клиента? Добавьте его в разделе
                  <a routerLink="/clients" (click)="closeCreate()">«Клиенты»</a>.
                </span>
              }
            </label>

            <label class="field">
              <span class="label">Марка *</span>
              <input
                class="input"
                name="brand"
                maxlength="50"
                placeholder="Lada"
                [(ngModel)]="newCar.brand"
              />
            </label>
            <label class="field">
              <span class="label">Модель *</span>
              <input
                class="input"
                name="model"
                maxlength="50"
                placeholder="Vesta"
                [(ngModel)]="newCar.model"
              />
            </label>
            <label class="field">
              <span class="label">Год выпуска</span>
              <input
                class="input"
                type="number"
                name="year"
                min="1950"
                max="2100"
                placeholder="2021"
                [(ngModel)]="newCar.year"
              />
            </label>
            <label class="field">
              <span class="label">Гос. номер *</span>
              <input
                class="input"
                name="plateNum"
                maxlength="15"
                placeholder="А123ВС64"
                [(ngModel)]="newCar.plateNum"
              />
            </label>
            <label class="field span-2">
              <span class="label">VIN</span>
              <input
                class="input mono"
                name="vin"
                maxlength="17"
                placeholder="17 символов"
                [(ngModel)]="newCar.vin"
              />
              <span class="hint">Необязательное поле, не длиннее 17 символов.</span>
            </label>
          </div>

          @if (createError()) {
            <div class="error-text form-error">
              <app-icon name="alert" [size]="15" />
              {{ createError() }}
            </div>
          }
        </div>

        <div class="modal-foot">
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost" (click)="closeCreate()" [disabled]="creating()">
            Отмена
          </button>
          <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary" [disabled]="!canCreate()">
            @if (creating()) {
              <span class="spinner"></span>
              Сохраняем…
            } @else {
              <app-icon name="check" [size]="16" />
              Добавить автомобиль
            }
          </button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  </div>
}

.mini-stats {

}

.mini-stat {

  align-items: center;
  gap: 11px;

  border: 1px solid var(--line);

}

  display: grid;

}

}

}

.car-grid {

.car-skeleton {

}

.form-error {

}

@media (max-width: 1180px) {
  .mini-stats {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }
}

@media (max-width: 980px) {

@media (max-width: 860px) {

@media (max-width: 460px) {
  .mini-stats {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .car-top {
    flex-wrap: wrap;
  }
}

## 89. `frontend/src/app/pages/services/services.ts`

Строк: 148

``ts
import { Component, HostListener, computed, inject, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { ApiService, errorMessage } from '../../core/api.service';
import { Service, money, pluralWord } from '../../core/models';
import { ToastService } from '../../core/toast.service';
import { Icon } from '../../shared/icon';

type SortMode = 'name' | 'price-asc' | 'price-desc';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-services',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [FormsModule, Icon],
  templateUrl: './services.html',
  styleUrl: './services.css',
})
export class ServicesPage {
  private readonly api = inject(ApiService);
  private readonly toast = inject(ToastService);

  readonly money = money;
  readonly pluralWord = pluralWord;

  readonly loading = signal(true);
  readonly services = signal<Service[]>([]);
  readonly search = signal('');
  readonly sort = signal<SortMode>('name');

  readonly createOpen = signal(false);
  readonly createError = signal('');
  readonly creating = signal(false);

  newService = { name: '', price: null as number | null, durationMin: null as number | null };

  readonly filtered = computed(() => {
    const query = this.search().trim().toLowerCase();
    let result = this.services();
    if (query) {
      result = result.filter((service) => service.name.toLowerCase().includes(query));
    }
    return [...result].sort((a, b) => {
      switch (this.sort()) {
        case 'price-asc':
          return Number(a.price) - Number(b.price);
        case 'price-desc':
          return Number(b.price) - Number(a.price);
        default:
          return a.name.localeCompare(b.name, 'ru');
      }
    });
  });

  readonly avgPrice = computed(() => {
    if (this.services().length === 0) {
      return 0;
    }
    return this.services().reduce((sum, service) => sum + Number(service.price), 0) / this.services().length;
  });

  readonly maxPrice = computed(() =>
    this.services().reduce((max, service) => Math.max(max, Number(service.price)), 0),
  );

  readonly avgDuration = computed(() => {
    const withDuration = this.services().filter((service) => !!service.durationMin);
    if (withDuration.length === 0) {
      return 0;
    }
    return Math.round(
      withDuration.reduce((sum, service) => sum + Number(service.durationMin), 0) /
        withDuration.length,
    );
  });

  constructor() {
    this.load();
  }

  @HostListener('document:keydown.escape')
  onEscape(): void {
    if (this.createOpen()) {
      this.closeCreate();
    }
  }

  load(): void {
    this.loading.set(true);
    this.api.services().subscribe({
      next: (services) => {
        this.services.set(services);
        this.loading.set(false);
      },
      error: (error) => {
        this.loading.set(false);
        this.toast.error(errorMessage(error));
      },
    });
  }

  openCreate(): void {
    this.newService = { name: '', price: null, durationMin: null };
    this.createError.set('');
    this.createOpen.set(true);
  }

  closeCreate(): void {
    if (this.creating()) {
      return;
    }
    this.createOpen.set(false);
  }

  canCreate(): boolean {
    return (
      !this.creating() &&
      this.newService.name.trim().length > 1 &&
      this.newService.price !== null &&
      Number(this.newService.price) >= 0
    );
  }

  create(): void {
    if (!this.canCreate()) {
      this.createError.set('Укажите название и неотрицательную стоимость услуги');
      return;
    }
    this.creating.set(true);
    this.createError.set('');
    this.api
      .createService({
        name: this.newService.name.trim(),
        price: Number(this.newService.price),
        durationMin: this.newService.durationMin ? Number(this.newService.durationMin) : null,
      })
      .subscribe({
        next: (service) => {
          this.creating.set(false);
          this.createOpen.set(false);
          this.toast.success(`Услуга «${service.name}» добавлена`);
          this.load();
        },
        error: (error) => {
          this.creating.set(false);
          this.createError.set(errorMessage(error));
        },
      });
  }
}
````

``html
<div class="page">
  <div class="page-head">
    <div>
      <p class="eyebrow">Прайс-лист</p>
      <h1 class="page-title">Услуги и работы</h1>
      <p class="page-sub">
        Стоимость и нормативная длительность работ. Цена услуги фиксируется в заявке на момент
        добавления.
      </p>
    </div>
    <div class="page-actions">
      <button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost" (click)="load()" [disabled]="loading()">
        <app-icon name="history" [size]="16" />
        Обновить
      </button>
      <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary" (click)="openCreate()">
        <app-icon name="plus" [size]="16" />
        Добавить услугу
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="mini-stats">
    <div class="mini-stat">
      <span class="mini-icon orange"><app-icon name="services" [size]="18" /></span>
      <span>
        <strong>{{ services().length }}</strong>
        <span>{{ pluralWord(services().length, 'услуга', 'услуги', 'услуг') }} в прайсе</span>
      </span>
    </div>
    <div class="mini-stat">
      <span class="mini-icon green"><app-icon name="ruble" [size]="18" /></span>
      <span>
        <strong>{{ money(avgPrice()) }}</strong>
        <span>средняя стоимость</span>
      </span>
    </div>
    <div class="mini-stat">
      <span class="mini-icon blue"><app-icon name="trend" [size]="18" /></span>
      <span>

        <span>максимальная цена</span>

    </div>
    <div class="mini-stat">

      <span>

        <span>средняя длительность</span>
      </span>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="toolbar card">

      <app-icon name="search" [size]="16" />
      <input
        type="search"
        placeholder="Название услуги…"
        [value]="search()"
        (input)="search.set($any($event.target).value)"
        aria-label="Поиск услуг"

      @if (search()) {

          <app-icon name="close" [size]="13" />
        </button>

    <label class="sort-control">

        <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" (click)="openCreate()">

        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  } @else {
    <div class="service-grid">
      @for (service of filtered(); track service.id) {
        <article class="service-card">
          <div class="service-top">
            <span class="service-icon"><app-icon name="services" [size]="20" /></span>
            <span class="service-id">#{{ service.id }}</span>
          </div>
          <h3>{{ service.name }}</h3>
          <div class="service-price">{{ money(service.price) }}</div>
          <div class="service-foot">
            <span class="chip">
              <app-icon name="clock" [size]="13" />
              {{ service.durationMin ? service.durationMin + ' мин' : 'время не задано' }}
            </span>
            <span class="service-apply">в заявку</span>
          </div>
        </article>
      }
    </div>
  }
</div>

<!-- ---------------------------------------------------- добавление услуги -->

@if (createOpen()) {
  <div class="modal-backdrop" (click)="closeCreate()">
    <div class="modal" (click)="$event.stopPropagation()">
      <div class="modal-head">
        <div>
          <h2>Новая услуга</h2>
          <p>Стоимость и длительность работы для прайс-листа</p>
        </div>
        <button type="button" class="modal-close" (click)="closeCreate()" aria-label="Закрыть">
          <app-icon name="close" [size]="17" />
        </button>
      </div>

      <form (submit)="$event.preventDefault(); create()">
        <div class="modal-body">
          <div class="form-grid">
            <label class="field span-2">
              <span class="label">Название услуги *</span>
              <input
                class="input"
                name="name"
                maxlength="150"
                placeholder="Например: замена масла в двигателе"
                [(ngModel)]="newService.name"
              />
            </label>
            <label class="field">
              <span class="label">Стоимость, ₽ *</span>
              <input
                class="input"
                type="number"
                name="price"
                min="0"
                step="0.01"
                placeholder="1500"
                [(ngModel)]="newService.price"
              />
            </label>
            <label class="field">
              <span class="label">Длительность, мин</span>
              <input
                class="input"
                type="number"
                name="durationMin"
                min="1"
                placeholder="40"
                [(ngModel)]="newService.durationMin"
              />
              <span class="hint">Необязательное поле, больше нуля.</span>
            </label>
          </div>

          @if (createError()) {
            <div class="error-text form-error">
              <app-icon name="alert" [size]="15" />
              {{ createError() }}
            </div>
          }
        </div>

        <div class="modal-foot">
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost" (click)="closeCreate()" [disabled]="creating()">
            Отмена
          </button>
          <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary" [disabled]="!canCreate()">
            @if (creating()) {
              <span class="spinner"></span>
              Сохраняем…
            } @else {
              <app-icon name="check" [size]="16" />
              Добавить услугу
            }
          </button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  </div>
}

}

.service-icon {

  gap: 8px;

  border-top: 1px solid #eef2f8;
}

.service-apply {

  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.service-skeleton {

}

.form-error {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
  margin-top: 14px;
}

@media (max-width: 1180px) {
  .mini-stats {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }

  .service-grid {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }
}

@media (max-width: 860px) {
  .toolbar {
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: stretch;
    gap: 10px;
  }

  .services-search {
    width: 100%;
  }

  .sort-control {
    justify-content: space-between;
  }

  .sort-control .select {
    flex: 1;
    max-width: 220px;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 640px) {
  .service-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 460px) {
  .mini-stats {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
}
