## 92. `frontend/src/app/pages/masters/masters.ts`

Строк: 92

``ts
import { Component, computed, inject, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { forkJoin } from 'rxjs';
import { ApiService, errorMessage } from '../../core/api.service';
import { Master, initials, plural, pluralWord } from '../../core/models';
import { ToastService } from '../../core/toast.service';
import { Icon } from '../../shared/icon';

const AVATAR_GRADIENTS = [
  'linear-gradient(135deg, #3b82f6, #06b6d4)',
  'linear-gradient(135deg, #8b5cf6, #6366f1)',
  'linear-gradient(135deg, #0d9488, #22d3ee)',
  'linear-gradient(135deg, #f97316, #f59e0b)',
  'linear-gradient(135deg, #ec4899, #8b5cf6)',
];

@Component({
  selector: 'app-masters',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [Icon],
  templateUrl: './masters.html',
  styleUrl: './masters.css',
})
export class Masters {
  private readonly api = inject(ApiService);
  private readonly toast = inject(ToastService);

  readonly initials = initials;
  readonly plural = plural;
  readonly pluralWord = pluralWord;

  readonly loading = signal(true);
  readonly masters = signal<Master[]>([]);
  readonly workload = signal<Record<string, number>>({});
  readonly search = signal('');

  readonly filtered = computed(() => {
    const query = this.search().trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!query) {
      return this.masters();
    }
    return this.masters().filter(
      (master) =>
        master.fullName.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        (master.specialization ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(query),
    );
  });

  readonly activeMasters = computed(() => this.masters().filter((master) => master.active).length);

  readonly specializationCount = computed(
    () => new Set(this.masters().map((master) => master.specialization).filter(Boolean)).size,
  );

  readonly totalWorkload = computed(() =>
    this.masters().reduce((sum, master) => sum + this.workloadOf(master.id), 0),
  );

  readonly maxWorkload = computed(() =>
    Math.max(1, ...this.masters().map((master) => this.workloadOf(master.id))),
  );

  constructor() {
    this.load();
  }

  load(): void {
    this.loading.set(true);
    forkJoin({ masters: this.api.masters(), workload: this.api.masterWorkload() }).subscribe({
      next: (data) => {
        this.masters.set(data.masters);
        this.workload.set(data.workload);
        this.loading.set(false);
      },
      error: (error) => {
        this.loading.set(false);
        this.toast.error(errorMessage(error));
      },
    });
  }

  workloadOf(masterId: number): number {
    return Number(this.workload()[String(masterId)] ?? 0);
  }

  workloadPercent(masterId: number): number {
    return Math.round((this.workloadOf(masterId) / this.maxWorkload()) * 100);
  }

  avatarGradient(masterId: number): string {
    return AVATAR_GRADIENTS[masterId % AVATAR_GRADIENTS.length];
  }
}
````

## 93. `frontend/src/app/pages/masters/masters.html`

Строк: 134

``html
<div class="page">
  <div class="page-head">
    <div>
      <p class="eyebrow">Команда</p>
      <h1 class="page-title">Мастера сервиса</h1>
      <p class="page-sub">
        Специализации и текущая загрузка. Мастера привязаны к пользователям системы и их ролям.
      </p>
    </div>
    <div class="page-actions">
      <button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost" (click)="load()" [disabled]="loading()">
        <app-icon name="history" [size]="16" />
        Обновить
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="mini-stats">
    <div class="mini-stat">
      <span class="mini-icon violet"><app-icon name="masters" [size]="18" /></span>
      <span>
        <strong>{{ masters().length }}</strong>
        <span>{{ pluralWord(masters().length, 'мастер', 'мастера', 'мастеров') }} в базе</span>
      </span>
    </div>
    <div class="mini-stat">
      <span class="mini-icon green"><app-icon name="check" [size]="18" /></span>
      <span>
        <strong>{{ activeMasters() }}</strong>
        <span>{{ pluralWord(activeMasters(), 'принимает заявки', 'принимают заявки', 'принимают заявки') }}</span>
      </span>
    </div>
    <div class="mini-stat">
      <span class="mini-icon blue"><app-icon name="settings" [size]="18" /></span>
      <span>
        <strong>{{ specializationCount() }}</strong>
        <span>{{ pluralWord(specializationCount(), 'специализация', 'специализации', 'специализаций') }}</span>
      </span>
    </div>
    <div class="mini-stat">
      <span class="mini-icon orange"><app-icon name="clock" [size]="18" /></span>
      <span>
        <strong>{{ totalWorkload() }}</strong>
        <span>{{ pluralWord(totalWorkload(), 'активная заявка', 'активные заявки', 'активных заявок') }}</span>
      </span>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="toolbar card">
    <div class="result-count">
      <strong>{{ filtered().length }}</strong>
      {{ pluralWord(filtered().length, 'мастер', 'мастера', 'мастеров') }}
    </div>
    <div class="search masters-search">
      <app-icon name="search" [size]="16" />
      <input
        type="search"
        placeholder="Имя или специализация…"
        [value]="search()"
        (input)="search.set($any($event.target).value)"
        aria-label="Поиск мастеров"
      />
      @if (search()) {
        <button type="button" class="search-clear" (click)="search.set('')" aria-label="Очистить">
          <app-icon name="close" [size]="13" />
        </button>
      }
    </div>
  </div>

  @if (loading()) {
    <div class="master-grid">
      @for (item of [1, 2, 3]; track item) {
        <div class="skeleton master-skeleton"></div>
      }
    </div>
  } @else if (filtered().length === 0) {
    <div class="card">
      <div class="empty">
        <span class="empty-icon"><app-icon name="masters" [size]="22" /></span>
        <strong>Мастера не найдены</strong>
        <span>Попробуйте изменить поисковый запрос.</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  } @else {
    <div class="master-grid">
      @for (master of filtered(); track master.id) {
        <article class="master-card" [class.inactive]="!master.active">
          <div class="master-head">
            <span class="master-avatar" [style.background]="avatarGradient(master.id)">
              {{ initials(master.fullName) }}
            </span>
            <div class="master-name">
              <strong>{{ master.fullName }}</strong>
              <span>{{ master.specialization || 'Универсальный мастер' }}</span>
            </div>
            <span class="badge" [class]="master.active ? 'badge done' : 'badge neutral'">
              {{ master.active ? 'активен' : 'неактивен' }}
            </span>
          </div>

          <div class="workload">
            <div class="workload-head">
              <span>Текущая загрузка</span>
              <strong>{{ plural(workloadOf(master.id), 'заявка', 'заявки', 'заявок') }}</strong>
            </div>
            <div class="workload-track">
              <span
                class="workload-fill"
                [style.width.%]="master.active ? workloadPercent(master.id) : 0"
              ></span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="master-foot">
            <span class="chip"><app-icon name="settings" [size]="13" /> ID {{ master.id }}</span>
            <span class="chip"><app-icon name="clients" [size]="13" /> user #{{ master.userId }}</span>
          </div>
        </article>
      }
    </div>

    <div class="master-note">
      <span class="note-icon"><app-icon name="database" [size]="17" /></span>
      <div>
        <strong>Как добавить мастера?</strong>
        <span>
          Мастер создаётся вместе с пользователем в таблице <code>users</code> и записью в
          <code>masters</code>. Загрузка считается по активным заявкам («новая» и «в работе»).
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  }
</div>
````

  width: 310px;
  background: var(--surface-2);
}

.search-clear {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 22px;
  height: 22px;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 6px;
  color: var(--muted);
  background: transparent;
}

.search-clear:hover {
  color: var(--ink);
  background: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.06);
}

.master-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
  gap: 14px;
}

.master-card {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 16px;
  padding: 18px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
  background: var(--surface);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-sm);
  transition: transform 0.18s ease, box-shadow 0.18s ease, border-color 0.18s ease;
}

.master-card:hover {
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  border-color: #c9d9f5;
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-md);
}

.master-card.inactive {
  opacity: 0.78;
}

.master-head {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 11px;
}

.master-avatar {
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 46px;
  height: 46px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  color: #fff;
  font-size: 14px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.02em;
  box-shadow: 0 10px 22px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.18);
}

.master-name {
  min-width: 0;
  flex: 1;
}

.master-name strong {
  display: block;
  overflow: hidden;
  white-space: nowrap;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
  font-size: 13px;
  font-weight: 800;
}

.master-name span {
  display: block;
  margin-top: 2px;
  overflow: hidden;
  white-space: nowrap;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 10.5px;
}

.workload {
  padding: 12px 13px;
  border-radius: 12px;
  background: var(--surface-2);
}

.workload-head {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 10px;
  margin-bottom: 8px;
  font-size: 10.5px;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.workload-head strong {
  color: var(--ink-soft);
  font-size: 10.5px;
  font-weight: 800;
}

.workload-track {
  height: 7px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: #e6ecf6;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.workload-fill {
  display: block;
  height: 100%;
  border-radius: inherit;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, #8b5cf6, #3b82f6);
  transition: width 0.45s ease;
}

.master-foot {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 7px;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
}

.master-skeleton {
  height: 196px;
}

.master-note {
  display: flex;
  align-items: flex-start;
  gap: 12px;
  margin-top: 18px;
  padding: 15px 17px;
  border: 1px dashed var(--line-strong);
  border-radius: 14px;
  background: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.6);
}

.note-icon {
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 34px;
  height: 34px;
  border-radius: 10px;
  color: var(--brand);
  background: var(--brand-soft);
}

.master-note strong {
  display: block;
  font-size: 11.5px;
  font-weight: 800;
}

.master-note span span {
  display: block;
  margin-top: 3px;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 10.5px;
  line-height: 1.5;
}

.master-note code {
  padding: 1px 4px;
  border-radius: 5px;
  background: var(--surface-2);
  color: var(--ink-soft);
}

@media (max-width: 1180px) {
  .mini-stats {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }

  .master-grid {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }
}

@media (max-width: 860px) {
  .toolbar {
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: stretch;
    gap: 10px;
    padding: 12px;
  }

  .masters-search {
    width: 100%;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 680px) {
  .master-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 460px) {
  .mini-stats {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
}
````

## 95. `frontend/src/app/pages/status-log/status-log.ts`

Строк: 152

``ts
import { Component, computed, inject, signal } from '@angular/core';
import { FormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { RouterLink } from '@angular/router';
import { forkJoin } from 'rxjs';
import { ApiService, errorMessage } from '../../core/api.service';
import {
  OrderStatus,
  OrderSummary,
  StatusLogEntry,
  plural,
  pluralWord,
  statusClass,
} from '../../core/models';
import { ToastService } from '../../core/toast.service';
import { Icon } from '../../shared/icon';

interface LogDay {
  key: string;
  label: string;
  entries: StatusLogEntry[];
}

@Component({
  selector: 'app-status-log',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [FormsModule, RouterLink, Icon],
  templateUrl: './status-log.html',
  styleUrl: './status-log.css',
})
export class StatusLogPage {
  private readonly api = inject(ApiService);
  private readonly toast = inject(ToastService);

  readonly statusClass = statusClass;
  readonly plural = plural;
  readonly pluralWord = pluralWord;

  readonly loading = signal(true);
  readonly log = signal<StatusLogEntry[]>([]);
  readonly statuses = signal<OrderStatus[]>([]);
  readonly orders = signal<OrderSummary[]>([]);

  readonly search = signal('');
  readonly orderFilter = signal<number | null>(null);

  readonly filtered = computed(() => {
    const query = this.search().trim().toLowerCase();
    const orderId = this.orderFilter();
    return this.log().filter((entry) => {
      if (orderId !== null && entry.orderId !== orderId) {
        return false;
      }
      if (!query) {
        return true;
      }
      const order = this.orderOf(entry.orderId);
      return (
        String(entry.orderId).includes(query) ||
        entry.changedBy.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        this.statusName(entry.oldStatusId).toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        this.statusName(entry.newStatusId).toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        (order?.client ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
        (order?.car ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(query)
      );
    });
  });

  readonly groups = computed<LogDay[]>(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, LogDay>();
    for (const entry of this.filtered()) {
      const date = new Date(entry.changedAt);
      const key = `${date.getFullYear()}-${date.getMonth()}-${date.getDate()}`;
      const group = map.get(key) ?? {
        key,
        label: new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ru-RU', {
          weekday: 'long',
          day: 'numeric',
          month: 'long',
          year: 'numeric',
        }).format(date),
        entries: [],
      };
      group.entries.push(entry);
      map.set(key, group);
    }
    return [...map.values()];
  });

  readonly uniqueOrders = computed(() => new Set(this.log().map((entry) => entry.orderId)).size);

  readonly uniqueAuthors = computed(() => new Set(this.log().map((entry) => entry.changedBy)).size);

  readonly todayCount = computed(() => {
    const today = new Date();
    return this.log().filter((entry) => {
      const date = new Date(entry.changedAt);
      return (
        date.getFullYear() === today.getFullYear() &&
        date.getMonth() === today.getMonth() &&
        date.getDate() === today.getDate()
      );
    }).length;
  });

  constructor() {
    this.load();
  }

  load(): void {
    this.loading.set(true);
    forkJoin({
      log: this.api.statusLog(),
      statuses: this.api.statuses(),
      orders: this.api.orders(),
    }).subscribe({
      next: (data) => {
        this.log.set(data.log);
        this.statuses.set(data.statuses);
        this.orders.set(data.orders);
        this.loading.set(false);
      },
      error: (error) => {
        this.loading.set(false);
        this.toast.error(errorMessage(error));
      },
    });
  }

  resetFilters(): void {
    this.search.set('');
    this.orderFilter.set(null);
  }

  orderOf(orderId: number): OrderSummary | undefined {
    return this.orders().find((order) => order.orderId === orderId);
  }

  statusName(id: number | null): string {
    if (id === null) {
      return '—';
    }
    return this.statuses().find((status) => status.id === id)?.name ?? `статус ${id}`;
  }

  time(value: string): string {
    return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('ru-RU', {
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit',
      second: '2-digit',
    }).format(new Date(value));
  }
}
````

## 96. `frontend/src/app/pages/status-log/status-log.html`

Строк: 162

``html
<div class="page">
  <div class="page-head">
    <div>
      <p class="eyebrow">Аудит</p>
      <h1 class="page-title">Журнал статусов</h1>
      <p class="page-sub">
        Каждое изменение статуса заявки записывается триггером <code>trigger_log_status_change</code>
        на стороне PostgreSQL.
      </p>
    </div>
    <div class="page-actions">
      <button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost" (click)="load()" [disabled]="loading()">
        <app-icon name="history" [size]="16" />
        Обновить
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="mini-stats">
    <div class="mini-stat">
      <span class="mini-icon blue"><app-icon name="history" [size]="18" /></span>
      <span>
        <strong>{{ log().length }}</strong>
        <span>{{ pluralWord(log().length, 'событие', 'события', 'событий') }} в журнале</span>
      </span>
    </div>
    <div class="mini-stat">
      <span class="mini-icon green"><app-icon name="clock" [size]="18" /></span>
      <span>
        <strong>{{ todayCount() }}</strong>
        <span>{{ pluralWord(todayCount(), 'изменение', 'изменения', 'изменений') }} сегодня</span>
      </span>
    </div>
    <div class="mini-stat">
      <span class="mini-icon orange"><app-icon name="orders" [size]="18" /></span>
      <span>
        <strong>{{ uniqueOrders() }}</strong>
        <span>{{ pluralWord(uniqueOrders(), 'заявка затронута', 'заявки затронуты', 'заявок затронуто') }}</span>
      </span>
    </div>
    <div class="mini-stat">
      <span class="mini-icon violet"><app-icon name="clients" [size]="18" /></span>
      <span>
        <strong>{{ uniqueAuthors() }}</strong>
        <span>{{ pluralWord(uniqueAuthors(), 'автор изменений', 'автора изменений', 'авторов изменений') }}</span>
      </span>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="toolbar card">
    <div class="filter-group">
      <label class="filter-field">
        <span>Заявка</span>
        <select
          class="select"
          [ngModel]="orderFilter()"
          (ngModelChange)="orderFilter.set($event)"
          aria-label="Фильтр по заявке"
        >
          <option [ngValue]="null">Все заявки</option>
          @for (order of orders(); track order.orderId) {
            <option [ngValue]="order.orderId">#{{ order.orderId }} — {{ order.client }}</option>
          }
        </select>
      </label>

      <div class="search log-search">
        <app-icon name="search" [size]="16" />
        <input
          type="search"
          placeholder="Клиент, автомобиль, автор…"
          [value]="search()"
          (input)="search.set($any($event.target).value)"
          aria-label="Поиск по журналу"
        />
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="filter-actions">
      <span class="result-count">Найдено: <strong>{{ filtered().length }}</strong></span>
      @if (search() || orderFilter() !== null) {
        <button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" (click)="resetFilters()">
          <app-icon name="close" [size]="13" />
          Сбросить
        </button>
      }
    </div>
  </div>

  @if (loading()) {
    <div class="log-skeletons">
      @for (item of [1, 2, 3, 4]; track item) {
        <div class="skeleton log-skeleton"></div>
      }
    </div>
  } @else if (groups().length === 0) {
    <div class="card">
      <div class="empty">
        <span class="empty-icon"><app-icon name="history" [size]="22" /></span>
        <strong>Событий не найдено</strong>
        <span>Измените фильтры или завершите заявку, чтобы триггер записал событие.</span>
        <button type="button" class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" (click)="resetFilters()">
          Сбросить фильтры
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  } @else {
    <div class="log-groups">
      @for (group of groups(); track group.key) {
        <section class="log-group">
          <header class="log-day">
            <span class="log-day-label">{{ group.label }}</span>
            <span class="log-day-count">{{ plural(group.entries.length, 'событие', 'события', 'событий') }}</span>
          </header>

          <div class="log-entries">
            @for (entry of group.entries; track entry.id) {
              <article class="log-entry">
                <div class="log-time">
                  <strong>{{ time(entry.changedAt) }}</strong>
                  <span>#{{ entry.id }}</span>
                </div>
                <span class="log-dot"></span>
                <div class="log-card">
                  <div class="log-top">
                    <a
                      class="order-chip"
                      routerLink="/orders"
                      [queryParams]="{ order: entry.orderId }"
                    >
                      Заявка #{{ entry.orderId }}
                    </a>
                    @if (orderOf(entry.orderId); as order) {
                      <span class="log-client">{{ order.client }}</span>
                      <span class="chip plate">{{ order.car }}</span>
                    } @else {
                      <span class="log-client muted">Заявка удалена или недоступна</span>
                    }
                    <span class="log-author">
                      <app-icon name="clients" [size]="13" />
                      {{ entry.changedBy }}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <div class="log-status">
                    <span class="badge" [class]="'badge ' + statusClass(statusName(entry.oldStatusId))">
                      {{ statusName(entry.oldStatusId) }}
                    </span>
                    <span class="status-arrow"><app-icon name="arrow" [size]="15" /></span>
                    <span class="badge" [class]="'badge ' + statusClass(statusName(entry.newStatusId))">
                      {{ statusName(entry.newStatusId) }}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </article>
            }
          </div>
        </section>
      }
    </div>
  }
</div>
````

``css
.mini-stats {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr));
  gap: 12px;
  margin-bottom: 18px;
}

.mini-stat {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 11px;
  padding: 13px 15px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 15px;
  background: var(--surface);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-sm);
}

.mini-icon {
  flex: 0 0 auto;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  width: 36px;
  height: 36px;
  border-radius: 11px;
}

.mini-icon.blue {
  color: var(--brand);
  background: var(--brand-soft);
}

.mini-icon.green {
  color: #047857;
  background: var(--success-soft);
}

.mini-icon.orange {
  color: #c2410c;
  background: var(--accent-soft);
}

.mini-icon.violet {
  color: #6d28d9;
  background: var(--violet-soft);
}

.mini-stat strong {
  display: block;
  font-size: 17px;
  font-weight: 800;
  line-height: 1;
  letter-spacing: -0.03em;
}

.mini-stat span span {
  display: block;
  margin-top: 4px;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 10.5px;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.toolbar {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 16px;
  padding: 10px 12px;
  margin-bottom: 20px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
}

.filter-group {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
}

.filter-field {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 9px;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 10.5px;
  font-weight: 700;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.filter-field .select {
  width: 240px;
  height: 38px;
}

.log-search {
  width: 300px;
  background: var(--surface-2);
}

.filter-actions {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 10px;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.result-count {
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 10.5px;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.result-count strong {
  color: var(--ink);
  font-weight: 800;
}

.log-groups {
  display: grid;
  gap: 26px;
}

.log-day {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 11px;
  margin-bottom: 13px;
}

.log-day-label {
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.11em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--ink-soft);
}

.log-day-label::first-letter {
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.log-day-count {
  padding: 3px 8px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: var(--surface-2);
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 9.5px;
  font-weight: 700;
}

.log-entries {
  position: relative;
  display: grid;
  gap: 10px;
  padding-left: 4px;
}

.log-entries::before {
  content: '';
  position: absolute;
  top: 10px;
  bottom: 10px;
  left: 88px;
  width: 2px;
  border-radius: 2px;
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #dbe6f7, #eef2f8);
}

.log-entry {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 76px 24px minmax(0, 1fr);
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0;
}

.log-time {
  text-align: right;
  padding-right: 13px;
}

.log-time strong {
  display: block;
  font-size: 11.5px;
  font-weight: 800;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
}

.log-time span {
  display: block;
  margin-top: 2px;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 9px;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.log-dot {
  position: relative;
  z-index: 1;
  justify-self: center;
  width: 9px;
  height: 9px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: #fff;
  border: 2px solid #93b4f8;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px #fff;
}

.log-card {
  margin-left: 13px;
  padding: 12px 14px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 13px;
  background: var(--surface);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-sm);
  transition: border-color 0.16s ease, box-shadow 0.16s ease, transform 0.16s ease;
}

.log-card:hover {
  border-color: #c9d9f5;
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-md);
  transform: translateX(2px);
}

.log-top {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 9px;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
}

.order-chip {
  padding: 3px 8px;
  border-radius: 7px;
  background: var(--brand-soft);
  color: var(--brand-dark);
  font-size: 10.5px;
  font-weight: 800;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.order-chip:hover {
  background: #d9e6ff;
}

.log-client {
  color: var(--ink-soft);
  font-size: 11px;
  font-weight: 700;
}

.log-author {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 5px;
  margin-left: auto;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 10px;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.log-status {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 8px;
  margin-top: 9px;
}

.status-arrow {
  display: inline-flex;
  color: #b3bfd1;
}

.log-skeletons {
  display: grid;
  gap: 10px;
}

.log-skeleton {
  height: 74px;
}

@media (max-width: 1180px) {
  .mini-stats {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }

  .toolbar {
    flex-direction: column;
    align-items: stretch;
  }

  .filter-group {
    flex-wrap: wrap;
  }

  .log-search {
    flex: 1;
    min-width: 240px;
  }

  .filter-actions {
    justify-content: space-between;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 680px) {
  .filter-field {
    width: 100%;
  }

  .filter-field .select {
    flex: 1;
    width: auto;
  }

  .log-search {
    width: 100%;
    min-width: 0;
  }

  .log-entries::before {
    left: 13px;
  }

  .log-entry {
    grid-template-columns: 24px minmax(0, 1fr);
    align-items: start;
  }

  .log-time {
    grid-column: 2;
    display: flex;
    align-items: baseline;
    gap: 6px;
    padding: 0 0 4px 13px;
    text-align: left;
    order: -1;
  }

  .log-dot {
    grid-column: 1;
    grid-row: 1 / span 2;
    margin-top: 5px;
  }

  .log-card {
    grid-column: 2;
    margin-left: 13px;
  }

  .log-top {
    align-items: flex-start;
  }

  .log-author {
    width: 100%;
    margin-left: 0;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 460px) {
  .mini-stats {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
}
````

## 98. `sto.sql`

Строк: 392

``sql


-- 1. Роли пользователей
CREATE TABLE roles (
    id          SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    name        VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    description TEXT
);

INSERT INTO roles (name, description) VALUES
    ('admin',   'Администратор системы'),
    ('manager', 'Мастер-приёмщик'),
    ('master',  'Автомобильный мастер'),
    ('client',  'Клиент СТО');

-- 2. Пользователи системы
CREATE TABLE users (
    id            SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    username      VARCHAR(50)  NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    password_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    full_name     VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
    role_id       INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES roles(id),
    created_at    TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 3. Клиенты
CREATE TABLE clients (
    id         SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    full_name  VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
    phone      VARCHAR(20)  NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    email      VARCHAR(100),
    created_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 4. Автомобили клиентов
CREATE TABLE cars (
    id        SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    client_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES clients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    brand     VARCHAR(50)  NOT NULL,
    model     VARCHAR(50)  NOT NULL,
    year      INTEGER CHECK (year BETWEEN 1950 AND 2100),
    plate_num VARCHAR(15)  NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    vin       VARCHAR(17)
);

-- 5. Мастера
CREATE TABLE masters (
    id             SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id        INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id),
    specialization VARCHAR(100),
    is_active      BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE
);

-- 6. Услуги
CREATE TABLE services (
    id           SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    name         VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
    price        NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL CHECK (price >= 0),
    duration_min INTEGER CHECK (duration_min > 0)
);

-- 7. Статусы заявок
CREATE TABLE order_statuses (
    id   SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    name VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL UNIQUE
);

INSERT INTO order_statuses (name) VALUES
    ('новая'), ('в работе'), ('завершена'), ('отменена');

-- 8. Заявки
CREATE TABLE orders (
    id          SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    client_id   INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES clients(id),
    car_id      INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES cars(id),
    master_id   INTEGER REFERENCES masters(id),
    status_id   INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES order_statuses(id) DEFAULT 1,
    description TEXT,
    created_at  TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    closed_at   TIMESTAMP
);

-- 9. Услуги в заявке
CREATE TABLE order_services (
    id         SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    order_id   INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES orders(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    service_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES services(id),
    quantity   INTEGER DEFAULT 1 CHECK (quantity > 0),
    price_at   NUMERIC(10,2) NOT NULL
);

-- 10. Журнал изменений статусов (для триггера)
CREATE TABLE order_status_log (
    id         SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    order_id   INTEGER NOT NULL,
    old_status INTEGER,
    new_status INTEGER,
    changed_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    changed_by TEXT DEFAULT current_user
);

-- Индексы
CREATE INDEX idx_orders_client ON orders(client_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_orders_master ON orders(master_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_orders_status ON orders(status_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_cars_client   ON cars(client_id);

-- Представление для расчёта стоимости заявки
CREATE VIEW v_order_total AS
SELECT
    o.id           AS order_id,
    c.full_name    AS client,
    cr.plate_num   AS car,
    os.name        AS status,
    COALESCE(SUM(osv.price_at * osv.quantity), 0) AS total_cost
FROM orders o
JOIN clients c         ON c.id = o.client_id
JOIN cars cr           ON cr.id = o.car_id
JOIN order_statuses os ON os.id = o.status_id
LEFT JOIN order_services osv ON osv.order_id = o.id
GROUP BY o.id, c.full_name, cr.plate_num, os.name;

-- Создание базовых ролей 
CREATE ROLE sto_admin   LOGIN PASSWORD 'admin_pass';
CREATE ROLE sto_manager LOGIN PASSWORD 'manager_pass';
CREATE ROLE sto_master  LOGIN PASSWORD 'master_pass';
CREATE ROLE sto_client  LOGIN PASSWORD 'client_pass';
-- Права администратора (полный доступ)
GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA public TO sto_admin;
GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON ALL SEQUENCES IN SCHEMA public TO sto_admin;
-- Права мастера-приёмщика (работа с заявками и клиентами)
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE ON clients        TO sto_manager;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE ON cars           TO sto_manager;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE ON orders         TO sto_manager;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE ON order_services TO sto_manager;
GRANT SELECT ON services                       TO sto_manager;
GRANT SELECT ON order_statuses                 TO sto_manager;
GRANT SELECT ON masters                        TO sto_manager;
GRANT USAGE, SELECT ON ALL SEQUENCES IN SCHEMA public TO sto_manager;

-- Права мастера (ограниченное обновление)
GRANT SELECT ON orders                 TO sto_master;
GRANT SELECT ON clients                TO sto_master;
GRANT SELECT ON cars                   TO sto_master;
GRANT SELECT ON services               TO sto_master;
GRANT SELECT, INSERT ON order_services TO sto_master;
GRANT UPDATE (status_id, closed_at) ON orders TO sto_master;

-- Права клиента (только просмотр через представление)
GRANT SELECT ON v_order_total TO sto_client;

-- 6.1. Создание заявки
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION create_order(
    p_client_id   INTEGER,
    p_car_id      INTEGER,
    p_master_id   INTEGER,
    p_description TEXT
)
RETURNS INTEGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_order_id INTEGER;
BEGIN
    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM clients WHERE id = p_client_id) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Клиент с id=% не найден', p_client_id;
    END IF;

    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM cars WHERE id = p_car_id) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Автомобиль с id=% не найден', p_car_id;
    END IF;

    INSERT INTO orders (client_id, car_id, master_id, status_id, description)
    VALUES (p_client_id, p_car_id, p_master_id, 1, p_description)
    RETURNING id INTO v_order_id;

    RETURN v_order_id;
END;
$$;

-- 6.2. Добавление услуги в заявку
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION add_service_to_order(
    p_order_id   INTEGER,
    p_service_id INTEGER,
    p_quantity   INTEGER DEFAULT 1
)
RETURNS VOID
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_price NUMERIC(10,2);
BEGIN
    SELECT price INTO v_price FROM services WHERE id = p_service_id;
    IF v_price IS NULL THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Услуга с id=% не найдена', p_service_id;
    END IF;

    INSERT INTO order_services (order_id, service_id, quantity, price_at)
    VALUES (p_order_id, p_service_id, p_quantity, v_price);
END;
$$;

-- 6.3. Закрытие заявки
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION close_order(p_order_id INTEGER)
RETURNS VOID
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM orders WHERE id = p_order_id) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Заявка с id=% не найдена', p_order_id;
    END IF;

    UPDATE orders
    SET status_id = (SELECT id FROM order_statuses WHERE name = 'завершена')
    WHERE id = p_order_id;
END;
$$;

-- 6.4. Расчёт стоимости заявки
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION calculate_order_total(p_order_id INTEGER)
RETURNS NUMERIC
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_total NUMERIC(10,2);
BEGIN
    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(price_at * quantity), 0)
    INTO v_total
    FROM order_services
    WHERE order_id = p_order_id;

    RETURN v_total;
END;
$$;

-- 6.5. Получение заявок клиента
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION get_client_orders(p_client_id INTEGER)
RETURNS TABLE (
    order_id   INTEGER,
    car_plate  VARCHAR,
    status     VARCHAR,
    total_cost NUMERIC,
    created_at TIMESTAMP
)
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT o.id,
           cr.plate_num,
           os.name::VARCHAR,
           COALESCE(SUM(osv.price_at * osv.quantity), 0),
           o.created_at
    FROM orders o
    JOIN cars cr           ON cr.id = o.car_id
    JOIN order_statuses os ON os.id = o.status_id
    LEFT JOIN order_services osv ON osv.order_id = o.id
    WHERE o.client_id = p_client_id
    GROUP BY o.id, cr.plate_num, os.name, o.created_at
    ORDER BY o.created_at DESC;
END;
$$;

-- Права на выполнение хранимых функций (выдаются после их создания)
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION create_order(INTEGER, INTEGER, INTEGER, TEXT) TO sto_manager;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION add_service_to_order(INTEGER, INTEGER, INTEGER) TO sto_manager, sto_master;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION close_order(INTEGER) TO sto_master, sto_manager;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION calculate_order_total(INTEGER) TO sto_manager, sto_master, sto_client;
GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION get_client_orders(INTEGER) TO sto_manager, sto_client;

-- 7.1. Триггер: автозаполнение closed_at
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION trg_set_closed_at()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.status_id = (SELECT id FROM order_statuses WHERE name = 'завершена')
       AND NEW.closed_at IS NULL THEN
        NEW.closed_at := CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;
    END IF;

    IF NEW.status_id <> (SELECT id FROM order_statuses WHERE name = 'завершена') THEN
        NEW.closed_at := NULL;
    END IF;

    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_set_closed_at
BEFORE UPDATE OF status_id ON orders
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION trg_set_closed_at();


-- 7.2. Триггер: логирование изменений статуса
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION trg_log_status_change()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.status_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.status_id THEN
        INSERT INTO order_status_log (order_id, old_status, new_status)
        VALUES (NEW.id, OLD.status_id, NEW.status_id);
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_log_status_change
AFTER UPDATE OF status_id ON orders
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION trg_log_status_change();


-- 7.3. Триггер: запрет добавления услуг в завершённую заявку
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION trg_check_order_status()
RETURNS TRIGGER
LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $$
DECLARE
    v_status VARCHAR(50);
BEGIN
    SELECT os.name INTO v_status
    FROM orders o
    JOIN order_statuses os ON os.id = o.status_id
    WHERE o.id = NEW.order_id;

    IF v_status IN ('завершена', 'отменена') THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'Нельзя добавить услугу в заявку со статусом «%»', v_status;
    END IF;
	
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

CREATE TRIGGER trigger_check_order_status
BEFORE INSERT ON order_services
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION trg_check_order_status();

-- 1. Создаём группу
CREATE ROLE sto_readonly_group;

-- 2. Даём группе права только на чтение
GRANT SELECT ON services       TO sto_readonly_group;
GRANT SELECT ON order_statuses TO sto_readonly_group;
GRANT SELECT ON masters        TO sto_readonly_group;

-- 3. Добавляем роли в группу с наследованием
GRANT sto_readonly_group TO sto_manager WITH INHERIT TRUE;
GRANT sto_readonly_group TO sto_master  WITH INHERIT TRUE;

-- 4. Отзываем прямое право у manager, чтобы проверить наследование
REVOKE SELECT ON services FROM sto_manager;

-- отзыв права на обновление закрытых заявок
SELECT grantee, privilege_type, column_name
FROM information_schema.column_privileges
WHERE table_name = 'orders'
  AND grantee = 'sto_master';

  REVOKE UPDATE (closed_at) ON orders FROM sto_master;
  -- возвращение права на обновление закрытых заявок
GRANT UPDATE (closed_at) ON orders TO sto_master;

SELECT
    rolname        AS "Роль",
    rolinherit     AS "Наследование",
    rolcanlogin    AS "Может входить"
FROM pg_catalog.pg_roles
WHERE rolname NOT LIKE 'pg_%'
ORDER BY rolname;

SELECT
    r.rolname       AS "Роль",
    t.table_name    AS "Таблица",
    t.privilege_type AS "Право"
FROM pg_catalog.pg_roles r
JOIN information_schema.role_table_grants t
    ON t.grantee = r.rolname
WHERE r.rolname NOT LIKE 'pg_%'
ORDER BY r.rolname, t.table_name, t.privilege_type;

SELECT
    grantee         AS "Роль",
    table_name      AS "Таблица",
    column_name     AS "Столбец",
    privilege_type  AS "Право"
FROM information_schema.column_privileges
WHERE grantee LIKE 'sto_%'
ORDER BY grantee, table_name, column_name;
````

## 99. `docker/init/02_seed.sql`

Строк: 112

``sql
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Тестовые (демонстрационные) данные для стенда СТО.
-- Выполняется автоматически после sto.sql при первом запуске контейнера.
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pgcrypto;

-- Пользователи. Пароли хешируются bcrypt средствами PostgreSQL (pgcrypto).
INSERT INTO users (username, password_hash, full_name, role_id)
SELECT 'admin', crypt('admin123', gen_salt('bf')), 'Поляков Роман Евгеньевич', r.id
FROM roles r WHERE r.name = 'admin';

INSERT INTO users (username, password_hash, full_name, role_id)
SELECT 'manager', crypt('manager123', gen_salt('bf')), 'Смирнова Анна Викторовна', r.id
FROM roles r WHERE r.name = 'manager';

INSERT INTO users (username, password_hash, full_name, role_id)
SELECT 'master', crypt('master123', gen_salt('bf')), 'Кузнецов Дмитрий Сергеевич', r.id
FROM roles r WHERE r.name = 'master';

INSERT INTO users (username, password_hash, full_name, role_id)
SELECT 'client', crypt('client123', gen_salt('bf')), 'Иванов Иван Иванович', r.id
FROM roles r WHERE r.name = 'client';

-- Мастер
INSERT INTO masters (user_id, specialization, is_active)
SELECT u.id, 'Диагностика, двигатель, ходовая часть', TRUE
FROM users u WHERE u.username = 'master';

-- Клиенты
INSERT INTO clients (full_name, phone, email) VALUES
    ('Иванов Иван Иванович',   '+7-900-100-10-01', 'ivanov@example.ru'),
    ('Петрова Мария Сергеевна', '+7-900-100-10-02', 'petrova@example.ru'),
    ('Сидоров Алексей Петрович','+7-900-100-10-03', 'sidorov@example.ru');

-- Автомобили
INSERT INTO cars (client_id, brand, model, year, plate_num, vin)
SELECT c.id, 'Lada',  'Vesta', 2021, 'А123ВС64', 'XTA218050M0123456'
FROM clients c WHERE c.phone = '+7-900-100-10-01';

INSERT INTO cars (client_id, brand, model, year, plate_num, vin)
SELECT c.id, 'Toyota', 'Camry', 2019, 'В456ЕК64', 'JTNBF3HK803012345'
FROM clients c WHERE c.phone = '+7-900-100-10-02';

INSERT INTO cars (client_id, brand, model, year, plate_num, vin)
SELECT c.id, 'Kia', 'Rio', 2020, 'С789МН64', 'Z94C241ABLR123456'
FROM clients c WHERE c.phone = '+7-900-100-10-03';

INSERT INTO cars (client_id, brand, model, year, plate_num, vin)
SELECT c.id, 'Lada', 'Granta', 2017, 'Е321ОР64', 'XTA219050J0123456'
FROM clients c WHERE c.phone = '+7-900-100-10-01';

-- Услуги
INSERT INTO services (name, price, duration_min) VALUES
    ('Компьютерная диагностика', 2000.00, 45),
    ('Замена моторного масла',    800.00, 30),
    ('Замена тормозных колодок', 2500.00, 90),
    ('Развал-схождение',         3000.00, 60),
    ('Замена ремня ГРМ',         6500.00, 180),
    ('Шиномонтаж (комплект)',    3200.00, 60);

-- Заявка №1: новая, без мастера
INSERT INTO orders (client_id, car_id, status_id, description)
SELECT c.id, cr.id, 1, 'Стук в передней подвеске при проезде неровностей'
FROM clients c JOIN cars cr ON cr.client_id = c.id
WHERE c.phone = '+7-900-100-10-01' AND cr.plate_num = 'А123ВС64';

-- Заявка №2: в работе, назначен мастер
INSERT INTO orders (client_id, car_id, master_id, status_id, description)
SELECT c.id, cr.id, m.id, 2, 'Плановое ТО: масло, фильтры, диагностика'
FROM clients c
JOIN cars cr ON cr.client_id = c.id
CROSS JOIN (SELECT id FROM masters LIMIT 1) m
WHERE c.phone = '+7-900-100-10-02' AND cr.plate_num = 'В456ЕК64';

-- Заявка №3: завершённая (закрываем после добавления услуг,
-- иначе сработает триггер запрета добавления услуг в закрытую заявку)
INSERT INTO orders (client_id, car_id, master_id, status_id, description)
SELECT c.id, cr.id, m.id, 1, 'Замена тормозных колодок и развал-схождение'
FROM clients c
JOIN cars cr ON cr.client_id = c.id
CROSS JOIN (SELECT id FROM masters LIMIT 1) m
WHERE c.phone = '+7-900-100-10-03' AND cr.plate_num = 'С789МН64';

-- Услуги в заявках
INSERT INTO order_services (order_id, service_id, quantity, price_at)
SELECT o.id, s.id, 1, s.price
FROM orders o
JOIN services s ON s.name = 'Компьютерная диагностика'
WHERE o.description = 'Плановое ТО: масло, фильтры, диагностика';

INSERT INTO order_services (order_id, service_id, quantity, price_at)
SELECT o.id, s.id, 1, s.price
FROM orders o
JOIN services s ON s.name = 'Замена моторного масла'
WHERE o.description = 'Плановое ТО: масло, фильтры, диагностика';

INSERT INTO order_services (order_id, service_id, quantity, price_at)
SELECT o.id, s.id, 1, s.price
FROM orders o
JOIN services s ON s.name = 'Замена тормозных колодок'
WHERE o.description = 'Замена тормозных колодок и развал-схождение';

INSERT INTO order_services (order_id, service_id, quantity, price_at)
SELECT o.id, s.id, 1, s.price
FROM orders o
JOIN services s ON s.name = 'Развал-схождение'
WHERE o.description = 'Замена тормозных колодок и развал-схождение';

-- Закрытие заявки №3 (триггеры заполнят closed_at и запишут журнал)
UPDATE orders
SET status_id = (SELECT id FROM order_statuses WHERE name = 'завершена')
WHERE description = 'Замена тормозных колодок и развал-схождение';
````
